# AMS Seedboard — specification, version 0.19 (build 19; parts 20 to 25 hold 0.14 to 0.19, part 25 is the newest)

**Version 0.11 (build 11, 2026-11-19)** is a hardening and improvement round: 38 picks from `Docs/IDEAS-0.11.md` (1–12, 14–24, 26, 27, 28, 30, 31, 33, 36–41, 43, 47). Everything is in part **17**. Item 13 was not built; item 14 needed no change. The board format is now **7** (new field `Board.due`). Where part 17 and an older part disagree, part 17 is right.

**Version 0.9 (build 9, 2026-11-18)** is a big round: 40 picks (A, B and 38 numbered ideas from `Docs/IDEAS-0.9.md`) in eight lanes: timeline, marks, views, maps, pencil, PARAGON, finding. Everything is in part **15**. Where part 15 and an older part disagree about a thing 0.9 changed, part 15 is right. Everything of 0.8 that 0.9 does not name is as it was. The board format is now **6**.

**Version 0.8 (build 8, 2026-11-18)** is a small version on top of 0.7: the day a bar or milestone was done is now also drawn on the timeline board itself, and the course puts the chosen row's bar at the bottom (part **14**). Where part 14 and an older part disagree about a thing 0.8 changed, part 14 is right. Everything of 0.7 that 0.8 does not name is as it was. The board format is still 5.

**Version 0.7 (build 7, 2026-11-18)** is a small version on top of 0.6: the day a bar or milestone was marked done is now shown (part **13**), and the course shows the iPad and the Mac too (13.3). Where part 13 and an older part disagree about a thing 0.7 changed, part 13 is right. Everything of 0.6 that 0.7 does not name is as it was. The board format is still 5.

**Version 0.6 (build 6, 2026-11-18)** is a small version on top of 0.5: two fixes and a course. A bar or milestone marked Done now goes to PARAGON ticked, not open (5.3 and 12.1); the app icon's menu on the iPhone and iPad never has more than four lines (11.19 and 12.2); the link back to the board in a PARAGON note can be pressed (12.3); and Seedboard has its first course, `Docs/intro/index.html` (12.4). Everything is in part **12**. Where part 12 and an older part disagree about a thing 0.6 changed, part 12 is right. Everything of 0.5 that 0.6 does not name is as it was. The board format is still 5.

**Version 0.5 (build 5, 2026-11-18)** is the big round after the second expert panel (`Docs/PANEL-0.5.md`, 25 picks, and the "fixed in any case" list). Everything new is in part **11** (11.1–11.20), written from the sixteen builders' notes in `Docs/notes-0.5/built-*.md` and the groundwork in `Docs/BUILDERS-0.5.md`. The board format is now **5** (3.2, 11.1). Where part 11 and an older part disagree about a thing 0.5 changed, part 11 is right; the older parts carry a short **0.5:** pointer where they are now wrong. Everything of 0.4 that 0.5 does not name is as it was. What was not seen on a real device is listed in 9.7.

Written from the code as it stands for 0.2, as of 2026-11-18, and from the nine builders' notes in `Docs/notes-0.2` (snap, look, timeline, maps, arrows, touch, shapes, bringback, mac). Where a note and the code disagree, this document says what the code does. Where the code does something other than the owner's feature list, it is marked **Note**. The last three parts to be built (Pencil drawing 3.15, the sequence diagram "Who does what" 3.16, and the bar beside the chosen thing with the menu, copy / cut / paste and Home 2.9) are written from their builders' notes in `Docs/notes-0.2` (pencil, sequence, bar).

**Version 0.4 (build 4, 2026-11-18)** is a small version on top of 0.3: a picture can have a caption (words under it) and can be cropped (3.20, 2.11), and the example person in the "Who does what" starter and the sample boards has a neutral name, Alex. The board format is now 4 (3.2). It is written from the code and from the builder's note in `Docs/notes-0.4/caption-crop.md`. Where this part and an older part disagree about a thing 0.4 changed (for example "a picture has no words", 2.10 and 3.17), 0.4 is right. Everything in 0.3 that 0.4 does not name is as it was.

**Version 0.3 (build 3, 2026-11-18)** adds, on top of 0.2: pictures on boards (3.17, 2.10), the owner's own starters and a page to rename, move, change and remove every starter (3.18, 2.10), seven small fixes (3.19), the key S for Draw, a ninth tool and a tool row whose tiles shrink to fit (2.3), and a typing rule (3.8). The board format is now 3 (3.2). It is written from the code as it stands and from the builders' notes in `Docs/notes-0.3` (pictures, starters, fixes). Where this part and an older part disagree about a thing 0.3 changed, 0.3 is right. Everything in 0.2 that 0.3 does not name is as it was.

## 1. What the app is

AMS Seedboard is a thinking tool for iPhone, iPad and Mac (one target, iOS 17 and macOS 14 and later, bundle id `com.schabbauer.AMSSeedboard`, team `D24ENP83QQ`, URL scheme `seedboard`, App Store category Productivity). Its purpose: start a thought on an endless board (mind map, flow with shapes and arrows, sticky notes) or on a timeline board (bars on days), grow it, and hand it to PARAGON (the owner's notes app) as a note, a project or Inbox tasks.

Layout of the repository:

- `Core/` — Swift package `SeedboardCore`: every rule, no screen. Files of 0.1: `Board.swift` (data), `BoardEdit.swift` (add, link, move, copy, remove, hit-testing), `MindMap.swift` (tree rules, sides and layout), `Room.swift` (a growing map pushes things aside), `Shapes.swift` (a shape's size from its words, changing its kind), `Viewport.swift` (where the board stands on screen), `Typing.swift` (the line-break rule for ideas), `Outline.swift` (lists in and out), `Route.swift` (arrow routing, the 0.1 rule for sides), `Starters.swift` (the six starts), `Paragon.swift` (the hand-over text), `Safety.swift` (the storage decisions). New in 0.2: `Snap.swift`, `DotGrid.swift`, `Tidy.swift` (falling in line, the dots, Line up / Even gaps / Same size), `Home.swift` (search, "Changed …", colour in use), `Timeline.swift` (the timeline board), `Reorder.swift` (ideas in another order), `ProjectPlan.swift` (a project by the ticks, one branch alone), `Arrows.swift`, `ArrowEdit.swift` (where arrows run; ends, dashes, the next shape), `Fingers.swift` (press areas, the shove), `ShapeOutline.swift` (the four new shapes), `Lanes.swift`, `ParagonLanes.swift` (lanes in frames), `Kept.swift`, `KeptFiles.swift` (removed boards, earlier versions), `ExportAll.swift` (export and import), `Remember.swift` (opens where you left), `Outside.swift` (boards from outside, the device's search), `MenuRules.swift` (menu lines and letters). Tests in `Core/Tests/SeedboardCoreTests`. (**Note**: `Core/BoardTests.swift` and `Core/StartersTests.swift` beside `Package.swift` are stray copies that are never built.)
- `App/Sources/` — the screens. Of 0.1: `SeedboardApp`, `RootView`, `BoardView`, `CanvasView`, `CanvasDraw`, `BoardSession`, `AppModel`, `BoardStore`, `ParagonGate`, `SendSheet`, `SettingsView`, `Guide`, `Sample`, `BoardPicture`, `Theme`. New in 0.2: `HomeView`, `BoardThumb`, `Look` (home page, small pictures, calmer colours), `SnapShow` (guides and dots), `TimelineView`, `TimelineDraw`, `TimelineBars`, `TimelineSession`, `MapOrder`, `SendBranch`, `ArrowDraw`, `ArrowSession`, `TouchLayer`, `Press`, `FingerSizes`, `ShapePaths`, `ShapeDraw`, `ShapePlate`, `LanesView`, `KeptPage`, `BringBack`, `BringBackBars`, `SampleKept`, `Menus`, `Remember`, `Intents`, `Spotlight`, `NewMarks` (stand-in names for marks not yet in the icon library).
- `UITests/` — screen tests (find controls by identifier), one file for each new screen: `SeedboardUITests`, `HomeUITests`, `TimelineUITests`, `MapsUITests`, `TouchUITests`, `ShapesUITests`, `BringBackUITests`, `MenuUITests`.
- New in 0.3. `Core/`: `Pictures.swift` (3.17), `StarterShelf.swift` (3.18); tests `PictureTests`, `StarterShelfTests`, `FixesTests`. `App/Sources/`: `PictureViews.swift` (the tool, the picker, `PictureHost`, the "not here yet" plate), `PictureStore.swift` (the picture files), `SamplePictures.swift`, `StarterStore.swift` (the starter files), `StarterModel.swift` (`AppModel` extension), `StartersPage.swift` (page, questions, banner, card menu), `TrackpadCatcher.swift` (iPad trackpad on the two kinds of board that place themselves). `UITests/`: `PictureUITests`, `StartersUITests`, `TypingUITests` (Mac only).
- New in 0.5: see 11.20 for the list of new files (Core rules, app files, tests) and the second target `AMSSeedboardShare` (11.13).
- `project.yml` — XcodeGen spec. Version lives only there (`MARKETING_VERSION`, `CURRENT_PROJECT_VERSION`; now 0.6 and build 6 (0.5: build 5); 0.5 also adds `NSUserActivityTypes`, `NSCameraUsageDescription` "To put a photo straight onto a board.", the Mac's print entitlement, `NSServices`, `CADisableMinimumFrameDurationOnPhone = YES`, and the share-extension target). Icons come from the AMS-Icons package, pinned to one revision. New key in 0.2: `UIApplicationSupportsIndirectInputEvents = true` (trackpad and mouse on the iPad).
- Apple facts: bundle id `com.schabbauer.AMSSeedboard`; App Store Connect app id `6836228189`; iCloud containers `iCloud.com.schabbauer.AMSSeedboard` (the boards) and `iCloud.com.schabbauer.AMSPara` (PARAGON's notes, add-only); PARAGON's shared waiting list is the app group `group.com.schabbauer.amspara` (iPhone and iPad) and `D24ENP83QQ.group.com.schabbauer.amspara` (Mac; own file `App/Config/AMSSeedboard-macOS.entitlements`, hardened runtime on). The Shortcuts actions and the device's search need no new entitlement.
- Build: `xcodegen generate`, then open `AMSSeedboard.xcodeproj`. Rules tests: `swift test --package-path Core`. Release: `Tools/release.sh <build> both` (or `ios` / `mac`).

## 2. Screens and controls

All identifiers below are accessibility identifiers. Every identifier of 0.1 is kept.

### 2.1 Window, home page and the lines at the top

- One window, no New Window command. Mac default size 1100 x 760, minimum 640 x 480. Split view: boards list on the left (width min 200, ideal 226, max 320), open board on the right. On iPhone (compact width) the list comes first and a board opens over it; opening or closing a board switches the column.
- Nothing open (Mac and iPad): the home page `HomeView` (identifier `board.none`, kept, with its children contained). Background `Paint(scheme).canvas`, ignoring safe areas (no black beside a charcoal board at night). Content: padding 16 x 12, at most 1120 wide.
  - Header: sprout mark 30, the heading "Plant a seed" (`Txt.big`), "Every idea starts on a board." (`Txt.label`, secondary). **0.5:** the header has NO button any more (`empty.newBoard` is gone); the starters are one sliding strip and the board cards come first — see 11.2, which replaces the starter-card lines below.
  - Cards lie in a grid of columns 150–210 wide, 10 apart. A card: plate `Paint.plate`, edge `Paint.edge` 1, corner 11, by day the same faint shadow as a shape; picture on top (1.6 : 1), a 1-point edge line, words below with padding 8 x 6.
  - `StarterCard` (one for each starter of the shelf, 3.18: the seven built-in `Starter.allCases` and the owner's own; 0.3): picture of the starter's own board (`Thumbs.board(of:)`), name (`.body` medium, 1 line), hint (`.caption`, secondary, 2 lines reserved). The blank starter shows a quiet green plus (26). Identifier on the home page `home.starter.<rawValue>`; in the chooser `starter.<rawValue>` (kept).
  - `BoardCard`: picture, 9-point colour dot + name (`.body` medium, 1 line), `board.changedWords()` (`.caption`, secondary; 3.14). An empty board's picture says "Empty". Identifier `home.board.<title>`.
  - Sections "PINNED", "RECENT" (not pinned, not sent), "SENT TO PARAGON" (purple): the same three groups and heading style as the list. With a search the cards are the found boards; nothing found → "No board has these words" (`home.noMatch`).
  - (0.2 note, out of date since the Home row of 2.9 and 0.5: board cards now have the list's press-and-hold menu, 11.2.)
- Opens where you left (7.3): the board that was open comes back at its zoom and place.
- Settings: on the Mac its own window (520 x 460), opened from the app menu (Settings…), ⌘, or the `boards.settings` link at the bottom of the list (a `SettingsLink`); with `-settings` it is opened by a message to the app. On iPhone and iPad it is a page (sheet) with Done.
- Opening `seedboard://board/<UUID>` opens that board (rules in 2.2).
- The clash line (`BringBackBars.swift`, `AppModel.clash: ClashNotice?`; identifiers `clash.notice`, `clash.words`, `clash.button`, `clash.close`): a line across the top of the window, over list and board, on all three devices (`safeAreaInset`), in `Part.kept`. Text `KeptRules.clashLine(title)`: "Another device changed “<Title>”. The other version is kept under Earlier versions." Buttons: **Show** (opens the page of 2.7 at that board and closes the line) and a cross. One line at a time (the latest board). Raised by the store (`onClash`) when iCloud held a newer version than the one being saved (mine went to the copies); when a version from another device was set aside (`-away`); when iCloud had parked a version of its own (a conflict version or an "<id> 2.seedboard" copy) and the two differed; and by the app when another device's news lost against what was typed meanwhile.
- The removed-link line (`removedLink.notice`, `.words`, `.button`, `.close`): `AppModel.removedLink` is shown as "This board was removed on 12 Nov 2026." + **Bring back** + cross. Bring back = `AppModel.bringBack(removed:)`, then the board opens and "“<Title>” is back." is said. 0 days left (by the mark's date): "… Its 30 days are over: it cannot be brought back.", no button. No readable copy: "No copy of this board is left to bring back."

### 2.2 Boards list (`boards.list`)

- Top: `boards.new` (full-colour green button "New board") opens the choice headed "Starters" (0.4: large, in the middle, `starters.heading`; 0.3 and older: "Start with", small, at the left): the starters of the shelf (3.18; the seven built-in ones in the owner's order, and his own) as cards with drawings and, at the right of the heading, a small "Edit" text button (`starters.edit`) that closes the pop-up and opens the Starters page (2.10) (`StarterPicker` in `HomeView.swift`; `starter.blank`, `starter.mindMap`, `starter.flow`, `starter.prosCons`, `starter.steps`, `starter.timeline`). On regular width a pop-up: 3 cards per row, each 150 (Mac) / 160 (iPad) wide, 8 apart; its size is worked out from the number of starters (six starters → 2 rows). On compact width (the iPhone) a page from the bottom: columns 150–240 (2 per row), detents 78 % of the screen and full height. Choosing one makes the board, saves it and opens it. The picker has no per-starter switch: a new `Starter` case appears by itself.
- Under it: the search field `SearchField` (`HomeView.swift`): search mark (`Glyph.new(.search)`, 16, green), plain field "Search boards" (`boards.search`), clear button (round cross, `boards.searchClear`, only when not empty); background primary 6 %, corner 8, padding 8 x 5. iPhone/iPad: no auto-capitals, no auto-correction, Search key. Mac: Escape empties it. The text lives in `RootView` (`@State search`), is given to the list and to the home page, and is not stored. Rule in 3.14.
- Groups, shown only when not empty, in this order: Pinned (`pinned == true`), Recent (not pinned, `sent == nil`), Sent to PARAGON (not pinned, `sent != nil`). A pinned board that was sent stays in Pinned. With a search the three groups show the found boards. The list is sorted by `changed`, newest first, when loaded, when iCloud brings news, and at every kept change (so the open board moves to the top as it is changed). A newly made or copied board goes to the top.
- Row `boards.row.<title>`: dot in the board's colour (9), title (one line), `countLine`. Chosen row (`Look.chosenRow`): a soft green tint (#2E8B57 at 14 % by day, #5FBF8A at 20 % by night, corner 8), the name `.body` bold in `Part.boards.text`, the second line `.secondary`, the dot in the board's own colour. A tiny dot (6 points, amber) at the row's end, `notInCloudDot` (spoken "Not in iCloud yet"), shows while the board's newest change is on this device but not in iCloud yet (6, rule 11); it is never shown when iCloud is off or with `-sampleData`. Context menu (right-click, or press and hold): Rename… (alert "Name of the board", Cancel, Save), Pin to the top / Unpin, Make a copy, Colour (Green, Pink, Orange, Blue, Teal, Purple), Open in PARAGON (only when `sent != nil`), Remove… (confirmation "Remove “<title>”?", button "Remove the board", message "It is kept for 30 days in the Removed folder.").
- iPhone row (compact width, `phone: true`, decided in `RootView` from the window's own size class): second line `"<changedWords> · <countLine>"`, and at the right `RowPicture` (60 wide, 1.6 : 1, corner 6, 1-point edge). Mac and iPad rows keep `countLine` only and no picture.
- Empty list text: "No boards yet. Press New board." Nothing found (and there are boards): "No board has these words" (`boards.noMatch`). **0.5, iPhone:** `boards.new` and the search field sit in the thumb row at the BOTTOM (11.3); an empty iPhone list shows the starters themselves (`boards.first`, 11.4; the fallback words are "No boards yet. Press + below.").
- Bottom: `boards.settings` (Settings) and `boards.version` ("<version> (<build>)").
- `countLine`: parts "N idea(s)", "N shape(s)", "N note(s)" joined with " · "; "Empty" when none. Shapes are the eight flow shapes (3.1). Text and frames are not counted. A timeline: "6 bars · 1 milestone · 2 phases" (each part only when not 0), or "Empty".

- Row menu (press and hold / right-click), 0.3: Rename…, Pin to the top / Unpin, Make a copy, **Save as a starter…** (only when `canBeChanged`; it opens the name sheet of 2.10 with the board as it is now, the open one from the live session), Colour, Open in PARAGON (when sent), Remove….
- 0.3: `countLine` also counts pictures after the other parts: "1 picture", "2 pictures" (3.17).

Rules of the list (AppModel):

- New board: `Starter.board()`, colour = `Palette.boards[count % 6]`. The title is the starter's name; if a title (case-insensitive) is taken it becomes "<name> 2", "<name> 3"… A blank board ("New board") is not numbered. (`AppModel.add(_:)` holds this; the Shortcuts actions use the same path, 7.1.)
- Make a copy: new id, title + " copy", then " copy 2", " copy 3"… while a title (case-insensitive) is taken, `created` and `changed` now, `pinned = false`, `sent = nil`, `sentTodos = []`; if the board is open, its live state is copied. It opens.
- Remove: the open session is dropped, the pending save cancelled, the board leaves the list, a removal mark is left and both copies move to Removed (see 6), flash "Board removed. It is kept for 30 days in the Removed folder." (0.5: the line is drawn at the window's level when no open board is in sight, so it is seen; 11.4.)
- Rename: trimmed; empty is refused. A change to a board that is not open is written at once. A board from a newer format cannot be renamed, pinned or coloured (refused), and a copy of one is shown but never written (see 6).
- Saving: every change of the open board is shown in the list at once and written 0.5 s later (also when another board is opened, and on `saveNow`). Words are handed over as they are typed, not only when the typing ends. When the app is left (scene no longer active) or closed (Mac: will-terminate), `AppModel.leaving` finishes the typing (`commitEdit`), saves at once and waits until the DEVICE copy is written (`flush`, 2 s at most). iCloud's copy follows by itself: on iPhone and iPad the app asks iOS for background time (`beginBackgroundTask` "Seedboard saves", ended when iCloud's step is done or the time is up); on the Mac, when the app closes for good, it waits up to 2 s more for iCloud's copy.
- A board that another device removed leaves this list too; if it was open, its typing is finished first: when the board was changed after the removal it stays, is saved again and comes back on every device (6, rule 5); otherwise it closes. A board removed here is not put back by a late save.
- News that iCloud brings for the open board while the owner is typing or dragging in it is HELD (not shown, not thrown away, not marked as seen) and offered again the moment the typing ends: without a change of his own the other device's version is shown; with a change his is the newer one and stays, the other version goes to `SafetyCopies` at the save that follows, and the clash line of 2.1 says so. Opening another board also ends the typing and offers the held news.
- A link `seedboard://board/<id>` (5.3) that arrives before the boards are loaded (the app was started by it) is held and followed once loading has finished. A board that is not on the device: if it has a removal mark, the removed-link line of 2.1 shows (with Bring back); otherwise "That board is not on this device yet. It opens when it arrives." and the board opens by itself if iCloud brings it before another board is opened. A result of the device's search and the Open board action follow the same path (7).
- Colour name of a board is one of `Palette.boards`: green, pink, orange, blue, teal, purple.

### 2.3 Board screen

Top bar (`primaryAction`): `board.undo` ("Undo", disabled when no step) and `board.redo` ("Redo") on regular width only (on compact width, the iPhone, they are the `UndoCluster` at the bottom left above the tools, same identifiers, greyed when there is no step), `board.more` (menu "More"), `board.send` ("Send to PARAGON", "Send" on compact width). The Send button is PARAGON purple (`Look.paragonButton`: #6B4EA8 by day, #7F62C6 by night; white words, contrast 6.4 : 1 day, 4.7 : 1 night; button style `ParagonButton`, the same shape as `FilledButton`: semibold body, padding 12 x 6, corner 8, 50 % when it cannot be pressed). Title = board title (inline on iOS).

More menu (0.5): Rename… (alert, text field `rename.field`), Pin to the top / Unpin, Save as a starter… (0.3; not shown while a starter is being changed or on a board that cannot be changed; 2.10), divider, Paste a list as a mind map (on a timeline "Paste a list as bars", 11.9; clipboard text; flash "Nothing to paste." in the problem tone if none; greyed on a Who does what board), Copy the board as text (flash "The board's words are copied."), **Make a timeline of it…** (`more.makeTimeline`; not on a board that places itself or a starter draft; grey on an empty board; 11.9), **Share…** (`board.share`, replaces "Share a picture…"; 11.12) and on iPhone and iPad **Print…** (`board.print`) — both only when the board is not empty — divider, Remove the board… (same confirmation as the list).

Canvas `board.canvas`: accessibility label "Board", value = `countLine`. Empty board: **0.5** the invitation of 11.4 (`board.empty`, now a container). A timeline board shows `timeline.canvas` instead (2.8).

Banners above the tools: read-only banner (a board from a newer format, or one holding a kind this version does not know, which is shown and can be looked at but not changed: "This board was made by a newer Seedboard. Update the app to change it."); arrow-tool banner ("Press the first shape, then the second." / "Now press the shape the arrow goes to.") with `board.linkCancel`; otherwise the selection bar `board.selectionBar`. A flash line `board.flash` (green capsule, 4 s) shows short messages.

Tool bar `board.tools` (a white plate, corner 15, a thin edge and a soft shadow; its own accessibility element with the buttons inside; scrolls sideways only when even the smallest tiles do not fit): nine tools in 0.3 (eight in 0.2), each 40 x 40 at the largest size (on iPhone and iPad with a press area of 46 x 46):

| Identifier | Help text | Action |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `tool.mindmap` | Mind map (M) | add a mind-map centre |
| `tool.box` | the shape tile (`ShapeTool`) | a plain press adds the shape picked last (a Box at first); hold 0.4 s, or the corner mark `tool.shapes`, opens the plate (below) |
| `tool.diamond` | Diamond (D) | add a diamond |
| `tool.arrow` | Arrow between two shapes (A) | toggles the arrow tool; with one non-frame item chosen the arrow starts there |
| `tool.note` | Sticky note (N) | add a note |
| `tool.text` | Text (T) | add free text |
| `tool.frame` | Frame (F) | add a frame |
| `tool.picture` | Picture (P) | asks for pictures (3.17): the photo picker (iPhone, iPad) or the open panel (Mac); the pictures are laid in the middle of the view. Its accessibility label is "Picture (P)"; part colour `Part.pictures` (day #0A7FA6, night #62C6EC); mark `Glyph.new(.picture)` |
| `tool.draw` | Draw (S) | switches the Draw tool on and off (3.15); the key S (0.3), help "Draw (S)" |

The tile size (0.3, `ToolSize` in `BoardView.swift`, environment value `toolSide`): the board screen offers `ViewThatFits` four rows of the ordinary tool row, tile side 40 (mark 24, gap 6), else 36 (mark 22, gap 4), else 32 (mark 20, gap 3), and last a horizontal scroll view at 32. The first that fits the width is used, so the row slides sideways only when even 32 does not fit. Before (0.2): eight tiles of 40, a row 374 wide that scrolled on an iPhone narrower than 394 points. With nine tiles the 40-point row is 9 x 40 + 8 x 6 + padding, wider than a small iPhone; the shrinking is what keeps it still. Timeline and Who does what boards have their own tool rows and are not changed (worked out for a 375-wide iPhone: about 264 and about 236 points, room to spare).

A new thing goes to the middle of the visible board, moved down-right by 34 points up to 14 times while its box (grown by 6) touches another non-frame, visible item (a frame is tested as 10 x 10). It is chosen and typing starts at once.

The plate with all shapes (`ShapePlate.swift`): `ShapePlate` has a heading in the shapes colour with Cancel (Escape), 8 tiles in 2 rows of 4 (the order of `ItemKind.flowShapes`: Box, Round box, Oval, Diamond, Person, Document, Slanted box, Store), each 54 high: the mark (26) with the kind's word under it; the current kind in full colour. Width 344 as a pop-up; the full width as a sheet. `PlatePresenter`: a pop-up when the width is regular (Mac, iPad, a big iPhone on its side), a sheet with a height detent (230; Lanes 250) when it is compact (iPhone). The plates of the Box and Picture tiles open ABOVE the tool row (`PlateEdge.overTile`: `.top` on the Mac, `.bottom` on iPhone and iPad, where iOS 18 and later read the edge as the side of the pop-up that carries the arrow; with `.top` the iPad squeezed the plate under the tool row into a 14-point strip — fixed in 0.5). `ShapeTool`: the tile shows the mark of the shape picked last; the corner mark is a small filled corner (7 points; help "All shapes"); on the iPhone the corner only shows and takes no presses. Title of the plate from the tile: "Add a shape". After a pick the plate closes first, then the shape is added and typing begins. The last pick is kept on the device (`UserDefaults` key `tool.lastShape`); Diamond is never kept (it has its own tile); with `-sampleData` nothing is read or kept (`ItemKind.boxTile(stored:)`). Identifiers: `plate.shapes`, `plate.shape.<stored name>` (adding), `plate.cancel`.

Zoom cluster (bottom right; on narrow widths a row above the tools, with the Undo cluster at its left on compact width): `zoom.out` ("Smaller", scale / 1.25), `zoom.value` (e.g. "100 %"), `zoom.in` ("Bigger", x 1.25), `zoom.fit` ("Show the whole board"). A timeline has its own cluster (2.8).

Selection bar (`board.selectionBar`, its own element with the buttons inside), shown above the tools while something is chosen and the board is not read-only. The chips are laid out by `WrapRow`: centred rows, and a further row when the width runs out (no scrolling). On iPhone and iPad every bar also has `sel.more` "Choose more" (before Remove; filled while on), which sets `choosingMore` so each press adds to or takes from the choice; Escape, pressing empty board or a new board clears it. On the Mac there is no such chip (Shift and the box do it). (Since the bar builder's work the bar no longer wraps and no longer sits above the tools on the Mac and iPad; see 2.9, which replaces this paragraph and the button lists below where they differ: the button "Copy" is now "Duplicate", the arrow's bar has 7 buttons.)

- One idea: `sel.child` "Idea under"; `sel.sister` "Next idea" (not for a centre); on iPhone and iPad only, for an idea that is not a centre, `sel.moveUp` "Move up" and `sel.moveDown` "Move down" (pale at 40 % and switched off when `canShiftIdea` is false); `sel.fold` "Fold"/"Unfold" (only when it has ideas under it); `sel.sides` "Both sides"/"One side" (centre only) or `sel.colour` "Colour" (non-centre); `sel.todo` "To do"; `sel.copy` "Copy"; `sel.words` "Words"; `sel.remove` "Remove".
- One shape (any of the eight): `sel.shape` "Shape" (`ShapeChip`: one button showing the current kind's mark; it opens the plate titled "Change the shape", whose tiles are `sel.shape.<stored name>`; the pick is one Undo step); `sel.colour`; `sel.todo`; `sel.copy`; `sel.words`; `sel.remove`.
- One sticky note: `sel.colour`, `sel.todo`, `sel.copy`, `sel.words`, `sel.remove`.
- One text: `sel.todo`, `sel.copy`, `sel.words`, `sel.remove`.
- One frame: `sel.lanes` "Lanes" (full colour when the frame has lanes; 3.12), `sel.colour`, `sel.copy`, `sel.words`, `sel.remove` (no To do).
- One arrow: `sel.words`; `sel.style.elbow` "Right angles", `sel.style.straight` "Straight", `sel.style.curve` "Curved" (the current one is filled); `sel.ends.oneWay` "One way", `sel.ends.bothWays` "Both ways", `sel.ends.none` "No ends" (the current one is filled); `sel.dashed` "Dashed" (filled when on); `sel.turn` "Turn round"; `sel.colour`; `sel.remove`.
- Several things: text "N chosen", `sel.todo`, `sel.copy`, then `sel.lineUp` "Line up" (from 2 tidyable things), `sel.evenGaps` "Even gaps" (from 3), `sel.sameSize` "Same size" (from 2 sizeable things), `sel.remove` (3.11).
- The colour row `board.colours` (its own element with the dots inside): dots `colour.<name>`; shapes and arrows also have `colour.none` first. Sticky notes offer `Palette.notes` (yellow, pink, blue, green, orange, purple), everything else `Palette.names` (pink, orange, teal, blue, purple, green, yellow, red, grey). The row closes when the choice changes. A ring marks the colour in use (`InUseRing`, `Look.swift`): `Circle().stroke(Color.primary 85 %, lineWidth: 2)` round the dot with a 3-point gap, taking no room of its own; the dot also gets the "selected" trait for VoiceOver. Which colour is in use is the Core rule `Board.colourInUse(of:)`: a shape or arrow its own colour ("" = No colour), an idea its branch's colour, a plain note `yellow`, a plain frame `teal`.
- Setting a colour on a mind-map idea sets the colour of its main branch (nothing for a centre).

Finger sizes (`FingerSizes.swift`: `Finger`, `pressRoom`; iPhone and iPad only, the Mac's sizes are unchanged). `pressRoom(across, down)` on iOS is `padding(+)` → `contentShape(Rectangle())` → `padding(−)`: the press area reaches further than the look and the layout is unchanged. iOS / Mac: chip height 36 / 28, press +4 above and below (44); chip and row gap 8 / 5; zoom button 36 x 36 / 28 x 28, press +4, 6 extra points before "whole board"; Undo / Redo 36 x 36 / 30 x 28, press +4, gap 8 / 2; colour dot 30 / 22, gap 14 / 7, press +7 (44), the row is a `WrapRow` on iOS (it wraps; on the iPhone the ten dots take two rows), its plate a rounded rectangle radius 21 on iOS and a capsule on the Mac; tool tile 40 x 40 on both, press +3 on iOS (46); the notice's Undo: the whole pill is the button, press +11 above and below; `FilledButton`: press +5 above and below; room beside the tools for the clusters 176 / 150.

Typing field `board.field`: sits over the item (for an arrow: where its words stand). On iPhone and iPad a keyboard toolbar (`.keyboard` placement) holds the keyboard bar (3.8) with the "Done" button `board.fieldDone` that keeps the words. Behaviour in 3.8. The count line is also a 1 x 1 clear element `board.count` (the screen tests wait for it). Beside it `board.chosenPlace`: a 1 x 1 clear text that says the chosen idea's place among the sisters of its side, e.g. "1 of 2"; empty when anything else is chosen (for screen tests).

### 2.4 Send page (`SendSheet`)

**0.5:** the page gains the row "Add what is new to “…”" (11.15), the box "What PARAGON gets" with its count line (11.16), the switches "Say where they come from" and "Also remind me when a bar starts" (11.16), corrected row words, the Send button's tick and the purple line after Send (11.4). The text below is 0.4's and stays true where 11.15–11.16 do not change it.

Title "Send to PARAGON". Rows `send.kind.note` ("As a note"), `send.kind.project` ("As a project"), `send.kind.todos` ("Only the to-do boxes") with explaining lines. The words of the rows: note — "The board's picture and its words as a tidy outline. It goes to Resources, and one line in the Inbox points to it." (timeline: "The timeline's picture and its bars with their dates. …"); project — "The branches become headings. The ideas you ticked To do become tasks; with nothing ticked, all of them do. It goes to Projects." (timeline: "The phases become headings, the bars become tasks with their start and due day. It goes to Projects."). Field `send.title` (name; note and project only; starts as `noteTitle(for: board.title)`). A line says how many to-do boxes become tasks; when "As a project" is chosen it is replaced by the project line `send.projectLine` (5.6). When the board was sent before: "Last sent <yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm> as a <kind>: “<title>”" and `send.openLast` ("Open in PARAGON"). Fixed line: "Seedboard only adds something new. It never changes a note you already have; sending again makes a second note." Errors in `send.problem` (red). Buttons `send.cancel` (Escape) and `send.send` (Return; label "Sending…" while busy; full-colour purple, paler when disabled).

The branch switch `send.branchOnly` (`SendBranch.swift`) shows only when exactly one idea is chosen; off at first: a row with the mark `.branch`, the words "Only the branch “<words>”" (a centre: "Only the map “…”"; no words: "Only this branch"; words longer than 32 letters are cut to 30 + "…") and a drawn switch 38 x 22 (PARAGON purple when on). Its accessibility value is "On" / "Off". When on, one line under it: "Just this idea and what hangs under it is sent, with a picture of it alone. The board is not marked as sent." Rules in 5.7.

Size: Mac 470 wide, as tall as its content. iPhone and iPad: the content (at most 560 wide) sits in a scroll view that only bounces when it does not fit; the only detent is large, so on the iPhone (and on an iPad in a narrow window) the page is full height and the title is never cut; on the iPad in a wide window the page is a card sized to its content (`presentationSizing(.form.fitted(horizontal: false, vertical: true))`, iOS 18 and later; on iOS 17 the system's own card size). When the page closes the board takes the keys back.

- If PARAGON is not found: `send.notReady` shows the line from Settings; Send is disabled.
- Send is enabled when PARAGON is found, not busy, and: for "Only the to-do boxes" at least one unsent to-do box with words AND an Inbox list reachable; for the others the board is not empty.
- On open the kind is "note", except when the board has no mind map, unsent to-do boxes exist and every item is a to-do box: then "to-do boxes".
- After success the page closes, the board is saved with `sent` and `sentTodos`, and a flash line says what happened.

### 2.5 Settings (`SettingsView`)

Sections: Help (`settings.howItWorks`, `settings.whatsNew`), PARAGON (`settings.paragonLine`, `settings.checkParagon` "Check again"), Your boards (`settings.safetyLine`; `settings.notInCloud`, only while boards wait for iCloud: "1 board is not in iCloud yet. It is safe on this device." / "N boards are not in iCloud yet. They are safe on this device."; `settings.saveWarning`, only when a write failed, in amber; Mac only `settings.showFolder` "Show the boards folder", which opens the device's `Boards` folder; then the rows of `KeptSettingsRows`: `settings.kept` "Removed and earlier versions" (opens the page of 2.7), `settings.export` "Export all boards" with the line `settings.lastExport` ("Last export: 18 Nov" or "No export yet."), `settings.import` "Import boards…" with the line `settings.importLine`), About (`settings.version`). Done `settings.done` (Escape on the Mac). `GuidePage` (`guide.page`) shows `Guide.guide`, `WhatsNewPage` (`whatsNew.page`) shows `Guide.news` (newest first).

PARAGON line texts: "Looking for PARAGON…"; "Sample boards: nothing is sent anywhere." (with `-sampleData`); "PARAGON's notes are found. Boards can be sent as a note, a project, or tasks for the Inbox."; "PARAGON's notes are found. Notes and projects can be sent; the Inbox cannot be reached from this device."; "PARAGON's notes were not found on this device. Open PARAGON once with its own iCloud folder, then check again."

Save warning (also shown once as a flash when it appears): "“<Title>” could not be saved on this device. Keep Seedboard open: it tries again by itself." (several: "N boards could not…"), or, when only iCloud's copy failed, "“<Title>” could not be put into iCloud. Nothing is lost: the device has it, and Seedboard tries again by itself." The warning goes away when the write succeeds.

Safety line: "<N board(s)> · last safety copy today|yesterday|d MMM HH:mm", or "· no safety copy yet", or in sample mode "· example boards, nothing is saved". When iCloud is off (no account, or sample mode excluded) " · iCloud is off: the boards are on this device only" is added at the end.

Export and import: rules in 6.2.

### 2.6 Menus (`Menus.swift`: `SeedMenus`; `BoardCommands` in `BoardView.swift`; `IdeaOrderCommands` in `MapOrder.swift`)

The same lines are on the Mac's menu bar and, with a hardware keyboard, on the iPad and iPhone window, where holding ⌘ lists them.

**0.5:** File is now New Board ⌘N, New Blank Board ⇧⌘N, —, Rename…, Send to PARAGON… ⌘↩, **Share…** (replaces Share a Picture…; 11.12), **Make a Timeline of It…** (11.9), —, Save as a Starter…, Starters…, —, Remove Board…; then **Print… ⌘P** in the system's print place (11.12) and, on the Mac, **Import from iPhone or iPad** (11.18). Board: the paste line reads "Paste a List as Bars" on a timeline (11.9). Help (Mac): How Seedboard Works ⌘?, What's New (11.18). The Dock menu and Services: 11.18. ⌘[ goes back through a door (11.7; it hangs on the "‹ name" button, not on a menu line).

- File (`CommandGroup(replacing: .newItem)`; no "New Window", one window as before; 0.3 adds, after Share a Picture… and a divider, **Save as a Starter…** (grey when the board cannot be changed or a starter is being changed) and **Starters…** (opens the Starters page; both open the sheets of 2.10)): New Board ⌘N → `MenuDesk.choosingStarter` (the starter page: `StarterSheet`, the starter cards as a sheet with Cancel, Escape on the Mac; identifiers `starters.sheet`, `starters.cancel`; the cards keep `starter.<name>`); New Blank Board ⇧⌘N → `AppModel.newBoard(.blank)`; Rename… → the board's own "Name of the board" question; Send to PARAGON… ⌘↩ → ends typing, opens the Send page; Share a Picture… → the board's PNG (the same picture as for PARAGON, written to the app's temporary folder as `<name>.png`) in the system's share choice (Mac: `NSSharingServicePicker` under the top bar; iPad: the share page; "The picture could not be made." when it fails); Remove Board… → the board's own "Remove …?" question.
- Edit: Undo ⌘Z, Redo ⇧⌘Z (`CommandGroup(replacing: .undoRedo)`; disabled with nothing to undo/redo; while words are typed on the board they go to the text field). `CommandGroup(after: .pasteboard)` adds Duplicate ⌘D (`BoardSession.duplicateSelection`; in 0.1 it was "Copy the Chosen Things" in the Board menu). On the Mac, Delete and Select All are the system's own lines; the drawing surface answers `delete:` and `selectAll:` (`BoardKeys`, `.onCommand`) while it has the keys — Delete only with something chosen on a board that can be changed, Select All only on a board that is not empty. The Delete KEY is still the board's own key handling, so one press never removes twice. On iPad/iPhone a Select All ⌘A line is added: on the board it chooses everything, anywhere else it is passed to the text field that has the keys. Cut, Copy and Paste are not touched. Paste (⌘V) on the canvas pastes a list (3.10) — on macOS only (`onPasteCommand`).
- View (`CommandGroup(before: .toolbar)`): Zoom In ⌘+ (x 1.25), Zoom Out ⌘- (/ 1.25), Actual Size ⌘0 (zoom 1; on a timeline 0.3: back to the Weeks scale, 3.19), Show the Whole Board ⌘1 (`fit`). (In 0.1 ⌘0 showed the whole board.) The on-screen buttons keep their words (Smaller, Bigger, Show the whole board).
- Insert (`CommandMenu`; 0.3: after the two extra shapes and a divider come **Picture…** with the key P, `PictureInsertLine`, then **Draw** with the key S, `drawLine`; both grey on a board that cannot be changed and on a timeline or Who does what board, and the letters are keys only when `lettersAreTools`, as for the others): the lines of `MenuRules.tools` (Mind map M, Box B, Diamond D, Arrow A, Sticky note N, Text T, Frame F) and, below a divider, `MenuRules.moreShapes` (Round box U, Oval O). A click adds the thing in the middle of the view, ready to type in (`BoardSession.add`); Arrow switches the arrow tool on or off, starting at the one chosen shape. Grey with no board or on a read-only board. The letter on a line is a real key only when `MenuRules.lettersAreTools` says so: the drawing surface has the keys (`MenuDesk.keysBoard` is this board, the Mac window is the one typed in), no words are typed, nothing and no arrow is chosen, the board can be changed. In every other state the line has no key, so a menu key can never take a letter from words. (**Note**: the Insert lines are not switched off on a timeline board; there they would add an ordinary thing the timeline does not draw.)
- Board (`CommandMenu`): To Do ⇧⌘T (grey with nothing chosen); Line Up, Even Gaps, Same Size (grey when there are too few; no keys); Move the Idea Up ⌘↑, Move the Idea Down ⌘↓; Paste a List as a Mind Map ⇧⌘V (clipboard text; grey with no board, on a read-only board and while words are typed).
- Move the Idea Up / Down on the Mac are never switched off (a menu key that is switched off can swallow ⌘↑ and ⌘↓, which move the cursor in every text field): when the key window's first responder is a text view and the board is not typing an idea (or the key window is a page/sheet), the line hands the key to the text (`moveToBeginningOfDocument` / `moveToEndOfDocument`); otherwise it moves the idea (`BoardSession.ideaToShift`). On iPhone/iPad the lines are switched off when no idea can move. (**Note**: on the Mac these two menu lines take ⌘↑ and ⌘↓ before the board's own key handling, and do nothing when a shape is chosen; "the next shape upward / downward" by ⌘↑ / ⌘↓ (3.9) may therefore not arrive on the Mac. Not proven in a real run.)
- Lines that cannot act are grey: with no board open everything except New Board and New Blank Board; Delete and Duplicate with nothing chosen; Share a Picture on an empty board; Rename and the Insert lines on a board made by a newer Seedboard.

Space and a drag (Mac only, `SpaceHand` in `Menus.swift`): Space is taken by the board only when the drawing surface has the keys, no words are typed, no text field is the first responder, and ⌘, ⌃, ⌥ are up. While Space is down: pointer = open hand; mouse down inside the board = closed hand; each drag step slides the view by the pointer's way (`BoardSession.pan`, so the board counts as moved by the owner). Mouse up: open hand; Space up: arrow. The press is never seen by the board's own drag handling, so nothing is chosen, moved or boxed. Space up without a drag, held under 0.5 s (`MenuRules.shortPress`), exactly one thing chosen (`MenuRules.spaceStartsTyping`): typing starts in it (in 0.1 it started when the key went down). The window or the app losing the keys while Space is down ends it.

### 2.7 Removed and earlier versions (`KeptPage.swift`, identifier `kept.page`)

- Title "Removed and earlier versions"; a List with two sections, row minimum height 10. Colour: the part `Part.kept` (day #B4492B, night #F0A183, a warm brick red) for the page's headings, marks, Bring back, Done, the lines at the top (2.1) and the row in Settings. Marks: `Glyph.new(.bringBack)` (heading "Removed", the row in Settings), `Glyph.new(.earlier)` (heading "Earlier versions"), `Glyph.new(.export)` (Export all boards); disclosure `ControlMark.chevron` (13, turned 90° when open); close `ControlMark.close` (15 in a 26-point press area).
- Reached: pushed inside Settings (iPhone, iPad, and the Mac's Settings window); as a sheet over the main window (520 x 540 on the Mac, with the title as a heading because a Mac sheet has no title bar) from the clash line, from the launch argument `-removed`, and from `model.keptPage = KeptPageAsk(board:)`.
- Done `kept.done`: full colour in `Part.kept`, `.cancelAction` (Escape). Pushed, it goes back to Settings; as a sheet, it closes the sheet.
- Removed row (`kept.removed.<title>`): drawing 50 x 38 (the board's own painter, whole board fitted, day or night ground, corner 6), name (body medium), counts (caption, `Board.countLine`), the line `kept.removedLine.<title>` "removed 12 Nov · 18 days left" (caption; in `Part.kept` when 3 days or fewer are left; 0 days = "last day", 1 = "1 day left"), **Bring back** (`kept.bringBack.<title>`, FilledButton `.kept`), **Remove for good** (`kept.forGood.<title>`, caption, borderless, secondary).
- Confirmation `kept.forGoodConfirm`: "Remove “Title” for good?" — "This cannot be undone. Links to it from PARAGON's notes will no longer open it." — Remove for good (destructive) / Cancel.
- Footers: "A removed board is kept for 30 days. Bring back returns it as it was, on every device." and "A copy of every board is kept once a day, 14 days back; a version that lost against another device's is kept 30 days. Open as a copy makes a new board; the board itself stays as it is."
- Empty: "Nothing was removed in the last 30 days." (`kept.removedEmpty`) / "No earlier versions are kept yet." (`kept.earlierEmpty`). Removed copies iCloud has not delivered yet are asked for and counted: "1 more removed board is on its way from iCloud." (`kept.waiting`).
- Board row of Earlier versions (`kept.board.<title>`): chevron, name, "3 earlier versions" plus " · removed" (the board is among the removed) or " · not in your boards any more" (only the copies are left). Version rows (`kept.version.<title>`) are indented 21: source line (subheadline; `KeptRules.sourceLine`: "daily copy" / "from another device" / "this device's version" / "set aside" + ", " + the version's date), counts (caption), **Open as a copy** (`kept.openCopy.<title>.<daily|away|here|aside>`; a soft button: `Part.kept` words on its soft tint, corner 8).
- A group opens by itself when it is the only one, or when the page was asked for at that board (then it is also scrolled to).
- Bottom line after Bring back (`kept.notice`): "“Title” is back." + **Open** (`kept.open`; capsule in `Part.kept.fill`, like the board's notice). Problems use the same capsule (`kept.problem`): "“Title” could not be brought back: no copy of it can be read.", "That version could not be read any more.", "That version was made by a newer Seedboard. Update the app to open it."
- Rules and files: 6.1.

### 2.8 Timeline screen (`TimelineView.swift` input, `TimelineDraw.swift` painter, `TimelineBars.swift` bars and the Dates page, `TimelineSession.swift`)

A board whose `kind` is "timeline" is shown by `TimelineView` (identifier `timeline.canvas`; empty: `timeline.empty`) instead of the canvas; `TimelinePainter` draws the live board and the picture. Rules in 3.13.

- Sizes: names column 170 (iPhone / narrow: 104); dates band 44; row 34 and bar 22 on the Mac, row 40 and bar 24 on iPhone and iPad; bar corner radius 7; names 13 (Mac) / 15; phase names semibold in the phase's colour; rows under a phase stand 10 further in; a milestone's name has a small diamond before it.
- Colours: bars `Paint.line(name)` (day and night sets); phase band `Paint.soft` at 55 % (night 40 %); the phase's thin bar 6 high at 80 %; weekend columns (Days only) edge colour at 20 %; date lines edge colour; Today line and tag `Paint.line("red")`, line 1.6, white "Today" in a red capsule in the top of the dates band (a small word that would touch it is left out); today's number is red at the Days scale; chosen row: a soft green plate in the names column and a 2-point green ring round the bar; grips: 12-point white circles with a green edge at both ends; the dot: a 10-point green circle 20 behind the end, joined by a dotted line; To do: the purple tick at the right of the name.
- Part colour `Part.timeline` (#C4551F day, #F59A66 night) for the Bar tool, the Colour and Dates chips, the Today button, the Dates page and the starter.
- Tools (`board.tools`, each with its word under it): `tool.bar` "Bar" (B), `tool.milestone` "Milestone" (M), `tool.phase` "Phase" (P), `tool.after` "Comes after" (A; "press the bar that comes first, then the one after it").
- Zoom cluster `timeline.zoom`: `zoom.out` ("Further away: weeks, then months"), `zoom.value` (Days / Weeks / Months), `zoom.in` ("Closer: weeks, then days"), `zoom.fit` ("Show the whole timeline"), `timeline.today` ("Bring today into view (T)").
- The chosen bar's buttons (`board.selectionBar`) stand at the bottom above the tools (at the top they would cover the dates): `sel.days` (its dates in words), `sel.name` "Name", `sel.colour` (the nine colours, `board.colours` › `colour.<name>`), `sel.dates` "Dates", `sel.todo`, `sel.copy`, `sel.more` (iPhone and iPad), `sel.remove`; for a chosen arrow `sel.after` (its words) and Remove. Several rows chosen: "N chosen", To do, Copy, Remove. The "comes after" hint and the read-only notice stay at the top.
- Dates page: `dates.start` "Starts", `dates.end` "Ends", `dates.length`, `dates.cancel` (Escape on the Mac), `dates.save`. Changing Starts moves the whole bar (its length stays); changing Ends makes it longer or shorter. When the bar comes after another: "It comes after <names>, so it cannot start before <day>."
- Press areas: grips ±12 outward (on a short bar only their outer side, so the middle can still be moved); dot ±11; bars +4/+5; arrows within 7.
- Drags: bar middle = days (nearest whole day; its dates show above it) and, one bar alone, rows (from 0.7 of a row up or down); grip = that end; dot = pull a "comes after" line (a dashed ring on the bar it would land on); name = reorder rows; empty place or the dates = slide. A press on the dot switches "Comes after" on from that bar. Two presses on a name or a bar = rename.
- Several rows chosen (Shift on the Mac, Choose more elsewhere): they move together by days.
- Zoom: the buttons step the scale; a pinch, ⌘-scroll, ⌘+ / ⌘- add up and step once per factor 1.2; the day under the pointer stays in place. The rows cannot slide further than the last row plus room for the tools (150; iPhone 215). The timeline has its own gestures (a drag and a pinch); the touch layer of 3.7 (two fingers slide and zoom together) is the ordinary board's. On the Mac two fingers scroll.
- Keys (no typing in progress): ↑ ↓ choose the row above or below; ← → move the chosen bar a day (with Shift a week); Return = next bar (`addBarAfter`); Space = rename; Delete = remove; Escape = choose nothing and leave "Comes after"; ⌘A = all rows; ⌘D = copy; with nothing chosen B bar, M milestone, P phase, A comes after, T today. With one row chosen a printable character starts typing its name.
- Undo: every change is one step through `BoardSession` (a drag, a stretch with everything it pushed, a row move, Dates Save, a new bar, a join). Typing a name is its own step.
- After a move that pushed others, a line says how many: "1 bar after it moved along." / "2 bars after it moved along."
- Read-only (newer format): everything can be looked at and slid; nothing changes.
- Not kept: the scale and the place of a timeline (it opens showing the whole timeline). Pasting a list does nothing on a timeline.
- Hooks in shared code: `BoardSession` (`timeline` state; `paste`, `duplicateSelection`, `pan`, `zoom`, `fit`, `reveal` hand a timeline to its own code), `BoardView` (canvas ↔ timeline; the three bars switch to the timeline ones), `CanvasDraw` (`BoardPainter.draw` hands a timeline to `TimelinePainter`, so pictures and small pictures show it).

### 2.9 The bar beside the chosen thing, the menu of a thing, copy / cut / paste, Home (`ChosenBar.swift`, `BarActions.swift`, `ChosenFrame.swift`, `BoardMenu.swift`, `ClipBoard.swift`; rules in `Core`: `BarPlace`, `Controls`, `Corner`, `Clip`)

This section replaces the description of the bar in 2.3 where they differ (picks 7, 17, 18 and the Home row).

**The bar (pick 7)**
- Two views, one content (`ChosenBar`): `FloatingBar` (horizontal size class regular: Mac, iPad) inside `BoardExtras` in `CanvasView`; `ChosenBarTop` (compact: iPhone, narrow iPad window) in `BoardView`, where `SelectionBar` was. Not on a timeline board (it has its own bar, 2.8), not on a Who does what board (3.16), not on a board that cannot be changed.
- Place (`BarPlace.spot(box:bar:room:)`, screen points): `box` = the chosen thing's box on screen grown by 5 (3 gap + 2), reaching up and down as far as its plus dots at their grown size; several things: the box round all; an arrow: the box round its line grown by 6. The bar is centred over the box, `gap` 12 above; if its top would be less than `air` 8 from the room's top: 12 below; if that does not fit either: at the room's top edge. Across it is clamped to 8 from the room's left and right. `room` = the board view minus 64 points at the bottom (the tools). A thing that has slid out of sight keeps its bar at the nearest edge.
- Hidden (floating bar only): while a drag goes on on the board (`BarDesk.dragging`: a thing, an idea, a size knob, a plus, an arrow end, the choosing box; not while the board itself slides), while words or a lane's name are typed, while the arrow tool is on. The iPhone's top bar stays, as in 0.1. The bar forgets an open colour row when it hides.
- One row, always (`ViewThatFits`), the first that fits: (1) Mac / iPad: every button, words on `.word` and `.wideWord` buttons; (2) every button, words on `.word` buttons only; (3) every button, a word only on the first main button and on buttons that open a list (Line, Ends), and no "N chosen" label; (4 and on) as (2) with one more button behind "…" each time: the buttons with the highest `drop` number go first, the later one first among equals; (last) the row scrolls sideways. `drop` 0 buttons are never hidden.
- "…" (`sel.overflow`): opens `ActionList`: a pop-up on Mac / iPad, a sheet with a height detent on the iPhone (`PlatePresenter`; height = lines x 42 + 76, lines 2 to 9). It is lit while open and while a hidden button is switched on (Choose more).
- Tint: `BarActions.part(of:)`: idea -> mind map, shape -> shapes, note -> notes, text -> text (ink), frame -> frames, arrow -> arrows, several of one kind -> that kind, mixed -> boards. To do -> `Part.paragon`; Remove -> `Part.kept` (red, was grey). A hairline (1 x chip height - 10, primary 16 %) stands before the first of To do / Remove. The timeline's bar wears the timeline's orange, Remove red.
- Chips: height 28 Mac / 36 iPhone and iPad (`Finger.chipHeight`), mark 15, word in `Txt.pill`, corner 8, gap 5 / 8; a mark-only chip is square; press area +4 up and down on touch. Plate: `Theme.plate`, corner 13, 5 padding, shadow as before. A mark-only chip shows its name as a help text on the Mac.
- Buttons per kind (identifier, show, drop):
  - idea: Idea under `sel.child` word 0; Next idea `sel.sister` (not the middle idea) word 0; Words `sel.words` wideWord 1; Fold / Unfold `sel.fold` (has ideas under it) wideWord 3; Both sides / One side `sel.sides` (middle idea) wideWord 3; Colour `sel.colour` (not the middle idea) wideWord 2; Move up / down `sel.moveUp` / `sel.moveDown` (iPhone, iPad; not the middle idea) icon 5; Duplicate `sel.copy` wideWord 4; Choose more `sel.more` (iPhone, iPad) icon 2; then, behind the hairline, To do `sel.todo` and Remove `sel.remove`.
  - shape: Shape `sel.shape` (the plate, `ShapeChip`) word 0; Colour word 2; Words wideWord 1; Duplicate 4; Choose more 3; To do; Remove.
  - note: Colour word 2; Words word 1; Duplicate; Choose more; To do; Remove.
  - text: Words word; Duplicate; Choose more; To do; Remove.
  - frame: Lanes `sel.lanes` (`LanesChip`) word 0; Colour word; Words; Duplicate; Choose more; Remove (no To do).
  - several: "N chosen" (`sel.count`); Line up `sel.lineUp` (2 and more), Even gaps `sel.evenGaps` (3 and more), Same size `sel.sameSize` (2 and more) wideWord 3; Duplicate; Choose more; To do; Remove.
  - arrow (7 buttons, before 10 or 11): Words word 0; Line `sel.line` which opens a list `sel.style.elbow|straight|curve` (Right angles, Straight, Curved); Ends `sel.ends` which opens a list `sel.ends.<raw value>` (One way, Both ways, No ends); Dashed `sel.dashed` wideWord 3; Turn round `sel.turn` wideWord 3; Colour wideWord 2; Choose more 4; Remove.
  - a drawing (3.15) or any newer kind: Duplicate, Choose more, Remove, until it gets its own list in `BarActions.itemBar`.
- "Copy" in the bar is now called "Duplicate" (`sel.copy` is its identifier, unchanged; it makes a second one right beside the first, ⌘D as before); real Copy, Cut and Paste are below. The timeline's bar also says Duplicate.
- Kept identifiers: `board.selectionBar`, `board.colours`, `colour.none`, `colour.<name>`, every `sel.*` of 0.1 and of the other 0.2 builders. New: `sel.overflow`, `sel.line`, `sel.ends`, `sel.count`, `plate.actions`, `plate.back`, `plate.cancel`. Colour dots: unchanged look (22 / 30 points, ring on the one in use), under the bar.

**Frame and controls (pick 7)**
- Drawn by `ChosenLayer`, a layer over the board's own drawing (never in a picture). `BoardPainter` no longer draws the frame or the handles (`handles(for:)`, `Handle`, `HandleKind`, `handleGap`, `handleSize` are gone; `session.chosenControls()` and `Controls.round` replace them).
- Frame: `paint.chosen`, 2 screen points wide, 3 screen points outside the outline (`Controls.frameGap`): box -> rounded box (corner 9 + gap); round -> capsule; oval -> oval; diamond -> diamond with every edge pushed out by the gap; person, document, slanted, store -> their own outline (`ShapePaths`) on the box grown by the gap; note -> corner 4 + gap; frame -> 14 + gap; middle idea -> 11 + gap; an idea on a branch and free text: the box grown by 5 across and 4 down, corner 9 (as 0.1).
- Plus dots (`Controls.round`): middle 17 screen points outside the box (`Controls.reach`), on right, down, left, up of a shape and a note; right and down of free text; the one outward side of an idea. Not on a side where an arrow leaves or arrives (`Board.sidesWithArrows`, from the drawn arrows); ideas keep theirs. Not where its 18-point box would touch the words of an arrow. At rest: a 10-point dot, plate fill, green outline 1.6. Under the pointer (within 9 points) on the Mac, and always on iPhone and iPad: 18 points, green, white plus. Sizes shrink with the zoom below 100 % down to 55 %, as before.
- Size knobs: 9-point squares (corner 2.5, plate fill, green outline 1.8; 2.6 under the pointer) on the four corners of the frame round a shape, a note and a frame (before: one, bottom right). Press areas (`Fingers.control`): 9 points round each control always; at least 32 points for a finger when no thing lies under it.
- Sizing (`Corner.rect`, `Board.size(_:corner:from:by:reach:)`, `BoardSession.liveSize`): the dragged corner follows, the opposite corner is fixed; least size 44 x 28 (a frame: 120 x 80 or what its lanes need); snapping as in 3.11; when the words inside make the thing bigger than asked, it grows towards the dragged corner. `BoardSession.liveResize` stays in place, unused.

**The menu of a thing (pick 18, `BoardMenu.swift`)**
- Opened by: right-click or Control + left click (Mac); a two-finger click on an iPad trackpad (a tap recogniser with `buttonMaskRequired = .secondary` in `TouchSurface`); resting a finger or the Pencil for 0.5 s.
- On a thing: Words, Colour (leads on), To do, Copy, Cut, Duplicate, Arrow from here; an idea also Idea under, Next idea, Fold; a frame Lanes (leads on: Rows / Columns, 2 to 5); a shape Shape (leads on); Remove. With several things chosen: To do, Copy, Cut, Duplicate, Line up, Even gaps, Same size, Remove. On an arrow: Words, Line (leads on), Ends (leads on), Dashed, Turn round, Colour (leads on), Remove. On empty board: Paste, New note here, New box here, New idea here, Select all, Show the whole board; the new thing or the paste lands at the pressed spot. A board that cannot be changed: a thing's menu has only Copy; empty board has Select all and Show the whole board.
- Mac: `PointerCatcher` (an `NSView` behind the board) hears right mouse down and Control + left mouse down inside the board, not where a bar lies over it (`BoardShield` on the tools, the zoom buttons and the floating bar), not inside the thing being typed in; it builds an `NSMenu` (`MacMenu`) and pops it up at the pointer.
- Finger and Pencil: `HoldWatch` in `TouchSurface`: a timer of 0.5 s from landing (`Fingers.hold`); cancelled when the press strays more than 4 points (`Fingers.still`, the same distance at which the board starts a drag) or a second finger lands. When it fires the menu opens, a medium tap is felt, the press stays alive but its moves are held back; more than 10 points from the landing (`Fingers.letGo`) closes the menu and hands the moves on (a drag); lifting ends the press without a "press" on the thing. Not on a plus dot, a size knob or an arrow's end dot. An idea still lifts after 0.25 s as before.
- What is pressed is chosen first (added to the choice while Choose more is on); on empty board the choice is cleared. Typing is ended, the arrow tool switched off.
- The plate on iPhone and iPad (`BoardMenuLayer`, identifier `board.menu`): 250 wide, lines 40 high, a heading with the kind's name in its colour and Cancel (`plate.cancel`); placed by `BarPlace.spot` round the pressed spot (above the finger, else below, 8 inside); scrolls when taller than the board; a press anywhere else closes it. Lines that lead on open in place with a way back (`plate.back`); colours show as dots.
- Identifiers: `menu.words`, `menu.colour`, `menu.colour.<name>`, `menu.colour.none`, `menu.todo`, `menu.copy`, `menu.cut`, `menu.duplicate`, `menu.arrow`, `menu.child`, `menu.sister`, `menu.fold`, `menu.lanes`, `menu.lanes.none`, `menu.lanes.rows|columns`, `menu.lanes.<way>.<n>`, `menu.shape`, `menu.shape.<kind>`, `menu.lineUp`, `menu.evenGaps`, `menu.sameSize`, `menu.remove`; arrow: `menu.line`, `menu.style.<raw>`, `menu.ends`, `menu.ends.<raw>`, `menu.dashed`, `menu.turn`; empty board: `menu.paste`, `menu.note`, `menu.box`, `menu.idea`, `menu.selectAll`, `menu.fit`.
- Pointer (Mac): a tracking area on `PointerCatcher`; over a size knob `NSCursor.frameResize` for that corner (macOS 15 and later; a crosshair before); over a plus dot or an end dot of the chosen arrow the pointing hand; the hand of Space-and-drag is left alone. On the iPad pointer there is no resize arrow or hand (the plus dots are always big there).

**Copy, cut, paste (pick 17, `ClipBoard.swift`; rules `Clip`, `Board.clip`, `Board.paste`, `Board.addWords` in `Core`)**
- Clipboard forms: `com.schabbauer.seedboard.clip` (JSON of `Clip`: `format`, `items`, `links`), plain text (`Clip.text`), PNG (the board painter on the clip as a small board, white ground). Nothing is stored in a board file: no new stored fields.
- `Board.clip(of:)`: the chosen things, ideas with all ideas under them, the arrows whose two ends are both copied; a timeline's rows and things of a kind this version does not know are left out; the top idea of a copied branch carries its branch's colour.
- `Board.paste(_:at:)`: new identities for every thing and arrow; the clip's middle lands on the point; frames go underneath; a branch without its parent becomes a middle idea (its ideas keep the branch colour); maps are laid out; returns the things to choose. `Board.paste(_:under:)`: a clip of ideas only hangs under an idea (the "hang under" rule). A clip from a newer Seedboard (`format` above this version's) is refused with a line of words.
- Where: the menu's pressed spot; else the pointer when it is over the bare board (Mac, iPad pointer); else `freeSpot` near the middle of the view. At a spot, the same clip pasted again within 20 points lands `Clip.step(again:)` = 28 x n right and down; after Copy the copied things' own place counts as the first paste.
- Words (`Board.addWords`): two non-empty lines or more -> `addMap`; else one sticky note. With one idea chosen: under that idea (`BoardSession.paste`, as 0.1).
- Keys: Mac: the system's Edit > Cut / Copy / Paste reach the board through `.onCommand` (`ClipKeys`) while the drawing surface has the keys; Delete and Select All are untouched. iPad / iPhone: `ClipCommands` (twice in `SeedboardApp.swift`) replaces the pasteboard group with Cut ⌘X, Copy ⌘C, Paste ⌘V: on the board they act on the board, anywhere else they are passed to the text field that has the keys. As a second way `CanvasView.handleKey` answers ⌘C / ⌘X / ⌘V when no menu line took them. Cut = Copy + Remove (one Undo step). While words are typed, ⌘C, ⌘X, ⌘V, ⌘A belong to the words.
- Drop: `.onDrop` of plain text on the drawing surface -> `addWords` at the drop point (a list becomes a mind map).
- Option-drag (Mac, `BoardSession.dropCopy`): decided when the drag is let go; everything the drag moved (a frame with what lies in it, a map with its ideas) is put back and `Board.duplicate` makes the copies at the drop offset; one Undo step. The copy shows only when the drag is let go.
- Choosing box with a finger: with Choose more on, a finger drag on empty board is the choosing box; what it catches is added to what was chosen before the press.
- Line of words after Copy: "Copied. Paste it here or in another board." / "N things copied. ...". On a timeline board and on a Who does what board (`board.placesItself`) Copy, Cut and Paste do nothing (grey on the Mac); their own bars have Duplicate (`ClipBoard.canCopy` / `canPaste`).
- A drawing carries any stored field untouched; strokes are stored from the drawing's own corner (3.15), so they travel with a paste, a duplicate and an Option-drag.
- "Paste a list as a mind map" (More menu, Board menu Shift ⌘V) is unchanged and now overlaps with plain ⌘V.
- Duplicate or Copy of a frame takes only the frame (the rule of `Board.duplicate`); Option-drag of a frame copies what lies in it.

**Home row**
- `RootView.swift`, `BoardList`: the first row of the list when not on the iPhone layout, above Pinned; `model.open(nil)`; bold and tinted while no board is open; identifier `boards.home`. Its mark is the sprout (`seedSprout`) until a house is drawn. On the iPhone the list itself is the home.

**Tests**: `BarClipTests.swift`, 8 data tests (bar place; sizing from a corner; no plus on a side with an arrow + four knobs + not on words; a still press of 0.5 s; copy with inner arrows (+ text, + newer clip refused); paste with new identities at a spot (+ another board, + frame underneath, + the 28 step); a branch copies with its children (+ hangs under an idea); words become a note / a list a map). Screen tests `UITests/BarUITests.swift` (2, written, not run: every kind's bar exists and is under 60 points high; Copy in a thing's menu, Paste in the menu of empty board of another board, Undo). `MapsUITests.testMoveDownChangesTheOrder` opens `sel.overflow` first on the iPhone when `sel.moveDown` is not in the row; `ShapesUITests` ends the typing before it looks for `sel.shape` / `sel.lanes`, because the floating bar hides while typing.

### 2.10 New screens of 0.3: pictures, the Starters page and its sheets

**Pictures** (`PictureViews.swift`; rules 3.17). No new page and no Cancel / Save: a picture is an ordinary thing on the board.

- The tool `tool.picture` (2.3), the key P, Insert ▸ Picture… and, on empty board, the menu line `menu.picture` "Picture…" (after "New idea here"; the picture lands where the menu was pressed) all send the same ask (`PictureDesk.shared.ask`, with the spot or nil for the middle of the view); the board's `PictureHost` answers. iPhone and iPad: Apple's photo picker (`PhotosPicker`, images only, up to 12 at once, no permission question). Mac: an open panel titled "Choose a picture", button "Choose", several files, images only.
- A chosen picture has its own bar (`BarActions`): `sel.copy` "Duplicate", Choose more (iPhone and iPad), `sel.remove` "Remove". No Words, no Colour, no To do. The menu of a picture (press and hold / right-click): Copy, Cut, Duplicate, Arrow from here, Remove. A double press or a letter key does not start typing. Four corner squares resize (3.17).
- A picture whose file is missing (or is still coming from iCloud) is drawn as a soft plate in `Part.pictures` with the picture mark and the words "Picture not here yet"; the file is asked for again and the board redraws by itself when it arrives (`PictureHost` listens to the store).
- Lines said (`board.flash`): "A picture cannot be put on this board." (timeline, Who does what); "That could not be read as a picture." (one) / "3 could not be read as pictures." (several).
- Paste and drop: `ClipBoard.swift` (3.17).

**The Starters page** (`StartersPage.swift`, identifier `starters.page`). A sheet (Mac: minimum 460 x 500) holding: a header with the mark `.app(.seedStarter)` (22), the heading "Starters" (`Txt.big`, `Part.boards`) and the full-colour **Done** (`starters.done`; Escape on the Mac); a `List` (`starters.list`, plain style, on iPhone and iPad always in edit mode so every row has a drag handle) of `model.shelf.shown`, one row each (`starters.row.<id>`): the starter's small picture (`BoardThumb(starter:)`, 64 wide), name (`.body` medium, 1 line), grey line (`.caption`, 2 lines), and at the right a small text menu **Edit** (`starters.more.<id>`, accessibility label "Edit <name>") with **Rename…** (`starters.rename.<id>`), **Change…** (`starters.change.<id>`), **Back to the original** (`starters.original.<id>`; only for a changed built-in) and **Remove…** (`starters.remove.<id>`; not for the Blank board). The same lines are the row's press-and-hold / right-click menu. Dragging a row calls `moveStarter` (3.18). Footer: "Drag a starter to move it. A new board begins as a copy of the starter you press." At the bottom, only when `shelf.missingBuiltins`: a section with **Bring back the built-in starters** (`starters.bringBack`, mark `.new(.bringBack)`). The page reads the shelf again when it appears.
- Remove asks: a confirmation titled `Remove the starter “<name>”?` with **Remove** (`starters.removeConfirm`, destructive) and Cancel.
- Where it opens from: the small **Edit** text button beside the heading "STARTERS" on the home page (`home.startersEdit`, accessibility label "Edit the starters", help "Rename, move, change and remove starters"); the **Edit** button in the "Start with" choice (`starters.edit`, which closes the choice first); File ▸ Starters… on the Mac; **Edit starters…** in the menu of any starter card; launch word `-starterPage`.
- The home page's starter row now shows `model.shelf.shown` (heading "STARTERS" in `Part.boards`, caption bold, kerning 0.5), cards `home.starter.<id>`; in the choice `starter.<id>`. `<id>` is a built-in's raw value ("mindMap") or a UUID string.
- Card menu (`starterMenu`, press and hold / right-click on a starter card, on the home page and in the choice): Rename…, Change…, Remove… (not for Blank), a divider, Edit starters….

**The name sheet** (`StarterNameSheet`, shown by `StarterAsks`): identifiers `starterName.sheet`, `.field`, `.hint`, `.cancel`, `.save`. For **Save as a starter** the heading is "Save as a starter", one field "Name" (filled with the board's title), and the note "A new board can begin as a copy of this one."; for **Rename…** the heading is "Rename the starter", the field "Name" and a second field "The grey line under the name" (`starterName.hint`), and the note "The grey line says what the starter is for." Buttons: Cancel (Escape) and the full-colour **Save** (Return; off while the name is empty). Mac: 360 wide; iPhone and iPad: a sheet 210 high (270 for Rename). Saving a board as a starter gives it the grey line "Your own starter", and says `Saved as the starter “<name>”.` (`board.flash`). A name that a shown starter already has becomes "Name 2", "Name 3" … (`freeName`).

**Changing a starter** (`StarterChangeBanner`, `starter.change.banner`): "Change…" opens the starter's working board (`workingBoard(for:)`, 3.18) in the ordinary board screen, with a pill at the top of the board in `Part.boards.soft`: the words `Changing the starter “<name>”` (1 line, shrinks to 0.8), **Cancel** (`starter.change.cancel`, Escape) and the full-colour **Save** (`starter.change.save`). Save (`saveChange`) stores the board and says `Saved the starter “<name>”.`. Cancel with changes (`dirty`) asks "Discard the changes?" with **Discard** (`starter.change.discard`, destructive) and "Keep changing"; Cancel without changes just ends. While a starter is being changed (`session.starterDraft`): the title is the starter's name; Rename renames the starter (`renameChange`); More has no Pin, no Save as a starter and no Remove; the top bar has no Send to PARAGON; File ▸ Send, Save as a Starter… and Remove Board… are grey. Leaving without Cancel or Save — opening another board (`open()`), going back to the list, or on the Mac quitting the app (`leaving(closing:)`) — calls `leaveChange()`: when something was changed (`dirty`) it is saved exactly as by Save (the same line is said); when nothing was changed the change just ends. Only Cancel (after the question) throws changes away. `leaving()` also flushes the starter files.

**Tests** of 0.3: 3.19 and section 10.

### 2.11 New in 0.4: the caption and the Crop page

**Caption** (`BarActions.swift`, `CanvasView.swift`, `PictureViews.swift`; rules 3.20). A chosen picture has the bar button **Caption** (`sel.caption`) and the menu line **Caption** (`menu.caption`), both before Crop. Bar of a picture (0.4): Caption, Crop, Duplicate, Choose more (iPhone and iPad), Remove. Menu: Caption, Crop, Copy, Cut, Duplicate, Arrow from here, Remove. Two quick presses on a picture start the same typing (`session.beginCaption`); letter keys on a chosen picture still do not. The words are typed in place under the picture in the board's usual field (`board.field`): Return ends, Escape cancels, Shift + Return makes a new line. Empty = no caption.

**Crop** (`CropPage.swift`). The bar button **Crop** (`sel.crop`) and menu line **Crop** (`menu.crop`) set `session.cropping`, which `BoardView` shows as a sheet `crop.page`. Header: the mark `.crop` and "Crop the picture". Body: the WHOLE picture fitted into the page; over it a frame `crop.frame` (accessibility label "Crop frame") with four round grips `crop.grip.topLeft`, `crop.grip.topRight`, `crop.grip.bottomLeft`, `crop.grip.bottomRight`; everything outside the frame is dimmed. Dragging a grip changes the frame; dragging inside the frame moves it. The frame never leaves the picture and is never smaller than 44 points on screen, nor than 5 % of the picture's width or height (`Item.cropLeast` 0.05). The page opens with the picture's present part in the frame. Buttons: **Cancel** (`crop.cancel`; Escape on the Mac), **Whole picture** (`crop.whole`; the frame becomes everything) and the full-colour **Save** (`crop.save`). Save runs `Board.cropPicture` inside one change, so one Undo takes it back. **Crop is greyed** on a picture whose file is "Picture not here yet" (`enabled: here`). Launch word `-cropPicture` (with `-sampleData -board balcony`): chooses the first picture and opens its crop page.

## 3. Rules (SeedboardCore and the canvas)

### 3.1 Data (Board.swift)

- `ItemKind`: idea, box, round, diamond, oval, note, text, frame; new in 0.2: bar, milestone, phase (a timeline board's own things), person, document, slanted, store (shapes), drawing (3.15), and who, message, band, section (a Who does what board's own things, 3.16); new in 0.3: picture (3.17); new in 0.5: door (11.7; `Item.door` = the board it leads to, `Item.text` = that board's name as last seen). New item field in 0.5: `doneDay` (ISO day a timeline bar or milestone was marked done, 11.10). New board field in 0.5: `sentTasks` (`[SentTask]`: `id` of the thing, `words` = the task line as sent without "- [ ] ", `place` = "inbox" or "note"; 11.14). The stored names are these words and never change. `ItemKind.flowShapes` = box, round, oval, diamond, person, document, slanted, store (the plate's order); `isShape` is true for all eight. Words: Idea, Box, Round box, Diamond, Oval, Sticky note, Text, Frame, Bar, Milestone, Phase, Person, Document, Slanted box, Store, Picture.
- Default sizes (w x h): idea 90 x 30, box 150 x 56, round 150 x 52, diamond 160 x 104, oval 150 x 72, note 150 x 110, text 120 x 28, frame 440 x 300, person 110 x 96, document 150 x 68, slanted 160 x 56, store 130 x 80, picture 240 x 180 (only a fallback: a new picture is laid by `Board.pictureSize`, 3.17); bar, milestone, phase 150 x 22 (not used: a timeline places by days and rows). A new centre idea is made 150 x 46 (then fitted to its words).
- `Item`: id, kind, text, x, y, w, h (a rectangle, top-left origin, y down), colour (a name; "" = the kind's own), todo, parent (idea only; nil = a centre or not an idea), order (place among sisters), folded, bothSides (centre only); new in format 2: `side` (a main branch of a both-sides map: the side it stays on; nil = not settled yet), `start`, `end` (timeline: ISO days such as "2026-12-01"; a milestone keeps only `start`, a phase neither), `row` (timeline: Int, 0 at the top), `lanes` (a frame only; 3.12); new in format 3: `picture` (a picture only: the name of its file, 3.17). Not stored fields of their own: `unknownKind` (the kind word from the file when this version does not know it) and `extra` (the file's fields this version does not know).
- `Link` (arrow): id, from, to, text, colour, style (`elbow` "Right angles" default, `straight`, `curve`), head; new in format 2: `fromSide`, `toSide` ("up", "down", "left" or "right": the side the owner chose for the start / the end; missing or an unknown word = found by the app), `tail` (true = a head at the near end too), `dashed` (true = drawn with dashes). `Link.ends` (`LinkEnds`: `oneWay`, `bothWays`, `none`; words "One way", "Both ways", "No ends") reads and writes head and tail: One way = head true, no tail; Both ways = head true, tail true; No ends = head false, no tail. `Link.isDashed`. Also `unknownStyle` and `extra`, as for an item.
- `SentInfo`: when, kind ("note" or "project"), title, path (e.g. "Projects/Summer cabin.md").
- `Board`: id, title, created, changed, format, pinned, colour (default "green"), items, links, sent, sentTodos (ids of to-do items already sent to the Inbox or inside a note/project); new in format 2: `kind` ("timeline", or missing for an ordinary board). Also `extra` (the file's fields this version does not know).
