- Timeline: the bars at the bottom, the name field's place, the Dates page and all drags were never seen on a screen; grip and dot sizes on the iPhone, the pinch stepping through the scales, dragging a name to reorder while the list is scrolled, the keyboard covering the names on the iPhone; trackpad scroll and pinch on the Mac over the timeline; keys reaching the timeline after a button was pressed; what ⌘0 (Actual Size) and the Insert menu do on a timeline.
- Home page and look: the purple Send button inside the real top bar (Mac toolbar, iPad and iPhone glass bar); the pop-up's arrow and size on the iPad; the height of the starter page on a small iPhone; whether the Mac puts the cursor into the search field at launch; scrolling the home page with 50 and more boards; the largest text sizes on the cards.
- Menus and Space (Mac): Edit ▸ Delete and Select All turn black and act when the board has the keys, and stay with the words while typing; ⌘A, ⌘D, ⌘N, ⇧⌘N, ⌘↩, ⌘0, ⌘1 arrive; Share a Picture's choice appears under the top bar; the open hand shows and stays, a short press of Space still starts typing, Space still types a space in every text field and still presses a focused button; whether a plain-letter menu key is given to the menu before the text field. iPad with a keyboard: the lines show when ⌘ is held; ⌘A in a name field chooses the words, not the board.
- Opening where you left: the board, zoom and spot come back on Mac, iPad and iPhone after a real quit; on the iPhone the board (not the list) is in front.
- From outside: Siri understands the phrases; the three actions show in Shortcuts and on the Action button; Raycast finds them; an action run while the app is closed opens it on the right board; the device's search shows boards by name and first words, opens the right board, and forgets a removed one.
- Removed and earlier versions, export: Bring back and Remove for good between two real devices through iCloud (marks cleared everywhere; the removed copies in iCloud really go; placeholders of removed copies arriving); the clash line after a real clash (two devices offline, both change a board, both come back); the save sheet for the export on iPhone and iPad and the save panel in the Mac's Settings window; the packed file opening in Files and the Finder; Import picking `.seedboard` files in Files; the look of the lines at the top on an iPhone.
- iCloud between three real devices (`.icloud` placeholders, " 2" files, set-aside versions), 120 Hz frame rate, and the first real Send into the real PARAGON notes: of a project by the ticks (checked against PARAGON's task reader by reading its code only), of one branch, of a frame with lanes, and of a timeline (dates read as due days; the project's `due:`).

### 9.5 What 0.3 did not see on a real device, and what it does not do

Seen in a simulator or on the Mac: the photo picker on the iPhone simulator opens and a chosen photo lands on the board; the screen tests for the Starters page and for a picture's bar pass on the iPad simulator; the Shift + Return test passes on the Mac. Everything else below was written and compiled, or tested as data, and not seen on a real device.

- The photo picker on a real iPhone or iPad and the Mac's open panel were not tried (the picker opened in the iPhone simulator). Paste of a screenshot and of image files copied in the Finder, and drops from Safari, Photos, Files and the Finder, were not tried.
- A picture arriving from another device through iCloud, the "Picture not here yet" plate and its redraw on arrival, and the 25-second tidy pass against a real iCloud folder were not seen. Corner resize of a picture by finger, how pictures look in the small list pictures (240 x 150) and memory with many 2048-pixel pictures (cache capped at 160 MB) were not tried. Starters made from a board with pictures: see below.
- Starters moving between devices (the files, the newer-wins rule, a removal reaching another device), the page's layout on an iPhone and its drag handles, the Edit menu inside an editing list, the banner on an iPhone, the dialogs, and day and night were not seen.
- The iPad trackpad and mouse on Timeline and Who does what boards (two-finger scroll, ⌘ + scroll, a trackpad pinch there), Shift + Return and Option + Return on an iPad keyboard (same code as the Mac, not tried), the new opening spot of a Who does what board on an iPhone, ⌘0 on a timeline and the key S were not seen on a screen. The tool row's three sizes (40, 36, 32) were worked out, not looked at on a small iPhone.
- Known gaps found while writing this part (read from the code, not run): (a) (the first draft of this list said the tidy pass ignores starters; the code and the lead say it searches them, so this is no gap); (b) (closed before shipping: "Export all boards" packs the picture files too, and Import boards takes them back, see 6.2); (c) leaving a change without Cancel or Save keeps it (see 2.10); (d) thumbnails of own timeline starters draw the plan at its saved days; (e) the text for PARAGON has a second wording, "A picture (on the board)", whose use the builder's note does not explain.

### 9.6 What 0.4 did not see on a real device, and what it does not do

Nothing of 0.4 was run on a device, a simulator or the Mac's screen by its builder; both apps compile, the rules are tested as data, the screen test is written, not run.

- The crop page under a finger (grip pressing by finger, mouse and trackpad; the sheet's size on the Mac; Escape), the board's redraw after a crop, a crop on a real photo, and Crop greyed while a file is missing.
- The caption's look beside its typing field: size, grey, centring, wrapping against the Core guess (3.20), and the field lining up with the drawn caption.
- The amber dot and the Settings line with real iCloud (pictures going up, the 3-second check, putting a picture back at the start).
- Not done: the room for a caption on Line up, Even gaps and dragging; the caption's room is a guess (3.20).

### 9.7 What 0.5 did not see on a real device, and what it does not do

Builders could use only the Mac debug build with made-up boards (the thumb lane alone used the iPhone simulator). Both apps compile; every rule has a data test; each new page has a screen test that was compiled, and only `ThumbUITests` was run (passed, 19 s).

Not seen on a real device or not seen at all:
- **Feel and motion:** no haptic was felt (iPhone, Mac trackpad); the iPad's lift pointer effect; the glides and land / lift / make-room motion with the eye on a device (smoothness, 120 Hz); the iPhone's typing-zoom glide with the keyboard arriving mid-glide; Send's purple in the top bar by night on any device; the slide / fade timings.
- **Home page and branches:** the iPad and iPhone (built only); the home card's press-and-hold menu; the dashed drop frame round an underlined idea; timeline and Who does what with the four marks.
- **iPhone thumb row:** VoiceOver, an SE-size iPhone, an iPhone on its side.
- **Thinking:** Tab / Next note / Next shape, the keyboard bar, dragging onto an idea, Turn into, Break into notes, By colour, Colour for several, Choose all of this colour, the Make a mind map page, paste of paragraphs (rule tests only; "Make a mind map" and "Tidy" seen by launch word).
- **Doors:** everything on iPhone and iPad (the sheet, press and hold, "‹ name" beside the system's back arrow — cut at 170 points); dragging a row on the Mac; the menus by hand; two presses / Return / ⌘[; a door to a board arriving late from iCloud.
- **Timeline:** iPhone and iPad (the corner line on the narrow names column, Catch up as a mark alone), a done bar by night, a pasted list on screen, dragging a phase by hand, the view coming back after a real restart.
- **PARAGON reading (echo):** iPhone and iPad; pressing Open in PARAGON; a note iCloud has not brought; his real PARAGON folder (never touched); coming to the front as the trigger.
- **Send:** PARAGON itself filing the lines (its code was read, build 340; a data test reads the aimed line with a copy of PARAGON's reader); how PARAGON shows a `seedboard://` link inside a task row; the page by night; iPhone and iPad.
- **Share, PDF, print:** pressing Share (the system's choice), Print… on any device, iPhone and iPad, the plate by night and with three What tiles, "Share this…" and the File lines by hand, a mind map and Who does what as PDF.
- **Sharing to Seedboard:** the real share page inside another app; the keychain fallback on a device; a signed archive with the extension.
- **Shortcuts and Handoff:** any action run from Shortcuts, Siri or Raycast; "Added to …"; "Open the board afterwards"; a picture handed to Add to board; Board as picture as a file; Find Boards in the Shortcuts app; the starter list in New board; Handoff between two devices; the search result with its picture.
- **Mac habits:** a real photo or scan from an iPhone; the Service from another app's menu; the Dock's own long press; the iPad's title menu; a real hand on the name or on Space.
- **iPhone extras:** the icon's menu (lines, marks, order; an iPhone shows only four lines, so the second pinned board falls off — to decide), the camera and the scanner, three-finger gestures and shake.
- **Pencil:** Tidy shapes with a real hand (the 0.4 s pause was only a timer's); everything on iPhone and iPad; "write straight onto a shape" by anyone.
- **Safety:** nothing with real iCloud (two devices, a full iCloud, files not arrived, parked versions, " 2" copies); a starter's working copy coming back after a real stop; 20 phone photos on one board; Export with missing pictures; Import of an export's pictures.

What 0.5 does not do (on purpose or left):
- Undo of "Move to a new board…" leaves the new board in the list. A door cannot be resized. A pasted (not dropped) board link stays a sticky note. No View-menu line for Back.
- Echo: a branch sent alone has no line and no done marks (its ideas show "in PARAGON"); Inbox to-dos are not read back; "5 of 13 done" is not in the narrow side list.
- ~~A bar marked Done on the board still goes to PARAGON as an open task (`- [ ]`).~~ **0.6:** fixed, see 12.1.
- ⌘V on an iPad or iPhone with a keyboard does not reach a timeline (More and ⇧⌘V do).
- Add what is new puts new tasks at the end of the Tasks part, not under their branch or phase heading.
- PDF: no outline page, no printing across several sheets; Print… always prints the whole board. The PDF's to-do marks may still be the plain ring (the share lane's note; the PNG has the four marks).
- Tidy, By colour and Make a mind map are not in the Mac's Board menu. (Until 0.18 two presses on empty board on iPhone and iPad showed the whole board, not a note; since 0.19 they make a note, see 25.1.) No names for colours.
- The Mac's Edit menu says "Undo", not "Undo Move".
- A picture laid larger than about 512 points can be a little softer in the picture for PARAGON (the 1024 copy), not in the PDF or print (files).
- A starter saved from a timeline with done bars keeps them done in every board made from it.
- Each separate "Add to board" starts a new column to the right (the shared arrival rule).

## 10. Tests

- Rules, 0.5: **434 data tests** counted in `Core/Tests` (291 in 0.4), in new files `Door05Tests` 12, `Echo05Tests` 11, `Extension05Tests` 8, `Feel05Tests` 9, `Iphone05Tests` 10, `Looks05Tests` 4, `Mac05Tests` 5, `Motion05Tests` 7, `Pencil05Tests` 11, `Safety05Tests` 11, `Send05Tests` 13, `Share05Tests` 8, `Shortcuts05Tests` 9, `Thinking05Tests` 11, `Timeline05Tests` 11, and the format-5 lists in `StoredFieldsTests` (which since format 5 also proves every field is really written, and watches strokes, lanes, starters and sent tasks). Each lane reported its own package green; the joined count was not run by the writer of this part.
- Screens, 0.5: **43 tests in 27 files**. New: `DoorUITests`, `EchoUITests`, `ShareInUITests`, `FeelUITests`, `IphoneUITests`, `HomeStripUITests`, `MacGuideUITests`, `PencilUITests` (2), `SendUITests` (2), `ShareUITests`, `ThinkingUITests`, `ThumbUITests` (run, passed), `Timeline05UITests`. `HomeUITests` now presses `home.starter.flow` where it pressed `empty.newBoard`.

- Rules, 0.4: **291 data tests (284 before)**: `CaptionCropTests` 7 (3.20) and the format-4 list in `StoredFieldsTests`/`PictureTests`. Screens, 0.4: **28 tests in 14 files** (27 before): `PictureUITests` has one more, `testCropOpensItsPageAndCancelClosesIt`. None of the new screen tests was run by the writer of this part.
- Rules (`swift test --package-path Core`): **284 data tests in 0.3 (258 before)**, all green. What 0.3 added: `PictureTests` 8 (3.17), `StarterShelfTests` 15 (3.18), `FixesTests` 2 (3.19), and the format-3 entry in `StoredFieldsTests`. The paragraph that follows is the older count, kept for its list of what each file tests. `BoardTests` 6, `StartersTests` 8, `EditTests` 21, `MindMapTests` 15, `OutlineTests` 20, `RouteTests` 9, `ParagonTests` 11 (board data and tolerant reading, starters, editing and hit-testing, mind-map layout and walking, outlines, the 0.1 arrow routing, the PARAGON texts and names); `SafetyTests` 11 (held news and what a board grew from, the dated removal mark and "a later change wins", the names of iCloud's copies, unknown kinds read-only and unknown fields carried, the Inbox line read by a copy of PARAGON's reader, the signs of PARAGON's folder, links that arrive early; and the stored-fields guard `StoredFieldsTests` with the golden lists of formats 1 and 2); `PanelFixTests` 9 (the view stays after a slide, sides of main branches, hanging a map under an idea of another and taking it off, moving out one level, the one-new-line-break rule, a shape's kind and size, wider first then taller, making room); new for the picks: `SnapTests` 14, `DotGridTests` 3, `TidyTests` 4, `HomeTests` 4 (search by name and by words, changed words, starters come coloured, colour in use), `TimelineTests` 19, `MapsTests` 7 (reorder, the drop decision, the project by ticks, one branch alone), `ArrowTests` 22, `TouchTests` 7 (two fingers slide and zoom, the shove taken back, a small thing's press area and nearest wins, a handle's press area, the iPhone's opening, typing zoom, the main thing), `ShapesLanesTests` 13, `KeptTests` 10 (days left, newest copy, copy title, tidy decision, kept file names, the version groups, the folders against a throwaway folder, export names and outline, the packed file, import), `RememberTests` 6, `MenuRulesTests` 3.
- Added by the last three builders (each counted in its own package run; all green at the time of their notes): `InkTests` 11 (3.15), `SequenceTests` 17 (3.16), `BarClipTests` 8 (2.9). Screen tests written and compiled, not run: `DrawUITests` 2, `SequenceUITests` 1, `BarUITests` 2. Two older screen tests were changed because a button moved (`MapsUITests.testMoveDownChangesTheOrder`, `ShapesUITests`). The totals in the line above are from before these three; the builders' notes give the package totals as 201, 200 and 230, each in its own copy.
- Tests changed for 0.2: `StartersTests` (the list of starter names ends with "Timeline"; "no starter overlaps its shapes" skips the timeline), `MindMapTests.testShiftIdeaMovesOnePlace` (a one-sided map), `EditTests.testAddNextJoinsAndKeepsKindAndSize` (an oval; a diamond is followed by a Box). `EditTests.testCarriedByAFrameTakesWhatLiesWhollyInside` still passes but its name is out of date (the rule is "its middle is inside", tested in `ShapesLanesTests.testAThingBelongsWhereItsMiddleIs`).
- Screens, 0.3: **27 tests in 14 files**. New in 0.3: `StartersUITests` 1 (the page lists every starter and closes with Done, `-starterPage`), `PictureUITests` 1 (a chosen picture's bar, `-board balcony -pickPicture`), `TypingUITests` 1 (Shift + Return makes a new line and the typing goes on; Return ends it; Mac only, `#if os(macOS)`, because the simulator's test keyboard cannot press Shift and Return together). Apple's run after each push is the safety net.
- Screens (the list of 0.2: 19 tests in 8 files; the 10 new ones were written and compiled, not run):
  - `SeedboardUITests` (9): the app opens with its boards; a new blank board is empty; the note tool adds a note; Return in a mind map makes the next idea and an empty one goes away; undo takes the last thing away; the Send page shows its three choices and Send is disabled without PARAGON; Settings shows Help and the version; choosing a shape shows its bar and Remove works; on the Mac Tab makes an idea under the chosen one.
  - `HomeUITests` (1): starter and board cards on the home page, "timber" finds "Summer cabin" and hides "Trip to Spain", "No board has these words", clear brings all back, the home page's New board opens the starters.
  - `TimelineUITests` (1): a new timeline takes a bar, shows its dates and zooms.
  - `MapsUITests` (3): the Send page offers the branch switch and the name follows it; no switch with nothing chosen; Move down changes the order (iPhone/iPad: `sel.moveDown`; Mac: ⌘↓) and Undo puts it back.
  - `TouchUITests` (1; skipped on the Mac): blank board, Bigger → 125 %, two presses on the canvas → 100 %, still "Empty". Since 0.5 two single presses with the launch word `-twoPressWindow 2` (DEBUG; `Finger.twoPressWindow`, 0.42 s otherwise): the test's own double tap comes 8 ms after the first press lifts and is lost on the iPad simulator (iOS 27) before the board hears it.
  - `ShapesUITests` (1): the plate opens, Person adds a thing, `sel.shape` and `sel.lanes` exist (touch: hold `tool.box`; Mac: `tool.shapes`).
  - `BringBackUITests` (1): the page lists both removed boards and the board with versions; Bring back shows the line with Open, the row leaves Removed, Done, and the board is in the list again.
  - `MenuUITests` (2): the starter page shows its choices and Cancel (`-starters`); on the Mac the menus list the tools and the zoom.

## 11. New in 0.5

Written from `Docs/notes-0.5/built-*.md` (door, echo, extension, feel, iphone, looks, mac, motion, pencil, safety, send, share, shortcuts, thinking, thumb, timeline) and the groundwork of `Docs/BUILDERS-0.5.md`. Every motion asks `Feel.moves` (false with Reduce Motion, `-sampleData`, `-stillMotion`) and jumps to its end state when false. A timeline and a "Who does what" board place their things themselves (`board.placesItself`): every new ordinary tool, menu line, paste and tidy command is off there.

### 11.1 Board format 5 and what is kept where

- `Board.currentFormat = 5`. New stored fields (all optional, `encodeIfPresent`, tolerant reading): item `door: UUID?` (the kind `door`), item `doneDay: String?` (ISO day), board `sentTasks: [SentTask]` (`id`, `words`, `place` = `SentTask.inbox` "inbox" / `SentTask.note` "note"; unknown fields carried in `extra`), starter entry `boardChanged` (a time; missing = `changed`; the content's stamp is the later of `boardChanged` and the board's own `changed`, `StarterEntry.boardStamp`, because a 0.4 app that changes the board carries an old stamp).
- Golden lists for format 5 (`StoredFieldsTests`): board — changed, colour, created, format, id, items, kind, links, pinned, sent, sentTasks, sentTodos, title; item — answer, bothSides, colour, crop, doneDay, door, end, folded, from, h, id, kind, lanes, order, parent, picture, row, side, start, strokes, text, to, todo, w, x, y; link — colour, dashed, from, fromSide, head, id, style, tail, text, to, toSide; sent — kind, path, title, when; stroke — colour, points, pressure, width; lanes — cuts, names, way; starter — board, boardChanged, builtin, changed, hint, id, name, order, removed, removedWhen; sentTask — id, place, words. From format 5 on, `testEveryStoredFieldIsReallyWritten` fills every field and proves it is written.
- A board this version changes becomes format 5. Seedboard 0.4 (and older) shows such a board read-only until that device is updated; 0.5 shows format 6 and above read-only. Same rule as every version before.
- Kept on the device only, never in a board and never in iCloud: `Application Support/Echo/read.json` (what was read from PARAGON, 11.14); `Application Support/Starters/<id>.working` (a starter being changed, field `starter`, 11.19); `UserDefaults` keys `seedboard.timelineViews` (11.10), `share.form` (11.12), `ink.tidyShapes` (11.11), `send.sayOrigin` (11.16), `seedboard.outsideArrivals`, `seedboard.searchTold.2` (11.17), `picturesUnnamedSince` (11.19); the way back through doors (memory only); the share-in waiting place (11.13). Nothing of these is written with `-sampleData`.
- Copies never carry sent marks: Make a copy, Import, an earlier version opened as a copy, a board from a starter, a duplicate clear `sent`, `sentTodos` AND `sentTasks`. Removing a thing forgets its entry; taking the To do tick off forgets its Inbox entry (its note entry stays).

### 11.2 Home page, branches, marks (looks lane; his picks 7, 8, 16's marks)

- Home page (`HomeView.swift`, `HomeStrip.swift`; Mac and iPad; the only home page): header sprout, "Plant a seed", "Every idea starts on a board." — no button. "STARTERS" heading with "Edit" (`home.startersEdit`) at the right, then ONE horizontal strip `home.starterStrip`: cards (`StarterStripCard`) 132 wide, 8 apart, picture 132 × 82 and the name in one line (subheadline, medium), no hint line (it is the help text and the spoken label). The Blank board keeps its quiet plus and is ALWAYS the first card (`StarterShelf.strip`, Core `Looks.swift`); the chooser and the Starters page keep the owner's order. Card menu as before (Rename…, Change…, Remove…, Edit starters…). Measured at 1280 × 800: strip with heading ≈ 150 points (0.4 ≈ 360); "PINNED" ≈ 265 under the top (0.4 ≈ 475); 12 board cards whole in sight (0.4: 2).
- Section headings `home.heading.Pinned`, `home.heading.Recent`, `home.heading.Sent to PARAGON`. Board card `home.board.<title>`: its press-and-hold / right-click menu is the list's own, word for word: Rename… ("Name of the board", Cancel / Save), Pin to the top / Unpin, Make a copy, Save as a starter…, Colour ▸, Open in PARAGON (sent board), Remove… ("Remove “<name>”?" — "It is kept for 30 days in the Removed folder." — Remove the board / Cancel). Gone: `empty.newBoard`, `-homeStrip`.
- Card words (echo, `DoneWords`): "Changed 2 days ago · 5 of 13 done"; narrower "2 days ago · 5 of 13 done"; narrower "5 of 13 done". An empty board's card: a faint sprout (22 pt, 35 %) instead of "Empty" (feel).
- Branches B: 3.4. Pictures (small, shared, PARAGON's) use the same painter.
- To-do marks: `TodoMark.draw(_ state:at:radius:paint:ground:&ctx)` (`TodoMark.swift`), used by `CanvasDraw`, `TimelineDraw`, `SequenceDraw`, `BoardThumb`, `BoardPicture`:

| State (`TodoState`) | Mark | Words |
|---|---|---|
| `waiting` | hollow purple ring, line 1.8 | as they are |
| `inParagon` | full purple disc, white arrow up and out (0.14; was a white tick) | as they are |
| `done` (read back) | full green disc, white tick | greyed |
| `cancelled` (read back) | full grey disc, white dash | greyed |

  Radius 7.5 on the board, 7 on a timeline row and a message. Purple #6B4EA8 (hollow line by night #B59CEB), green #2E8B57 / #3FA26C, grey #8B94A1 / #7A818C. Greyed words: the board's faint grey; on a sticky note the dark ink at 45 %. A thing shows a mark when it is ticked OR the board knows it was sent (`board.todoState(of:)`, laid over by `ParagonEcho.marks(for:)`). Change for 0.4 boards: an unsent to-do now shows the hollow ring.

### 11.3 iPhone thumb row (his pick 31)

- iPhone only (the iPhone itself, narrow layout; Mac, iPad, a narrow iPad window and a big iPhone on its side keep 0.4's top layout). `boards.new` and the search field leave the top; the list starts under the title "Seedboard".
- At the bottom, between the last row and the Settings line, ONE row `boards.thumbRow`: a thin line over it; left the search field (`boards.search`, 44 tall, capsule, ground 6 % of the text colour, green hand-drawn magnifier 18, "Search boards", a press anywhere starts typing, round "x" 19 = `boards.searchClear`); right, 10 apart, a round "+" (`boards.new`, 44 across, `Part.boards.fill`, white plus 22, spoken "New board"; pressed 12 % darker, 97 %). Room: 12 left/right, 8 above, 4 below (8 while typing).
- "+" opens the same Starters page from the bottom, also with the keyboard up. While typing, the row rides on the keyboard and the Settings line steps aside (`goneWhile`), back when typing ends. Settings and the version stay the last line.
- Files: `ThumbRow.swift` (`ThumbRow`, `ThumbSearchField`, `RoundFilledButton`, `View.goneWhile`). Screen test `ThumbUITests.testSearchAndPlusSitUnderTheThumbOnTheIPhone` (run on "Seedboard iPhone 18 Pro": passed). The window-level line sits above the thumb row.

### 11.4 Feel (picks 2, 4, 5, 6 and the eight rough edges)

- Haptics (`Feel.swift`; rule `FeelRules.mayCatch`): iPhone Taptic; Mac trackpad (guide = alignment, life size and the end = level change, set down / joined = generic). Catch (a NEW guide line while dragging, sizing or pulling a "+", at most once per 0.12 s); Lift (iPhone light 0.6: a shape, note, picture or group starts to move; an idea after its hold; a Who does what bar); Set down (rigid 0.7: a map hung on an idea; an idea landing under another or between sisters); Joined (rigid 0.8: an arrow made; an end dragged onto ANOTHER shape); To do on (off is silent); Exactly 100 % (a pinch passes life size; not on a timeline); The end (20 % / 300 %, once); Sent / Not sent. Nothing while the Pencil draws, for plain buttons, or with `-sampleData`. iPad: no motor.
- `PressStyle`: a tile or bar button with its own plate → pressed 95 % in 0.08 s, back in 0.18 s, its colour laid over its plate (6 % → ≈ 12 % by day, 12 % → ≈ 24 % by night), pointer over → half of that in 0.12 s; a plain button → 95 %, pointer wash 5 %, corner 8; a button with an outer plate or a colour dot → 95 % only. `FilledButton` / `ParagonButton` pressed: solid, 12 % darker, 97 % (0.4: 75 % see-through); disabled = same colour at 50 %. Home cards: pressed 97 %; pointer → edge boards green 60 %, shadow 6 % / r 2 → 12 % / r 6 in 0.12 s; iPad: Apple's `lift` pointer effect. Reduce Motion: no shrink, 70 % opacity for the press.
- Send as a hand-over: "Sending…" → white hand-drawn tick on purple for 0.45 s (`send.done`), `Feel.sent()`, the page closes. The line: PARAGON purple (#6B4EA8 / #7F62C6), white send mark, "“Summer cabin” is in PARAGON under Projects.", pill **Open** (`board.flashAction`, white on white 22 %) opening the new note, 6 s. To-dos: purple, no Open. Failure: `Feel.notSent()`, page stays with its red line.
- Empty board (`EmptyBoard.swift`, `board.empty` container): sprout 40 (boards green 70 %), "What is on your mind?" (`empty.words`), three buttons with mark and word: Mind map (pink, `empty.mindmap`), Sticky note (amber, `empty.note`), Paste a list (green, `empty.paste`; 45 % while nothing is copied — asked without reading the clipboard, no paste banner; pressed anyway "Nothing to paste." red). 36 high on iPhone/iPad, 28 on the Mac; stacked on a too-narrow iPhone. Mac: "or double-click anywhere for a sticky note" (`empty.hint`). Fades in 0.15 s. Not on timeline / Who does what / with Draw on. A read-only board: sprout and "An empty board".
- iPhone with no boards (`FirstSeeds`, `boards.first`, `first.starter.<id>`): sprout 40, "Plant a seed", "Every idea starts on a board.", the starter cards two in a row (`FeelRules.showsFirstSeeds`).
- Rough edges: (1) Send off the top bar's glass (`OffGlassItem`, `sharedBackgroundVisibility(.hidden)` on iOS 26 / macOS 26) — its own #7F62C6 by night; (2) the not-in-iCloud dot is always amber; (3) list dots follow day/night; (4) the line at the bottom (`FlashLine`, `WindowFlash`) is drawn at the window's level when no open board is in sight (above the iPhone's thumb row), inside a board above the tools, never both; (5) tones `.good` green, `.paragon` purple, `.problem` brick red — red now: "Nothing to paste.", "The picture could not be made.", "That could not be read as a picture." / "N could not be read as pictures.", "A picture cannot be put on this board.", "These things were copied in a newer Seedboard. Update the app to paste them.", "This board was made by a newer Seedboard. Update the app to copy it.", "That board is not on this device yet. It opens when it arrives.", "That board could not be brought back: no copy of it is left.", the save warning, "These two are joined already.", "A phase is a heading: join the bars under it.", "That would go round in a ring."; (6) messages slide up 8 points and fade in 0.18 s, fade out 0.25 s (the line, the Undo notice, the arrow tool's banner); (7) the press-and-hold menu fades in growing from 96 % (0.12 s), fades out 0.10 s; (8) Undo and zoom boxes (also the timeline's) wear the bar's shadow (black 10 %, r 5, 2 down).
- Launch words: `-emptyBoard`, `-noBoards`, `-say good|problem|paragon`, `-pressLook over|down`, `-sendDone` (with `-send`).

### 11.5 Motion (picks 1 and 3)

- Glides (`Glide.swift`, `BoardGlide.swift`; ease-out, 87.5 % of the way at half time): zoom step of 25 % (buttons, ⌘+ / ⌘−, ⌘0) 0.22 s, the middle point fixed, the read-out counting (122 % half-way from 100 %); Show the whole board (button, ⌘1, menu line; until 0.18 also two presses on iPhone/iPad) 0.30 s; bringing an idea into sight (Tab, Return, plus, Shift-Tab, ⌘↑/⌘↓, arrow-key walking, a paste out of sight) 0.18 s; the iPhone's typing zoom and back 0.18 s (the keyboard arriving mid-glide retargets it). A press, click, scroll, pinch, slide or key (without ⌘) ends a glide where it is; pinch / scroll / slide stay 1:1. A new zoom press during a glide counts from its end (100 → 125 → 156 → 195 %). No glide on opening a board, on a window resize, or on a timeline. The three `withAnimation` wrappers are gone (they moved only the bar).
- Motion layer (`Motion.swift`, `BoardMotion.swift`): lands — 92 % → 100 % round the middle with fade, 0.16 s (its branch fades in); leaves — fade and 94 %, 0.12 s, drawn on top, arrows go at once; lifts — by day shadow 6 % / r 2 / 1 down → 16 % / r 8 / 4 down in 0.10 s (sticky note from 14 % / 3 / 1.5; things with no shadow from none), by night the outline 15 % lighter instead, no glow; words alone, a sketch and a frame show no lift; makes room — any change not made under a finger glides old → new place and size in 0.20 s (map re-layout, push-aside, Line up, Even gaps, Same size, Undo, Redo, an arrow-key nudge, a drag taken back, a newer version), arrows and branches follow, a thing moved again goes on from where it shows; the thing typed in never glides; folds — children slide into the idea and fade, 0.18 s, then the count dot; unfold the reverse; the map closes up in 0.20 s. Off with `!Feel.moves`, on boards over 150 things, and on timeline / Who does what. Pictures for PARAGON, Share, the small pictures and kept versions always draw the end state.
- iPhone Pro: 120 frames a second (`CADisableMinimumFrameDurationOnPhone`). Launch words (DEBUG): `-motionTry zoom|fit|add|child|remove|fold|move|lift`, `-motionFreeze <0…1>`.

### 11.6 Thinking (picks 10, 11, 12 and the paste bug; Core `Thinking.swift`, app `Thinking.swift`)

- Next note: Tab while typing a sticky note keeps the words and makes the next note (same colour, width and height), 16 to the right, tops in line; if taken, under the FIRST note of the row (notes within 40 points to the left, about the same height), left edges in line, 16 under the tallest note of the row; then further down by one note height + 16 until free. A next note left empty goes away with no Undo step and the note before is chosen. Making it is one Undo step. Tab on a chosen (not typed) note does nothing new.
- Keyboard bar (iPhone/iPad): a sticky note gets **Next note** (iPad) / **Next** (iPhone, narrower than 520) in the notes colour (`board.key.nextNote`); a shape **Next shape** / **Next** (`board.key.nextShape`); then Done. 30 high, mark 17. All notes share one typing field and all shapes one, so the keyboard stays up.
- Mac: two clicks on empty board within 0.42 s always make a note at that spot (the first click un-chooses).
- Turn into (menu line "Turn into" with Sticky note, Box, Text, Idea; several; bar behind "…", which now always shows when something can be turned): notes, texts, all flow shapes, ideas — not frames, pictures, sketches, doors, nor a centre with branches. Keeps words, tick, middle, arrows, colour (into a note: same paper if notes have it; teal → green, red → pink, grey / none → yellow; an idea brings its branch colour; into a text the colour name is kept). New kind's own size (note 150 × 110, box 150 × 56), a shape → Box keeps an owner-given size. Into an idea: a lone centre. An idea turned leaves its map; its children move up to its parent at its place. One Undo step; things stay chosen. Identifiers `sel.turn`, `sel.turn.note|box|text|idea`, `menu.turn` (+ `.note|.box|.text|.idea`).
- Drag onto an idea: ONE chosen note, plain Box or text over an idea → dashed green frame; on drop it springs back for a moment and becomes the last idea under that idea (words, tick, arrows stay). On a centre: a main branch keeping its colour if branches have it, else the next free branch colour; elsewhere the branch's colour. Line "Now an idea" with Undo, 6 s; `Feel.setDown()`.
- Make a mind map (from 2 notes / texts chosen; bar `sel.makeMap`, menu `menu.makeMap`): question "The middle idea", line "The 4 chosen notes become the branches of one mind map round it.", field `makeMap.field` (the board's name), Cancel / Save (Return / Escape). Branches in reading order (rows from the top, each from the left); centre in the middle of where they lay; empty → "Ideas"; > 4 branches → both sides. Notes in a named frame hang under a branch with the frame's name (placed at the frame's reading position); a chosen frame that held only these notes goes away. Colours: kept if a branch colour, a yellow note takes the next free one. One Undo step; the centre is chosen and in sight.
- Break into notes (`menu.breakNotes`, an idea's menu): the idea and its subtree become notes in one column from the idea's top left, 16 apart, list order, 150 wide, height ≥ 110 as the words need, paper from the branch colour (teal → green, red → pink, centre → yellow). Ticks and arrows stay; the map closes up; the notes are chosen. Works on a centre.
- Colour for several (`sel.colour`, `menu.colour`, dots `colour.<name>` / `menu.colour.<name>`): six papers when a note is among them, else nine colours, "No colour" first when all are shapes; ring only when all show that colour; an idea colours its branch; texts, pictures, sketches untouched.
- Tidy (`sel.tidy`, `menu.tidy`; ≥ 3 notes / shapes / texts): grid at the pile's top left, gap 16, ≤ 5 across, rows filled evenly (6 → 3+3, 7 → 4+3, 11 → 4+4+3), reading order; column width = widest, row height = tallest. Frames, pictures, sketches, maps stay.
- By colour (`sel.byColour` behind "…", `menu.byColour`; ≥ 3): one column per colour in dot order (notes only: yellow, pink, blue, green, orange, purple; else pink, orange, teal, blue, purple, green, yellow, red, grey; none last), reading order inside, gaps 16, top left stays.
- Choose all of this colour (`menu.sameColour`, one note's menu): chooses every note with the same paper.
- Several-things bar as seen: "4 chosen · Line up · Even gaps · Same size · Tidy · Colour · Make a mind map · Duplicate · … | To do · Remove".
- Paragraphs: 3.10. Launch words: `-makeMap`, `-think tidy|byColour|map|note|box|text|idea|break|sameColour|next`.

### 11.7 Doors (pick 13; Core `Door.swift`, app `Doors.swift`, `DoorDraw.swift`, `DoorPage.swift`)

- Card 190 × 56, one size, no knobs or plus: left the other board's small picture 64 × 40; its colour dot 8 and its name bold 13, two lines, "…"; "›" at the right in its colour; edge 1.6, corner 10, in its colour; white plate by day with a shape's shadow, dark by night. Always the board as it is now. Chosen frame corner 10 + 3.
- Missing board: dashed grey edge, the door mark, the remembered name in grey and "This board was removed" (brick red, it is in Removed) or "Not on this device yet" (grey).
- Count "1 door" / "2 doors" after pictures; search finds a board by its doors' names; the remembered name is brought up to date quietly when the board is open (no Undo step).
- Opening: two presses / Return / Open → that board; top bar "‹ <name>" (`board.back`, boards green, tooltip "Back to “<name>” (⌘[)", ⌘[). A trail A → B → C; any other way of opening ends it; not stored. Removed: the top line "This board was removed on 18 Nov 2026." with Bring back (after 30 days: "… Its 30 days are over: it cannot be brought back."); the door's own Bring back returns the board to the list without opening it ("“<name>” is back."; none left: "No copy of this board is left to bring back." red). Not arrived: "That board is not on this device yet. It opens when it arrives." Own board: "This door leads to the board you are on."
- Making: (1) menu of empty board "Door to a board…" (`menu.door`, after "Picture…") → page `door.page`: heading "Door to a board" (`door.heading`), "Press the board the door leads to.", green "New board" (`door.new`; with search words "New board “<words>”"), "Search boards" (`door.search`, `door.searchClear`), rows `door.row.<name>` (pinned first, then newest; never the open board; "Changed 2 hours ago · 5 shapes · 5 notes" and the small picture), "No other board yet. Press New board." / "No board has these words" (`door.noMatch`), Cancel (`door.cancel`, Escape). Mac 380 × 480; iPhone/iPad sheet half height. New board makes an empty board (search words or "New board", " 2" if taken), not opened. (2) Drag a list row onto the open board (Mac, iPad; `DoorDrag`): a door there, chosen; own board: "A board cannot have a door to itself." (red); on boards that place themselves nothing. What travels is the board's link as words. (3) "Move to a new board…" (`menu.moveOut`, before Remove, for one, several and a door): "Name of the board" question (`door.moveName`; filled with the one thing's words or "Part of <board>"; "5 things move there. A door to it is left in their place." / "1 thing moves there. A door to it is left in its place."; Cancel / Save). The things move keeping place, colour, ticks and `sentTasks` entries (a task already sent is not sent again); an idea with its subtree; a frame with its things; a branch without its parent becomes a centre there with its colour, the old map closes up; arrows among them go along, arrows to things that stay now end at the door; ONE door left in the middle of the room they took (lowered in steps of its height + 12 if a closing map lies there), chosen; the new board joins the list, not opened; "Moved to the new board “<name>”" with Undo, 6 s. The new board is written BEFORE the things leave. Undo restores the things and removes the door; the new board stays.
- No door on boards that place themselves ("A door cannot be put on this board.").
- Bar: Open (`sel.open`) · Bring back (`sel.bringBack`, only when removed) · Duplicate · Choose more · Remove. Menu (heading "Door"): Open (`menu.open`), Bring back (`menu.bringBack`), Copy, Cut, Duplicate, Arrow from here, Move to a new board…, Remove. No words, no tick, no colour; can be moved, copied, cut, pasted (also elsewhere), duplicated, lined up; copies of a board keep doors. Arrows start/end at a door; others go round it.
- PARAGON: "## Doors" after Notes (note: before Tasks; project: before "## Log"), `- [Kitchen plan](seedboard://board/<id>)` (square brackets → round inside the link); Flow names the board ("- Ask Alex → Kitchen plan"); never a task. Copy as text: "Doors:" with `- <name>` after "Shapes:". The picture shows the card.
- Launch words: `-doorPage`, `-doorSearch <w>`, `-door <word>|removed|away`, `-doorBack <word>`, `-moveOut`, `-moveOutNow`.

### 11.8 Paste of words

- Words from elsewhere (⌘V, menu Paste, a drop from another app, "New board from text", Services, the share page, Add to board) follow `Outline.isList` (3.10): list → map; paragraphs → one Text (wraps at 360); one line → a sticky note; one idea chosen → the lines hang under it.

### 11.9 Make a timeline of it, and lists as bars (pick 15; Core `TimelineMake.swift`, app `TimelineOfIt.swift`)

- More "Make a timeline of it…" (`more.makeTimeline`) and File "Make a Timeline of It…": a NEW board "<name> timeline" (then " 2"), opened at once; the source is untouched; nothing is asked. Not on boards that place themselves or a starter draft; grey on an empty board.
- The mirror of the project rule (`ProjectPlan.swift`): main branch with ideas → phase in its colour; each idea below → bar in that phase in map order (deeper ideas right after their parent), branch colour; a main branch with none → bar above the first phase; a note / text marked To do → bar above the first phase, in its colour; a flow shape with words → bar (its colour, else the next bar colour), an arrow shape → shape = "comes after" (a question with two answers gives two bars that both start when it ends); a frame with lanes → one phase per lane "<frame>: <lane>"; the centre gives the name. Ticks decide as for a project; a chosen set decides instead of ticks. A ticked thing's bar keeps To do.
- Days: 7 each, from today; within a phase and above the first phase each after the one before, joined; shapes follow their arrows (unjoined shapes start today); phases start together; a passed-over shape is bridged.
- Lines (6 s): "12 bars in 3 phases. Drag them to their days." / "4 bars. Drag them to their days." / "1 bar. Drag it to its days." / "6 bars in 2 phases. 1 thing stayed on the board." / "4 bars in 2 phases. 4 things without a tick stayed on the board."; red: "Nothing here can become a bar. Bars are made of ideas, shapes and things marked To do." ("The board is empty: there is nothing to make bars of.").
- A list on a timeline: ⌘V (Mac, also Edit ▸ Paste), More "Paste a list as bars", Board "Paste a List as Bars" (⇧⌘V); off on Who does what. One bar per line, 7 days, chained; a line with indented lines → phase, all below → its bars in its colour, own chain; markers stripped. Nothing chosen → under the last row from today; a row chosen → right under it, a bar / milestone continues the chain (starts the day after, comes after); phases placed where they split no phase. New bars chosen, the first in sight; "5 bars pasted." / "1 bar pasted." / "7 bars in 2 phases pasted."; none: "Nothing to paste."; one Undo. Not reached: ⌘V on iPad / iPhone with a keyboard.

### 11.10 Timeline to plan with (pick 18; Core `TimelinePlan.swift`, app `TimelineProgress.swift`, `TimelineKeep.swift`)

- Done (`sel.done`, between Dates and To do; for several before To do when one is a bar or milestone; not on a phase): sets `doneDay` = today (full colour while done); again → off; several: all on, or all off when all were done. One Undo. A done bar: 36 % by day / 42 % by night, a tick in its colour at its end (inside from 22 points of width, else just behind; behind the diamond), its name grey. A bar `.done` in `todoStates` (read back) draws done without writing, counts done, is never late, is left by Catch up; taking Done off says "It is ticked off in PARAGON, so it stays done." A duplicate is not done.
- Late = last day before today and not done: red last 6 points and red name; a milestone red edge and name. The undone part before Today: black 20 % by day / 30 % by night over it.
- Corner line (`timeline.progress`): "3 of 8 done · 1 late · ends 20 Feb" (bars and milestones; "· 1 late" only when > 0, in red; "ends" with the year when not this year); count in ink, rest grey; 11 points (10 on the iPhone), up to three lines; none without bars. Drawn by the painter (in pictures too).
- Catch up (`timeline.catchUp`, after Today, only while late and changeable; red of the Today line, white words, the mark; iPhone mark only; help "Catch up: late bars are stretched to today, and what comes after moves along"): each late bar ends today; a late bar after another late bar keeps its length and starts after it; a late milestone moves to today; successors pushed; done bars untouched; one Undo; line 6 s "4 bars moved. The plan now ends 27 Feb (was 20 Feb)." / "… The plan still ends 20 Feb." / "1 bar moved. …".
- Week numbers on Weeks: every Monday "w 49" (9.5 pt, grey) over its date, the date 4 points lower; ISO weeks (Monday, week 1 holds the first Thursday; 1 Jan 2027 is week 53). `Day.weekNumber`, `weekWord`.
- A phase carries its rows (`Board.carryRow`): lands only where it splits no phase; a little into another phase stays, past its middle jumps past it all.
- The view comes back (`seedboard.timelineViews`, ≤ 200, oldest forgotten): scale, the day beside the names, the rows' slide, in days not points. Fit / never moved → whole. Not with `-sampleData` or scene launch words. Fixes a 0.4 trap (a slid timeline could come back at the year 2000).
- The echo line sits at the bottom above the tools on a timeline and a Who does what board.
- Launch words: `-makeTimeline`, `-doneBars <n>`, `-catchUp`; the sample timeline has two late bars by itself; `-scale weeks`.

### 11.11 Pencil: Tidy shapes and writing onto a shape (pick 30; Core `InkShape.swift`; app `PencilSwitches.swift`, `InkTidy.swift`, `ShapeScribble.swift`)

- Both ship OFF: `Finger.tidyShapesAtFirst = false` (the switch's default), `Finger.scribbleOntoShapes = false` (nothing of it is set up, no switch).
- Switch "Tidy shapes" (`draw.tidy`, value On / Off) in the Draw plate between the colours and Done: off = soft raspberry tint, on = full raspberry, white words; kept per device (`ink.tidyShapes`). Mac help: "Draw a box, an oval or a diamond and hold still for a moment: it becomes a real shape. A line from one thing to another becomes an arrow."
- Rule: only a stroke that BEGINS a new sketch; the tip still for 0.4 s at the end (trembling ≤ 6 screen points), then lifted. Loop → shape when closed (ends within 20 % of the box diagonal, or overrunning), 0.8–1.35 turns, ≥ 44 × 28, fits a rectangle / oval / diamond both ways within 3.5 % of the diagonal (best wins), lean up to 10° (tried in 2° steps; the shape stands straight), and lies over nothing (a frame does not count). Result: Box / Oval / Diamond of the loop's size and place (whole points), the line's colour (plain ink → the kind's own), no words, on top, not chosen. Line → arrow when not closed, starts at one thing and ends at another (inside or within 14 screen points; the top one wins; not frames, not sketches), ≤ 2.5 × the straight way, ≥ 12 long, not already there (the reverse is new); an ordinary right-angle arrow with a head. One Undo gives the raw line back, Redo the shape. Never on boards that place themselves or read-only boards. Stays a sketch: triangle, house, rectangle leaning 25°, scribble, oval twice round, spiral, figure of eight, straight line, there-and-back, corner, three sides, handwriting loop, flat loop, dot, a ring round a thing, a line to nothing / itself / a frame / a sketch, a wandering line, an existing arrow. Differs from the panel note on purpose: fit both ways at 3.5 % (not 8 %), the pause at the end, OFF.
- Writing onto a shape (when switched on; iPhone/iPad): `UIIndirectScribbleInteraction` over things that take writing (`Board.takesWriting`: idea, the eight shapes, note, text; not sketch, picture, frame, door, folded, boards that place themselves, read-only); writing beginning gives up the Pencil's press and takes the shove back unless the tip went > 10 screen points (then it is a drag); focus delayed; the hold menu cancelled; the field looked for every 0.05 s for 0.6 s; nothing while Draw or the arrow tool is on. Never seen running.
- Launch words: `-tidyShapes`, `-tidyDemo`, `-tidyDemo undo` (DEBUG, with `-draw`).

### 11.12 Share, PDF and print (pick 22; Core `SharePages.swift`; app `SharePlate.swift`, `BoardPaper.swift`)

- Lines: More "Share…" (`board.share`) and on iPhone/iPad "Print…" (`board.print`), both not on an empty board; File "Share…" and "Print… ⌘P" (grey with no board / empty board); menu of a thing or several "Share this…" (`menu.share`), after a line, before Remove (not on boards that place themselves).
- Plate `share.plate` (Mac 400 wide, a sheet; iPhone sheet 440 tall, pulls to full; iPad card): heading share mark + "Share", boards green. What: `share.what.whole` "Whole board" (always), `share.what.chosen` "What is chosen" (when something is chosen; an idea brings its subtree, a frame its things), `share.what.frames` "Each frame a page" (when a frame has a name). As: `share.as.picture` / `share.as.pdf` / `share.as.words`. Chosen tile: soft green fill, green outline, bolder words. Line `share.line`: whole — "One picture of the whole board." · "The whole board on one page, sharp at any size." · "The board's words as a small text file."; chosen — "One picture of the chosen thing." / "… of the 3 chosen things." · "The chosen thing on one page, sharp at any size." / "The 3 chosen things on one page, sharp at any size." · "The words of the chosen thing as a small text file." / "… of the 3 chosen things …"; frames — "One picture of the named frame." / "4 pictures, one of each named frame." · "One page with the named frame, its name on top." / "4 pages, one for each named frame, its name on top." · "The words frame by frame, each under its frame's name, as a small text file." `share.leftOut`: "1 thing lies outside the named frames and stays out." / "3 things lie outside the named frames and stay out." (a map counts as one). Buttons `share.cancel` (Escape), `share.share` (full green, Return; "Making…"). Problems `share.problem` (red): "The picture could not be made." · "The PDF could not be made." · "There are no words to share here." · "The share choice could not be opened." The share choice opens at the button; shared → the plate closes; left without sharing → it stays. Form remembered (`share.form`, first time Picture). From More/File: Whole board; from "Share this…": What is chosen. Boards that place themselves: Whole board only. Empty board (launch word only): "There is nothing to share yet."
- Picture: PNG as before (white, day look, 40 air, 2x; 1x over 3000); "<board>.png", "<board> - part.png", "<board> - <frame>.png" (" 2" for a twin name). PDF "<board>.pdf" / "<board> - part.pdf": the board's painter, lines and real letters, white, day look; A4 (Letter in US, Canada, Mexico by region); landscape by itself when wider than tall (timeline always; per page with frames); 28-point edge; top line grey: name at the left (bold, ≤ 70 letters), date at the right ("18 Nov 2026"; "18 Nov 2026 · 2 of 4"); frames in reading order (two are one row when the second starts above the first's middle), the frame's name as heading (15 bold); never larger than life size; top of the page, centred across; no soft shadows on paper. Words: "<board>.txt" = Copy the board as text (chosen: only those; frames: name, a line of dashes, its list; two empty lines between). "/ : \" → "-"; no name → "Board". Photos read from their stored files for that drawing. Files in a throw-away "Share" folder, cleared after an hour at the next Share.
- Print: Mac File ▸ Print… ⌘P (a sheet); iPhone/iPad More ▸ Print… (AirPrint; iPad at the top right under More). The whole board's PDF, one page fitted, landscape by itself (timeline always); job named after the board. Lines: "There is nothing to print yet." · "The page could not be made." · "Printing is not possible here."
- Checked: zoomed 800 % sharp; every word read back as text; Lanes and timeline PDFs hold no picture, Balcony exactly its two photos. Launch words: `-share`, `-shareWhat chosen|frames`, `-shareAs picture|pdf|words`, `-print`, `-shareTo <folder>` (DEBUG).

### 11.13 Sharing to Seedboard (pick 24; target `AMSSeedboardShare`, app `ArrivalBox.swift`)

- A share extension "Seedboard" (bundle id `com.schabbauer.AMSSeedboard.Share`, iOS + macOS, embedded, version/build from the app's settings; activation: text, 1 web link, 1 web page, up to 10 pictures). `Tools/release.sh` signs both and stops a release when the share page is missing or its version/build differs ("share page: 0.5 (N), keychain group yes, shared folder yes").
- Page (Mac 420 × 480): sprout + "Send to Seedboard" (green, large, bold); grey plate `shareIn.what`: "A link" / "Words" / "1 picture" / "Words and 2 pictures" / "A link, words and 1 picture" / "Nothing to send", the first two lines or the title (joined " · "), the address small, pictures 44 × 44 (`shareIn.picture`); while reading a spinner and "Getting what you shared…". `shareIn.newBoard` "New board" first, full green, ring and "+", picked at open; Pinned, Recent (green headings), rows `shareIn.board.<name>` (ring, dot, name), pinned first then newest, ≤ 30; not offered: timeline, Who does what, newer-format boards. Red `shareIn.problem`: "Pictures cannot be shared to Seedboard yet. Words and links can." (keychain fallback) · "Seedboard cannot take things from other apps on this device yet. Open Seedboard once, then share again." · "1 thing could not be read as a picture. Seedboard takes words, links and pictures." / "3 things could not be read as pictures. …" · "Seedboard takes 10 pictures at a time. 2 more were left out." · "That could not be kept for Seedboard. Press Send again." Grey `shareIn.later` "It is on the board the next time you open Seedboard." `shareIn.cancel` (Escape; takes away the pictures kept for this share), `shareIn.send` (full green, Return, paler — never grey — until sendable; writes once).
- In the app: words → note / list → map (`Board.addWords`; > 20 000 letters cut); a link → note "title\naddress" (or the address); a picture → shrunk in the extension to 2048 (on white, JPEG), `PictureStore.adopt`, a picture thing; each at `board.arrivalSpot` (a free column right of everything, one under the other). New board: one per Send, named first line / link title / site name / "Shared pictures" (≤ 40), next board colour, unique name. A removed or now-timeline target → a new board. Open board: one Undo step, the things chosen; while typing or dragging, retried after 3 s. Line: "4 things came in from Share." / "1 thing came in from Share." 6 s with **Show** (open board) or **Open** when all went to one board, else 4 s; "1 picture from Share could not be read." red. When: end of loading, every coming to the front (iOS `didBecomeActive`, Mac `NSApplication.didBecomeActiveNotification`).
- Safety: a line leaves the waiting list only after its board was written on the device (`saveNow` + `flush`, failed writes checked); a line read twice lays nothing twice (the thing carries the line's identity); unreadable lines stay; unnamed kept pictures swept after a day. The app tells the extension its boards (`boards.json`) 2 s after changes.
- Homes: (1) app group `group.com.schabbauer.amspara` (Mac `D24ENP83QQ.group.com.schabbauer.amspara`) `Seedboard/arrivals/` (`arrivals.jsonl`, `boards.json`, `pictures/`) — words, links, pictures; (2) fallback keychain group `D24ENP83QQ.com.schabbauer.AMSSeedboard` (after first unlock) — words and links only. Entitlements `App/Config/Share/AMSSeedboardShare.entitlements` and `…-macOS.entitlements` (app group in a marked block). Launch words `-sharePage`, `-sharePageNoPictures`, `-arrivalsFolder <f>`, `-arrivalsShow`.

### 11.14 What PARAGON did with it (pick 16 reading half, echo; Core `Echo.swift`; app `ParagonLook.swift` (`EchoLook`), `EchoKeeper.swift`, `SentLine.swift`)

- Line `board.sentLine` (container; `board.sentLine.words`, `board.sentLine.open`): soft purple capsule, thin purple outline, send mark 13, PARAGON purple words, pill size. "Project “Summer cabin” · 5 of 13 done · checked 14:02" + full-purple pill **Open in PARAGON**; "Note “…”"; no task: "Note “Ideas” · checked 14:02"; earlier day: "checked 17 Nov 14:02"; not here: "… · not checked on this device yet" (Open stays); no note holds the link: "… · no longer in PARAGON's notes" (no Open); narrow: "… · 5 of 13 done" with pill **Open**, then cut in the middle. Under the top bar, centred, above the chosen bar; at the bottom above the tools on timeline / Who does what. Not on a starter draft or an unsent board.
- Count: of the board's things found as tasks in the note, how many are ticked; cancelled, re-worded or removed ones left out.
- Marks: ticked → `.done`, `- [-]` → `.cancelled`, open → `.inParagon`; re-worded stays in PARAGON; a 0.4 board is matched by the lines it would send now (so never-ticked ideas get marks too).
- Open in PARAGON (the line, the Send page's Last sent, the list menu) opens the FOUND note's present title (front matter `title:`, else its file name), also after a rename or a move to Archive.
- Card / row: 11.2; iPhone row "Changed 2 days ago · 5 of 13 done · 5 shapes · 5 notes".
- When: a sent board opens · right after a Send · when PARAGON's notes are found after the start · coming to the front (≤ once per 15 s; looks at every sent board). Off the main thread. Where: the last found place, then `SentInfo.path`; it counts when it holds `seedboard://board/<id>` (or stands at the path when no other holds it). Gone: every note on the device is searched (several: most of the board's tasks, then newest); by itself at most every 10 minutes. Never in bulk: un-arrived notes passed over (only the ONE note at its known place is asked for), hidden folders and `Pictures` skipped, notes > 2 MB skipped.
- Task lines by PARAGON's rule (`- [ ]`, `* [x]`, `+ [X]`, `- [-]`, `- [>]`, indented too; not in ``` / ~~~); x/X done, - cancelled, blank / > open. Matching by `sentTasks` words with PARAGON's marks lifted both sides (`^t…`, `@done(…)`, `@repeat(…)`, `@since(…)`, `@deadline(…)`, the first `>yyyy-mm-dd[ time]`, `!`–`!!!`, `~45m`/`~1h`/`~1h30m`; spaces squeezed); note entries first, then Inbox entries (filed by hand into the note); twins in note order. Kept: `Application Support/Echo/read.json`, dropped for gone boards; nothing written with `-sampleData` / `-paragonFolder`. Never writes `^t` ids. Not read: the Inbox; a branch sent alone. Launch word `-echoEvery <s>` (DEBUG).

### 11.15 Add what is new (pick 17; `SendTasks.swift`, `ParagonGate`)

- Offered as the NEW FIRST ROW `send.kind.add`, chosen at open, when the board went to PARAGON as a note or project and holds things that became tasks since: **Add what is new to “Summer cabin”**. Under it: at its place — "3 new tasks go into the project. Nothing that is there is touched." ("1 new task goes into the project. …"; a note: "… into the note. …"); not at its place — "The project is not where it was: the 3 tasks go to the Inbox." ("… the task goes to the Inbox.").
- Seedboard looks first, reading only, off the main thread: the note at `SentInfo.path` there / not yet brought (a `.Name.md.icloud` counts as there) / gone; only paths inside PARAGON's folder.
- New = for a 0.5-sent board every task thing not in `sentTasks`; for a 0.4-sent board the note is READ and a thing whose words already stand there as a task is not new (remembered from then on); if that note cannot be read, no row (never the same tasks twice).
- Send writes ONLY waiting-list lines: aimed at the note (`target: {"note": {"path": "Projects/Summer cabin.md"}}`, PARAGON's `CaptureTarget`), PARAGON files them at the end of that note's "## Tasks" (`Vault.capture` → `Note.append(task:)`, PARAGON 340). Note gone → the Inbox, with the way back. New timeline bars keep their days. Seedboard never opens a PARAGON note for writing. This is the first time something from Seedboard lands inside an existing note; Martin said yes to it knowing this.
- Cannot follow (`send.cannotFollow`, under the box): "2 things were renamed and 1 bar moved since 18 Nov. PARAGON keeps the old ones." ("… the old one."); a bar counts as moved when only its days changed. Beside the row: **Send a fresh copy** (`send.freshCopy`) → As a project / As a note, whichever it was (a second note).
- With the add row: the Name field is hidden; the bottom line reads "Seedboard only hands PARAGON the new lines. PARAGON puts them at the end of the tasks itself; Seedboard never changes a note you already have." (else as before: "Seedboard only adds something new. It never changes a note you already have; sending again makes a second note."). The branch switch never offers it; switching it on moves to As a note.
- After Send: "1 new task is on the way into “Summer cabin”. They appear there when you switch to PARAGON (PARAGON 340 or newer)." / "3 new tasks are on the way to the PARAGON Inbox. They appear …"; nothing new: "Nothing on the board is new for PARAGON." The board is marked and stored BEFORE the hand-over; a failed hand-over takes the marks off. Limit: lands at the end of Tasks, not under its heading.

### 11.16 The Send page and the texts (picks 19, 20; TF 1; TA 2, 5, 6)

- Recording: every task line goes through `ParagonExport.taskLine`, which also notes the thing and its words; `board.noteSent(…)` with place `note` (note, project, branch), `inbox` (to-dos), or where Add what is new sent them.
- "(already in the Inbox)": in a note or project a thing already in the Inbox is `- Permit (already in the Inbox)`, never a second `- [ ]`; the count line says "1 already in the Inbox"; As a note says "The 2 to-do boxes become tasks in it." (only those the Inbox lacks).
- What PARAGON gets (`send.gets`): heading "What PARAGON gets" (caption, purple), a box 168 high that scrolls, read out of the very text Send writes (`ParagonExport.preview(ofNote:)` / `preview(ofTasks:)`): small picture 54 high (a branch: "The picture"), parts bold, `###` semibold purple, tasks with an empty purple box 10 × 10 and their day at the right ("12 Dec"), plain lines grey, 14 per level; links shown as their name; the day taken out of the words. Count `send.count`: "13 tasks · 3 headings · 1 already in the Inbox · last day 20 Feb" (zero parts out; "1 task", "1 heading"; "No tasks").
- Row words: to-dos "2 to-do boxes go to the Inbox as tasks. They appear there when you switch to PARAGON (PARAGON 340 or newer)."; all already sent "All 4 to-do boxes are already in PARAGON." / "The 1 to-do box is already in PARAGON."; none "No to-do boxes are waiting. Choose a box and press To do first."; timeline project "All 6 bars and 1 milestone become tasks with their days." / "The bar becomes a task with its days." / "The milestone becomes a task with its day." After Send (to-dos): "2 tasks are on the way to the PARAGON Inbox. They appear there when you switch to PARAGON (PARAGON 340 or newer)." (0.4: "… They show the next time PARAGON opens.")
- Inbox to-dos keep their days: "Permit (starts 1 Dec) >2026-12-05".
- **Say where they come from** (`send.sayOrigin`, on at first, kept per device): shown with the to-dos and with Add what is new going to the Inbox. On: each Inbox to-do ends, before the day, with "[Summer cabin](seedboard://board/<id>)"; for a board that is a project at its place "— [[Summer cabin]]". Square brackets in a name → round inside the link.
- **Also remind me when a bar starts** (`send.remindStarts`, timeline only, off each time): every bar longer than 3 days gets "- [ ] Start: Foundation >2026-12-08" right before its own task, under the same heading (note, project, to-dos, add); the box shows them.
- Frames as headings (TF 1): note "## Notes" — loose things first, then "### Pros" and its things, "### Cons" …, reading order; a thing belongs to the smallest named frame its middle lies in; lanes keep their rule; unnamed frames give no heading; a map's ideas belong to no frame. Project: ticked notes / texts in a named frame → tasks under "### <frame>", others under "### To do"; Notes grouped like the note. Copy as text: "Notes:" loose lines, then "Pros:" … A shared page does not repeat its frame's name.
- Launch words: `-sentBefore`, `-sentGone`, `-sendAs add`, `-starts`, `-noOrigin`, `-sendNow project+add+todos`.

### 11.17 Shortcuts, search, Handoff (picks 23, 28; AP 6; Core `OutsideAdd.swift`; app `IntentsAdd.swift`, `OutsideAdd.swift`, `HandoffDesk.swift`)

- Six actions (AddToBoardIntent, BoardAsPictureIntent, BoardAsTextIntent, NewBoardFromTextIntent, NewBoardIntent, OpenBoardIntent), two entities (BoardEntity with Name, Changed, Pinned, Sent to PARAGON; StarterEntity), six App Shortcut groups.
- **Add to board**: "Add [Text] to [Board]", Picture, switch "Open the board afterwards" (off; iOS 18.2 / macOS 15.2+). Description "Adds a thought to one of your boards without opening Seedboard. One line becomes a sticky note, a list becomes a mind map, a picture becomes a picture. With no board chosen it goes to the board you changed last." Text required ("What do you want to add?"). Phrases "Add to a Seedboard board", "Add a thought in Seedboard", "Add to <board> in Seedboard". One line → note; lines → `addWords`; on a board that is one mind map only, one line → a new main branch; a picture ≤ 320 long side; at `arrivalSpot` (40 to the right of everything, top in line; empty board: middle); words + picture: picture 16 under the words. No board → the newest board that can take it. Answer "Added to Summer cabin." (+ " The picture could not be read."), returns the board. Refusals: "Nothing was added. “Trip plan” is a timeline and places its things itself. Choose another board." · "… “Hand-over” is a “Who does what” board and places its things itself. Choose another board." · "… “…” was made by a newer Seedboard. Update the app to add to it." · "Nothing was added. There is no board yet that a thought can be added to. Make a board first." · "There was nothing to add." · "Nothing was added. That could not be read as a picture." · "Nothing was added. That board is not on this device any more." · "Nothing was added. “…” cannot be changed just now. Try again in a moment." The device copy is written before answering (iOS: iCloud gets up to 8 s). Arrivals chosen once at the next opening on this device (`seedboard.outsideArrivals`), view nudged after 0.5 s; on the open board chosen at once, one Undo.
- **Board as text** "Get [Board] as text" (= Copy the board as text; phrases "Get a Seedboard board as text", "Get <board> as text from Seedboard"). **Board as picture** "Get [Board] as a picture" (PNG "Summer cabin.png", PARAGON's picture, pictures read from files; empty: "“…” is empty, so there is no picture of it."; phrases "Get a Seedboard board as a picture", "Get <board> as a picture from Seedboard"). **Find Boards**: Name (is / contains / begins with), Changed (after / before), Pinned, Sent to PARAGON; all / any; sort Name or Changed; limit; capitals and accents ignored.
- New board: "Start with" = the whole shelf in his order with hints; empty = blank (a removed starter → blank); phrase "New <starter> in Seedboard" follows the shelf (≈ 2 s). Trap: a 0.4 shortcut with a starter may show it empty once.
- Search (AP 6): every word up to 20 000 letters (searched, not shown); the description still the first 300; the small picture (home look, day, 480 × 300 JPEG; none for an empty board); retold once after the update (`seedboard.searchTold.2`); pictures only for changed boards, 2 s after, one at a time, never while typing or dragging. On iOS 18 / macOS 15 entries are tied to `BoardEntity`.
- Handoff: `.userActivity` on the open board; same board, zoom and middle; view sent 0.6 s after moving stops. Not offered: sample boards, a starter draft, a waiting board; timeline / Who does what without view. Faster than iCloud: the offer carries `changed`; an older local copy shows read-only with "Waiting for the newest version from iCloud…" (`board.handoffWait`, boards-green capsule, tools hidden) until it arrives or 20 s; leaving ends the wait. Not here / removed: as a link. Launch words `-addThought "<words>"`, `-addTo <word>`, `-handoffWait`.

### 11.18 The Mac's own habits (pick 29; Core `MacHabits.swift`; app `MacHabits.swift`, `MacTitle.swift`)

- Help menu: "How Seedboard Works" ⌘? and "What's New" (the system's search field stays). Either opens the guide in its own window (`guide.window`; 460 × 640 at first, top right; min 360 × 320; title = the page's), two plates `guide.tab.how` "How it works" / `guide.tab.news` "What's new" (shown one full green, the other soft); Escape closes; no Window-menu line. (Fixes: 0.4's Help said help was not available.)
- Dock menu: New Board (`dock.new`; brings the window, shows the starters), a line, pinned boards (≤ 10, newest first), a line, the five newest unpinned (`dock.board.<title>`); a line opens its board like a link. Before loading: only New Board.
- Continuity Camera: File ▸ "Import from iPhone or iPad" ▸ per device Take Photo / Scan Documents / Add Sketch; the same line in the right-click menu of empty board after "Picture…" (`menu.camera`, pale with no device near). From the right-click: lands at the clicked spot if within 2 minutes and the same board is open; else near the middle. A scan: each page a picture (≤ 12, white ground, 2048). The window comes to the front, the board takes the keys. Not on read-only / timeline / Who does what. Not a picture: "That could not be read as a picture." (red).
- Services "New Seedboard Board from Selection" (`NSServices`): a new board like "New board from text" (list → map, else a note; named after the first line). No words: "There were no words to make a board from." Shows after the first start (else System Settings ▸ Keyboard ▸ Keyboard Shortcuts ▸ Services).
- The board's name (`title.name`): a click → "Name of the board" (not on read-only); a right-click → Rename… (`title.rename`, pale on read-only), Pin to the top / Unpin (`title.pin`), Make a copy (`title.copy`), Save as a starter… (`title.starter`, not on read-only); a starter draft: Rename… only. A double-click on the name no longer zooms the window. macOS 14: plain as in 0.4. iPad: the system's title menu with the same lines. iPhone unchanged.
- Space on ONE chosen picture (short press, no drag; `MenuRules.spaceShowsPicture`): Quick Look of the whole stored picture (also when cropped), named by caption (first line, ≤ 60) else "Picture 2" by reading order, unique ("Cabin", "Cabin 2"); arrow keys step through the board's pictures; Space / Escape close; temporary copies removed. Not on this Mac yet: "This picture is not on this Mac yet." (red). Other Space uses unchanged.
- The app now has an `NSApplicationDelegate` (`MacDelegate`). Launch words `-guide`, `-whatsNew`, `-lookPicture`, `-macCheck`, `-titleCheck` (DEBUG).

### 11.19 iPhone and iPad extras (pick 31 rest; Core `QuickStart.swift`, `SystemUndo.swift`; app `QuickActions.swift`, `PictureWays.swift`, `SystemUndoHost.swift`)

- **0.6:** at most four lines (12.2); New mind map is left out while two boards are pinned.
- App icon menu (set by the app after loading and on leaving): New board (plus), New mind map (his Mind map starter; removed → blank + "That starter is not here any more. This is a blank board."), New board from the clipboard ("New board from text" rule, words only; nothing → blank + "There were no words to paste. This is a blank board."; iOS may ask "Allow Paste?"), then the first two pinned boards with their count line. Marks: template pictures `QuickAdd`, `QuickMindMap`, `QuickPaste`, `QuickBoard` (copies of the icon library's drawings). Not with `-sampleData` for boards. An iPhone shows four lines: the second pinned board falls off (to decide).
- Links `seedboard://new/<starter id>` (built-ins `blank`, `mindMap`, `flow`, `prosCons`, `steps`, `timeline`, `sequence`, or his starter's id; case-insensitive; `seedboard://new` = blank) and `seedboard://new/clipboard`; on all three devices; always a NEW board.
- Picture tile: a press = Photos; hold 0.4 s (iPad also the 7-point corner `tool.pictureWays`) → plate `plate.picture` "Add a picture" with Cancel (`plate.cancel`), three tiles 54 high: `plate.picture.photos` Photos · `plate.picture.camera` Take a photo · `plate.picture.scan` Scan a page. iPhone: low sheet 150; iPad: pop-up 320 wide. Camera: full screen, "Use Photo" lays it chosen near the middle (≤ 2048, upright). Scanner: every page a picture, all chosen, ≤ 12 ("A scan puts 12 pages on a board at most. The first 12 of 15 are here.", 6 s); failure "The scan did not work. Nothing was put on the board." (red); no camera "This device has no camera." (red). Permission words "To put a photo straight onto a board." Not on boards that place themselves or read-only boards. Launch word `-plate picture`.
- Three-finger swipe left = undo, right = redo, three-finger tap = the device's bar, shake = "Undo" question. ONE undo path: the window's `UndoManager` holds exactly one undo and one redo that call the board's own; buttons, ⌘Z and the Edit menu go straight to the board. While typing: the field's own undo. While a third finger is down and 0.3 s after, two-finger slide and zoom rest. Mac untouched.

### 11.20 Data safety (DS 1–12) and files

- DS 1 starter draft: written to `Starters/<id>.working` 0.5 s after each change and on leaving; never on the shelf or in iCloud before Save; at start reopened as changed with "You were changing the starter “Weekly review”. Your changes are back." (6 s); removed meanwhile → kept as a NEW own starter ("Your own starter"), "The starter “Weekly review” was removed. What you were changing is kept as a new starter."; Save / Cancel / leaving delete it. DS 2: name, hint, place and removed from the copy changed last, the board from the copy whose content is newer (`boardStamp`); a replaced different board kept in SafetyCopies as `starter-<id>-<yyyyMMdd-HHmmss>-<6>.seedstarter` for 30 days (no page shows them); his own Save is not kept aside. DS 3: starter files follow the boards' iCloud rules (not-arrived never overwritten, parked versions cleared only after joining, "<id> 2.seedstarter" newer wins, saving reads iCloud first). DS 4: a board no longer on screen never saves (`SafetyRules.maySave`); late pictures → "The pictures were not laid: the board was closed." (red). DS 5: a picture not in iCloud's folder is never counted sent; retried every 3 s for a minute, then 30 s; iCloud's folder asked again (≤ 30 s apart). DS 6: export line "Exported 14 boards, 2 starters and 31 pictures." / "… 3 pictures are not on this device yet and are not in the file." / "1 picture is not on this device yet …" / "Exported 3 boards."; missing pictures asked for; own and changed built-in starters packed in "Starters/<name>.seedboard" with their pictures; pictures read in the background. DS 7: Undo / Redo keep `sent`, `sentTodos`, `sentTasks` (`Board.keepingSentMarks`). DS 8: memory copies ≤ 1024 long side (≈ 3 MB; ≈ 50 photos in 160 MB), counted by bytes; a picture in view drawn > 1.2 × its copy is read in full (64 MB budget, never pushing out one on screen, let go after 30 s or on memory warning); "Picture not here yet" only when the file is truly missing, else the mark alone; Crop reads off the main thread; PARAGON / Share PNG / Copy / small pictures use the 1024 copy (PDF, print and Board as picture read files). DS 9: two lines of work for starters, leaving waits ≤ 2 s for the device line only. DS 10: Import keeps a picture's bytes (after a check) and never overwrites a name on the device or in iCloud (file or placeholder). DS 11: a picture is deleted only when unnamed at two passes ≥ 7 days apart and older than 30 days (`picturesUnnamedSince`); only `.<name>.icloud` counts as "still in iCloud"; no pass while a board waits for iCloud (retry every 5 minutes for half an hour). DS 12 (lead): the stored-fields guard proves every field is written. A starter from a newer Seedboard → a blank board and "This starter was made by a newer Seedboard. Update the app to use it. A blank board was made instead." (red, 6 s).
- Marks: the fifteen 0.5 marks are real drawings from AMS-Icons 8e79eaf (`seedDoor`, `seedNextNote`, `seedNextShape`, `seedTurnInto`, `seedTidy`, `seedByColour`, `seedBreakNotes`, `seedDone`, `seedCatchUp`, `seedShare`, `seedPDF`, `seedWords`, `seedPrint`, `seedCamera`, `seedScan`).
- New Core files: `Door.swift`, `Echo.swift`, `FeelRules.swift`, `Glide.swift`, `Motion.swift`, `InkShape.swift`, `Looks.swift`, `MacHabits.swift`, `OutsideAdd.swift`, `QuickStart.swift`, `SystemUndo.swift`, `SharePages.swift`, `Thinking.swift`, `TimelineMake.swift`, `TimelinePlan.swift`, `StarterSafety.swift`, `PictureSafety.swift`, `SentSafety.swift`, `Sent.swift`, `SendTasks.swift`. New app files: `Doors.swift`, `DoorDraw.swift`, `DoorPage.swift`, `EchoKeeper.swift`, `ParagonLook.swift`, `SentLine.swift`, `ArrivalBox.swift`, `EmptyBoard.swift`, `FeelLooks.swift`, `FeelMoments.swift`, `Feel.swift`, `QuickActions.swift`, `PictureWays.swift`, `SystemUndoHost.swift`, `HomeStrip.swift`, `BranchLook.swift`, `TodoMark.swift`, `LooksLaunch.swift`, `MacHabits.swift`, `MacTitle.swift`, `BoardGlide.swift`, `BoardMotion.swift`, `PencilSwitches.swift`, `InkTidy.swift`, `ShapeScribble.swift`, `SharePlate.swift`, `BoardPaper.swift`, `IntentsAdd.swift`, `OutsideAdd.swift`, `HandoffDesk.swift`, `Thinking.swift`, `ThumbRow.swift`, `TimelineProgress.swift`, `TimelineKeep.swift`, `TimelineOfIt.swift`, `PressStyle.swift`; the share extension's sources and entitlements.
- To decide (open from the lanes): five or four icon-menu lines (iPhone shows four; **0.6: decided, four**, 12.2); a done bar sent as `- [ ]` or `- [x]` (**0.6: decided, `- [x]`**, 12.1); "Add to board" with no board — newest that can take it (built) or refuse on a timeline; one line on a pure mind map → a branch (built) or a note; the arrow-key nudge glides (built) or stays instant; a removed starter's draft kept as a new starter (built); starters exported as boards (built); the 1024 copy for PARAGON's picture.

## 12. New in 0.6

Version 0.6 (build 6, 2026-11-18). `project.yml`: `MARKETING_VERSION` 0.6, `CURRENT_PROJECT_VERSION` 6. The board format stays 5. Changed files: Core `SendTasks.swift` (`taskLine`), `Paragon.swift` (`inboxTasks`), `SendNew.swift` (`newTasks`), `SendPreview.swift`, `QuickStart.swift` (`QuickActions.list`); app `SendParts.swift`; texts `Guide.swift`. New test file: `Core/Tests/SeedboardCoreTests/Done06Tests.swift` (the done rules); the icon-menu tests in `Iphone05Tests.swift` follow the four-line rule.

### 12.1 A done bar or milestone goes to PARAGON done

- Before (0.5): a timeline bar or milestone with `doneOn` set (the Done button, 3.x timeline, 11.10) was sent as an open task, `- [ ] Permit (starts 1 Dec) >2026-12-05`. Now: `ParagonExport.taskLine` writes a thing with `doneOn` as `- [x] <words> @done(<day>)`, the day being the day it was marked done in ISO form. This is the way PARAGON writes a done task itself, for example `- [x] Permit (starts 1 Dec) >2026-12-05 @done(2026-12-05)`. It holds in a note and in a project, and for every kind of timeline row that is a task. The thing is still noted down as made (`made.tasks`), so the board remembers it as sent.
- Start: reminders (the switch "Also remind me when a bar starts"): a done bar gets NO "Start: …" line.
- Only the to-do boxes (`ParagonExport.inboxTasks`): a thing with `isDone` is never sent to the Inbox, whether or not it is marked To do. The count, the message and "No to-do boxes are waiting." follow what is left.
- Add what is new (`newTasks`): a task whose board item is done (`isDone`) is not offered as new, even though it is not in `sentTasks` or `sentTodos`. When the Done mark is taken off again, it is offered again.
- What PARAGON gets (`PreviewRow.done`, `SendPreview.swift`, `SendParts.swift`): the preview reads the very text Send writes, so a `- [x]` line is a task row with `done` set; the `@done(…)` words are taken out of the shown words (as `@since` and the line mark are). A done row shows a filled purple box (10 × 10, corner 2.5) with a drawn white tick, and its words are struck through in secondary grey. The count line adds the done tasks after the task count: "5 tasks (1 done)"; with none done the line is as before.
