- Not changed: a bar marked done in PARAGON (read back, `todoStates`) is as before (3.x, 11.14); the day on a done bar in the note is the day it was marked here.

### 12.2 The app icon's menu has at most four lines

- `QuickActions.most` = 4 (iPhone and iPad show four of the app's own lines; the fifth fell off, so the second pinned board was lost). `QuickActions.list(pinned:)` builds: New board; New mind map only when the pinned boards shown plus three are at most four, that is while fewer than two boards are pinned; New board from the clipboard; then the first two pinned boards (`pinnedShown` = 2) with their count lines.
- So: no or one pinned board = 3 or 4 lines (all of the first three, then the pinned board); two pinned boards = New board, New board from the clipboard, board 1, board 2.
- The links of the lines are as in 11.19.

### 12.3 The link back is pressed in PARAGON

- Text only (commit `c09e983`, now released): the line "Open the board in Seedboard" in a PARAGON note opens the board when pressed: a tap on the iPhone and iPad, ⌘-click on the Mac, in PARAGON build 339 or later, also while the note is edited (before: only in the reading view). The link is still `seedboard://board/<board UUID>` (5.3).
- The Send page's sentence that to-dos appear in PARAGON's Inbox when you switch to PARAGON (PARAGON 340 or newer), `ParagonGate.whenTheyShow`, is unchanged since 0.5 (11.16).

### 12.4 A course

- Seedboard has its first course: a short interactive tour of the whole app, with a practice screen, a web page in `Docs/intro/index.html`. It is not part of the app and is not built into it. What's new says only: "A course: a short interactive tour of the whole app, with a practice screen."

### 12.5 Texts and tests

- `Guide.swift`: a new 0.6 entry in What's new (eight lines, above 0.5); How it works changed in Timeline board (Done), Send to PARAGON (done bars; Start: and Add what is new), What PARAGON gets (the ticked box, the count) and the three lines about the icon menu.
- Tests: `Done06Tests` (2 data tests: a done bar arrives ticked with its day and an open one stays open; a done bar never goes to the Inbox); `Iphone05Tests` follows the four-line rule (two pinned boards: New board, New board from the clipboard, board 1, board 2; one pinned board: all four lines). What was not seen on a real device: the long press on the real icon on an iPhone with two pinned boards; a done task ticked in a real PARAGON note.

## 13. New in 0.7

Version 0.7 (build 7, 2026-11-18). `project.yml`: `MARKETING_VERSION` 0.7, `CURRENT_PROJECT_VERSION` 7. The board format stays 5 (`doneDay` has been stored since 0.5, 11.10). Texts: `Guide.swift`; course: `Docs/intro/index.html`.

### 13.1 The Done button shows the day

- Before (0.6 and older): `doneDay` (ISO day, set by Done) was kept and sent to PARAGON as `@done(day)` (12.1) but never shown. Now: it is shown in two places.
- The selection bar (1221, `sel.done`): with ONE bar or milestone chosen that is done on the board (has `doneDay`), the button reads "Done 18 Nov" (day and short month, as the other dates in the app). When the year of `doneDay` is not the current year, the year is added: "Done 3 Dec 2025". The button is in full colour as before; pressing it still takes the mark off.
- With SEVERAL chosen (any mix), the button reads "Done", as before, whether all, some or none are done, because their days may differ. The rule for several (all on, or all off when all were done) is unchanged.
- A thing that is not done: the button reads "Done" (grey as before, 3.x).
- A bar drawn done only because its task is ticked off in PARAGON (read back, `todoStates`) has NO `doneDay`: the button reads "Done", because no day is kept for it. Taking the mark off still says "It is ticked off in PARAGON, so it stays done."

### 13.2 The Dates page says when it was done

- The Dates page (Starts, Ends, Cancel and Save) of a done bar or milestone has one more line under the dates: "Done on 18 Nov" (the year added when it is not this year, as in 13.1). It is text only; it changes nothing and Cancel and Save work as before.
- A bar or milestone that is not done, and one done only in PARAGON, has no such line.
- Nothing is written to the board or to PARAGON by this version; `doneDay` and `@done(day)` are as in 12.1.

### 13.3 The course shows the iPad and the Mac

- `Docs/intro/index.html`: a device switch iPhone · iPad · Mac at the top changes the pictures and the wording to that device. A new chapter "iPad and Mac" shows the list of boards beside the board, the Mac menu bar, the Mac keys, Escape = Cancel, and the Pencil and the trackpad. The course is still not built into the app.

### 13.4 Texts and tests

- `Guide.swift`: a new 0.7 entry in What's new (six lines, above 0.6); How it works changed in Timeline board (a new line after Done: the day it was done).
- `Docs/WHAT-TO-TEST-0.7.txt` is new.

## 14. New in 0.8

Version 0.8 (build 8, 2026-11-18). `project.yml`: `MARKETING_VERSION` 0.8, `CURRENT_PROJECT_VERSION` 8. The board format stays 5 (`doneDay` is stored since 0.5). Texts: `Guide.swift`; course: `Docs/intro/index.html`.

### 14.1 The day it was done, drawn on the board

- Before (0.7): the day was shown only when a done bar or milestone was chosen (Done button, 13.1; Dates page, 13.2). Now: it is also drawn on the board, as a small label a little past the end of the bar or milestone.
- Text: Core `Item.doneLabel(today:)` in `TimelinePlan.swift` returns `doneOn?.words(seenFrom: today)`: day and short month, for example "18 Nov"; the year is added when it is not the current year, for example "3 Dec 2025". It is the same wording as the Done button and the Dates page. The result is `nil` for a thing that is not done and for one drawn done only because its task is ticked off in PARAGON (`todoStates`; no `doneDay` is kept), and then nothing is drawn.
- Drawing: `TimelinePainter.drawState` (`TimelineProgress.swift`), in the branch that draws the tick of a done item. Size 10 points, medium weight, in the app's faint grey (`paint.faint`), left-aligned, centred on the row's middle line. Place: always 31 points after the end (`dotGap` 20 + `dotRadius` 5 + 6), or 9 points after the tick's centre when that is further (a milestone or a narrow bar, whose tick is behind the end). So it is clear of a "comes after" arrow leaving the end (out 8) and of the grip and the dot a chosen bar wears there, and it never jumps when the bar is chosen. The red Today line may cross it.
- Taking the Done mark off removes `doneDay` and so the label. Nothing else about the tick, the bar's colour or the late mark changes.
- The label is part of the board drawing, so the board's picture shows it too: the picture that goes to PARAGON (12.1) and Share as Picture and PDF.
- Nothing about the data, the Done button, the Dates page or what goes to PARAGON changes.

### 14.2 The course

- `Docs/intro/index.html`: on the pretend iPhone, the bar for a chosen timeline row now stands at the bottom, just above the tools, as in the real app (11.x, 2.9). Before: it stood at the top.

### 14.3 Texts and tests

- `Guide.swift`: a new 0.8 entry in What's new (four lines, above 0.7); How it works, Timeline board: a new line after "The day it was done" (the day on the board).
- Data test: `DoneDay07Tests` (`Core/Tests/SeedboardCoreTests/`) checks `doneLabel(today:)`: "18 Nov" for this year, "3 Dec 2025" for another year, `nil` for a thing not done.
- `Docs/WHAT-TO-TEST-0.8.txt` is new.

## 15. New in 0.9

Version 0.9 (build 9, 2026-11-18). `project.yml`: `MARKETING_VERSION` 0.9, `CURRENT_PROJECT_VERSION` 9. The board format is now **6**. Texts: `Guide.swift`; test text: `Docs/WHAT-TO-TEST-0.9.txt`.

### 15.1 Board format 6

- `Board.currentFormat = 6`. What format 6 adds (all optional; absent = old behaviour): item `votes: Int?`, `locked: Bool?`, `linkTitle: String?`, `pictureWords: String?` (nil = not read, "" = no words); board `legend: [String: String]?` (colour name → words), `backdrop: String?` ("plain" or "squares"; nil = dots), `numberSteps: Bool?`, `putAway: Date?`; stroke `pen: String?` ("highlighter"; nil = ordinary pen). Marks on a picture are the picture item's own `strokes`, measured from its corner. Reused: `Item.folded` (also a folded timeline phase), `Link.text`, `ItemKind.phase`.
- Rule as for earlier formats: a board changed by 0.9 carries format 6 and opens read-only in a version older than 0.9 (looked at, never changed or written over); every device must be updated to 0.9. Boards not changed by 0.9 keep their number. `StoredFieldsTests` holds the golden list of field names for format 6.
- Things kept on the device, not in the board: the copy of due days read from PARAGON (Echo `read.json`), the calendar memory (`calendar.added.v1`), the small-map switch, the highlighter colour (`ink.highlightColour`) and rubber size (`ink.rubberSize`).

### 15.2 Timeline: the done day on the board (A, B) — timeline-a

- A: the label of 14.1 is drawn on a plate: the board's background (`ground ?? paint.canvas`) at 92 %, corner radius 8, 4 points of padding at the sides, drawn AFTER the red Today line, so the line never runs through the words.
- B: after the day a dot and the comparison of `doneDay` with the bar's planned last day: "18 Nov · 8 days late", "18 Nov · 3 days early"; on time: the day only; singular "1 day late" / "1 day early". Colours: day and dot `paint.faint` grey; late `paint.line("red")` at 85 % (day) / 95 % (night); early `paint.chosen` (green 2E8B57 day, 5FBF8A night) at 90 % / 95 %. Not done, or ticked off only in PARAGON: no label.
- Dates page line: "Done on 18 Nov · 8 days late". Core: `Item.doneTiming`, `Item.doneHint`, `DoneTiming`, `DoneHint`.
- Tests: `Timing09Tests` (3); `DoneDay07Tests` and `TimelineTests` wording updated.

### 15.3 Timeline: Today, shading, months (picks 1, 2, 10) — timeline-a

- Today (1): the button "Today" (identifier now `today.button`, was `timeline.today`) in the zoom cluster, key T (timelines only), Mac View, "Go to Today" (timelines only; the key is handled by the timeline view). Today lands a third of the way in from the names, not in the middle.
- Shading (2): weekends in Days AND Weeks (before: Days only); edge colour 20 % / 22 % (day / night) in Days, 15 % / 18 % in Weeks; Swedish public holidays 38 % / 42 %. Months: none. `SwedishHolidays` in Core (16 days a year; May Day is "1 May"). Holiday names are not drawn (a 28-point day column is too narrow).
- Months (10): the Months scale existed; month marks now read "Jan 2027" (before "Jan"). A timeline never moved or zoomed opens in Months when its plan lasts more than 183 days (`TimeScale.opening(planDays:fitting:chosen:)`, `board.timelinePlanDays`); a remembered view wins; "Show the whole timeline" fits as before. New launch word `-goDay`.
- Tests: `Timing09Tests`.

### 15.4 Timeline: phases and calendar (picks 7, 12) — timeline-b

- Fold phases (7): a phase with rows has a chevron before its name (down open, right folded; 14 points of room); a press on it, or Fold / Unfold in the chosen phase's bar, sets `folded` on the phase. A folded phase hides its rows, rows below move up (`shownTimelineRows`, `shownPhaseGroups`), and its row shows one band from the group's first to last day (corner 7, height 18) with `foldWords`: "4 bars", "1 bar, 2 milestones". "Comes after" arrows to a hidden bar end at the band; arrows between two bars hidden in the same phase are not drawn. What a fold hides is dropped from the choice. A new row under a folded phase goes after the hidden ones. More (timelines only): "Fold all phases" / "Unfold all" (ids `more.foldAll`, `more.unfoldAll`); Mac File "Fold All Phases" / "Unfold All Phases"; Mac right-click on a timeline: Fold / Unfold (phase under the pointer), Fold all phases, Unfold all, Add to Calendar…. One Undo step each; read-only boards cannot fold. Sending to PARAGON is unchanged (every bar goes); the board's picture shows every row open.
- Calendar (12): More "Add to Calendar…" (`more.calendar`; Mac File and right-click), page `calendar.page`: sections "Milestones" then "Bars", each row a tick circle, the name, the day or "1 Dec – 12 Dec 2026 · 12 days", and a status "Added 18 Nov" / "Day changed — add again" / "Days changed — add again". New and changed milestones are ticked at first, bars never. Buttons Cancel / Add (full colour; Escape = Cancel, Return = Add). Empty: "This timeline has no milestones or bars with days yet." Access refused: "Seedboard cannot add to your calendar yet. Allow it in Settings ▸ Privacy ▸ Calendars (on the Mac: System Settings ▸ Privacy & Security ▸ Calendars), then try again." After: "1 event is in your calendar." / "N events are in your calendar."; failure: "The calendar did not take them. Nothing was added." All-day events in the default calendar, notes "From the Seedboard board “title”"; a bar is one multi-day all-day event. Write-only access (EventKit): Seedboard never reads the calendar; what was added is remembered on this device only (`CalendarMemory`: row id → first, last, added). Adding again makes a second event. Permission text: "To put a timeline's milestones into your calendar." (`NSCalendarsWriteOnlyAccessUsageDescription`; Mac entitlement `com.apple.security.personal-information.calendars`). The page can open on a read-only timeline.
- Launch words: `-foldPhases`, `-calendarPage`. Tests: `Phases09Tests` (3). Core: `CalendarPlan.swift`, `Timeline.swift`.

### 15.5 Board and ideas — marks (picks 14, 15, 20, 21, 22, 23)

All off on timeline and Who does what boards; all go through Undo; none changes a read-only board.
- Vote dots (14): bar "Vote" (`sel.vote.add`), menu `menu.vote.add`, Mac Edit "Vote Dot" ⌥⌘V; the press-and-hold / right-click menu also "Remove a dot", "Clear dots" (`menu.vote.remove`, `menu.vote.clear`); More: "Sort by dots" (`more.sortDots`: up to 5 side by side, most dots first, 16 points apart, from the corner of the first in board order; pinned stay), "Clear all dots" (`more.clearDots`). Orange dots with a ring in the board colour on the top right edge; more than 5 = 5 dots and the number. Limit 99. `votes` stored.
- Colour legend (15): More "Colour legend…" (`more.legend`), "Hide legend" (`more.hideLegend`, only when a legend exists). Page "Colour legend": a swatch and the field "What does yellow mean?" (`legend.field.<colour>`), Hide legend / Cancel / Save (`legend.hide`, `legend.cancel`, `legend.save`; Escape = Cancel). Words at most 40 letters. Card `legend.card` bottom left above the tools: dot and words, only coloured notes with words; a press opens the page. A note with no colour counts as yellow. Stored in `legend`.
- Tick list (20): note lines beginning "[ ] " / "[x] " draw as a hand-drawn box (ticked: box with tick, words a little greyer) and the words; a press on the box ticks or unticks (Undo works). Bar / menu "Tick list" (`sel.tick.list`, notes only; Mac Edit "Tick List") turns every non-empty line into a tick line, or back when all already are. The note's height follows its lines. To PARAGON a note with tick lines goes as "- [ ] words" / "- [x] words", one per line (`Paragon.swift noteBullet`; Outline copy-as-text); other notes unchanged.
- Link title (21): the first http(s) address in the words (Core `WebAddress`). The title is fetched once per address with LinkPresentation after typing ends (not while typing, not on read-only boards), stored in `linkTitle`, no Undo step. A small line UNDER the thing: a tiny drawn arrow and the title (the tidied address while none), in blue; a press opens the browser; bar / menu "Open link" (`sel.link.open`). Mac sandbox: `com.apple.security.network.client` added to `project.yml` and `AMSSeedboard-macOS.entitlements`.
- Pin in place (22): bar / menu "Pin in place" / "Unpin" (`sel.pin.toggle`), Mac Edit "Pin in Place". Core move, resize, tidy, `moveSnapped` and size skip `locked` things, so dragging, size knobs (hidden), arrow-key nudge, Line up, Even gaps, Same size, Tidy into a grid and Sort by dots leave it; a moved group moves the others only. A tiny drawn pin at its top left corner while chosen.
- Background (23): More ▸ "Background" ▸ Plain / Dots / Squares (`more.background`). nil = Dots, so old boards look the same; "plain" and "squares" are stored. Squares: lines every 48 points, never closer than 24 on screen, fading below 40 % zoom like the dots. Only on the live canvas, never in the PARAGON picture.
- Tests: `Marks09Tests` (8). Picture: `-sampleData -board Marks`.

### 15.6 Board and ideas — views (picks 16, 17, 18, 19, 24)

- Focus (16): one frame chosen: bar "Focus" (`sel.focus`), menu `menu.focus`, Mac Board "Focus on the Frame" ⌥⌘F. The view glides to the frame, everything outside is dimmed to about 25 % (board painter); things inside stay editable. Pill "Focus · name  Done" (`focus.done`) at the top; Done or Escape leaves and the view comes back. Not on timeline / Who does what.
- Show (17): More "Show frames" (`show.start`; grey with no frame), Mac "Show Frames" ⌥⌘P. Order: top rows first, then left to right; zooms to each frame with air; tools and the selection bar hidden (and the top bar and back button, so a swipe cannot leave the board). Bottom bar "‹  2 of 5  ›  Done" (`show.bar`, `show.back`, `show.next`, `show.done`); arrow keys, space, Escape; swipe left / right on iPhone and iPad. Ending returns to the view as it was.
- Tidy into a grid (19): "Tidy" (0.5, at least 3 things, at most 5 across) is renamed "Tidy into a grid" and is now `Board.tidyIntoGrid`: columns = ceil(√n), gaps 16 points, reading order from the group's top left, pinned things stay, arrows follow, one Undo step. More "Tidy into a grid" (`more.tidyGrid`) works on the chosen things or, with nothing chosen, on all loose sticky notes; messages "Lined up in a grid." / "Loose notes lined up in a grid." Mac "Tidy into a Grid" ⌥⌘G.
- Small map (24): `SmallMap` (`board.smallMap`), 120 × 80 points (90 × 60 on iPhone), bottom right above the tools, things as tiny coloured boxes, the visible part outlined; press or drag jumps there. Shown only when the things spread over more than twice the screen; hidden while drawing, during a show, on timeline / Who does what. More switch "Small map" (`more.smallMap`), on by default, kept on the device.
- Starters (18): "Pros and cons" already existed. New built-ins at the end of the seven: "Decision grid" ("Options down, what matters across": a frame "Which one?" with Option A / B / C rows, Price / Effort / Fun across, a note in each cell), "Weekly review" ("Done, Stuck, Next week and Ideas", four frames), "SWOT" ("Strengths, weaknesses, chances, threats", four frames green / red / blue / orange). Ten built-in starters in all; Shortcuts and Siri offer them. The sample own starter "Weekly review" was renamed "Friday look-back".
- Tests: `Views09Tests` (6). Launch words: `-views focus|show|map`, `-starterChange "Decision grid"`, `-starterPage`.

### 15.7 Mind maps and flowcharts (picks 25–30) — maps

- Balance (25): a chosen mind-map centre with two or more branches: "Balance" (bar; behind … on narrow bars; right-click / press-and-hold; Mac Board "Balance the Map"). Turns on both sides, spreads the main branches by size (heaviest first onto the lighter side, ties to the right), order kept per side, one Undo step.
- Fold (26): Fold / Unfold already existed. Before: a folded idea showed a dot with the number of direct children, a press did nothing, no "Unfold all", no Mac line. Now: a pill "+5" counting ALL hidden ideas at the outer edge; one press opens it. Menu "Fold branch" / "Unfold branch", "Unfold all"; Mac Board "Fold Branch", "Unfold Branch", "Unfold All".
- Yes / No (27): already existed (first arrow "Yes", second "No", changeable); a test was added.
- Words on arrows (28): before, "Words" (bar / menu) and two presses typed words, drawn near the line's start with no background. Now: drawn at the middle of the line on a small patch in the board colour; the typing field sits there too.
- Round shapes (29): already existed since 0.2 (12 points clearance, 6 nearest boxes, plain path kept when nothing is free); tests added.
- Number the steps (30): More "Number the steps" / "Stop numbering the steps" (stored `numberSteps`); Mac Board "Number the Steps". Starts at shapes with arrows leaving but none arriving (top, then left), follows arrows breadth-first; only flowchart shapes are numbered (a round blue badge, top left); unreached things get none; a closed ring starts at its top-left shape. Not on timeline / Who does what.

### 15.8 Who does what (pick 33) — maps

- Names already had colours; a name with no colour now takes one by its place from the branch colours (before: always blue); messages follow their sender's colour. New: a small "sends 4" under each name (answers count), hidden when 0.
- Tests: `Maps09Tests` (7). Launch words: `-board Balanced -maps09 -sampleData`, `-board Order -maps09 -sampleData`, `-board Loan -sampleData`. Balance had no mark of its own in 0.9 (the even-gaps mark was used); 0.10 gives it one, see part 16.

### 15.9 Pencil and pictures (picks 35–39) — pencil

- Highlighter (35): `Stroke.pen = "highlighter"` (`Ink.highlighter`); width `Ink.highlighterWidth` (three times the thick pen), 35 % opacity (`Ink.highlighterOpacity`), flat ends, four colours (yellow, green, pink, blue; any other becomes yellow), drawn UNDER the ordinary ink of the same drawing (`Item.paintedStrokes`). Key H while drawing, button "Highlighter" (`draw.highlighter`), colours `draw.highlight.<name>`; the colour is kept on the device. Pen, highlighter, rubber and lasso are one at a time.
- Rubber sizes (35): `RubberSize` small / medium / large, reach `Ink.rubber` / 2, × 1, × 2 in screen points (medium is as before); buttons `draw.rubber.small|medium|large` (Small, Medium, Large); whole strokes only; the size is kept on the device.
- Make typed (36): a chosen sketch: "Make typed" (`ink.totext`; Mac "Make Sketch Typed"). The sketch is drawn into an image (long side about 1600 pixels, 1 to 4 pixels a point) and read on the device (Vision); lines joined with line breaks. The sketch becomes Text at the same place and under the same name, so arrows stay. Nothing read: "No words found in this sketch" (`InkText.noWords`). Refused on a board that may not be changed or places itself.
- Draw on it (37): a chosen picture: "Draw on it" (`picture.draw`; Mac "Draw on Picture"). The Draw tool opens with that picture as its target, with the line "Drawing on the picture. Press Done when you are finished." (`draw.onPicture`). Strokes are written into the picture's own `strokes`, measured from its corner (they may lie outside; the picture clips them); they scale with the picture (`kx`, `ky`) and keep their place on a crop.
- Lasso (39): key L while drawing, button "Lasso" (`draw.lasso`). A loop (even-odd rule, closing itself) chooses strokes of sketches with more than 50 % of their length inside (`Ink.lassoShare`, measured at about 3-point steps); a loop under 12 points or with fewer than 3 points is a tap. A sketch of which only some strokes were chosen is split: the chosen strokes become a new sketch so they can move alone.
- Tests: `Pencil09Tests` (9). Core: `InkPencil09.swift`; App: `InkWords.swift`, `InkDesk`, `InkViews`, `InkDraw`.

### 15.10 PARAGON (picks 40–42) — paragon

- Only this frame (40): one frame chosen: the Send page has the switch "Only this frame (name)" (a frame with no words: "Only this frame"), on at first (`send.frameOnly`), with the note "Just what lies inside this frame is sent, with a picture of the frame alone. The board is not marked as sent." The note's name is the frame's words; the picture is the frame and its things. Opens from the Send button, the frame's bar button "Send this frame to PARAGON…" (`sel.sendFrame`), right-click (`menu.sendFrame`), Mac File "Send This Frame to PARAGON…". Before: only "Only the branch" for one idea.
- Due day (41): PARAGON's ">2026-12-05" (also with a time) is read back from the note's task line and drawn small beside the to-do ring: "5 Dec"; "Today" in purple; past due and not done: faint red; done: grey. Read each time, kept on the device (Echo `read.json`), never in the board. Only tasks that live in a note or project (what the read-back reads); not on timeline / Who does what boards.
- Today's three (42): 1 to 3 chosen things: bar "Today's three" (`sel.today.three`, behind … when narrow), right-click (`menu.today.three`), Mac File "Today's Three to PARAGON…". Page "Today's three": "These go to the PARAGON Inbox as tasks due today:", each line with "Today", switch "Say where they come from" (`today.sayOrigin`), Cancel (`today.cancel`) / Send (`today.send`, full purple). More than three chosen: not offered. Sent through the waiting list as "words >YYYY-MM-DD"; remembered (`noteSent`, inbox) so a thing shows "in PARAGON" and is never sent twice.
- Tests: `Paragon09Tests` (4). Launch words: `-frameOff`, `-marks -dueDays`, `-marks -todayThree`.

### 15.11 Finding and tidying (picks 46–50) — finding

- Put away (46): press-and-hold menu "Put away" on a board in the list (all devices) and on home cards; Mac File "Put Board Away". A pinned board is unpinned first. Line: "Board put away. Find it under Put away." A quiet row "Put away (3)" at the bottom of the list and the home page opens the page "Put away" (newest first; each row: name, "Put away 3 days ago · <line>", button "Bring back"; the name opens the board; Done / Escape). Empty: "Nothing is put away." Search still finds put-away boards in a section "Put away", with " · put away". Never deleted; stored in `putAway`, so it reaches other devices. No swipe action (the list is not a system list).
- Line under each board (47): list and home cards. Ordinary board: "34 ideas · 12 done" (ideas = things with words; frames, pictures, sketches, doors not counted; done = to-dos PARAGON shows done, read back on this device); an empty board "Empty"; no worded things: the old count line. Timeline: "6 bars · 1 milestone" while nothing is done or late; otherwise bars and milestones are counted together: "7 things · 4 done · 1 late" (late = past its last day and not done; shorter so it fits an iPhone row). Who does what keeps its own line. Parts at zero are left out; singular and plural.
- Words inside pictures (48): each picture is read once (Vision, on the device, off the main thread, one at a time, 1.5 s after a board opens; waits while typing or dragging), stored in `pictureWords` ("" when none). Only when a board is OPENED (it then moves to the top of Recent once, only if it had unread pictures). A picture not yet on the device is tried at the next open. Search matches picture words; a board found only so shows " · in a picture".
- Spotlight (49): boards were already in Spotlight and a result opened the board. Now it also gives the kind ("Board", "Timeline", "Who does what"), a line of words and ALL words, including picture words; every board is told again once; put-away boards stay findable.
- Undo list (50): page "Undo list": steps newest first, for example "Moved 3 things · 21:40", with "Press a step to go back to just before it."; a press goes back to just before that step; all steps stay redoable. Step names: Added a sticky note / Added 2 things / Removed 2 things / Changed words / Coloured 1 thing / Moved 3 things / Resized 1 thing / Added an arrow / Removed an arrow / Changed a sketch / Changed to-do marks / Gave a vote / Pinned in place / Renamed the board / Changed 1 thing / Changed the board. Opened by ⌥⌘Z, Mac Edit "Undo List…", or a right-click / press-and-hold on the Undo button (toolbar and the iPhone's bottom pair). The list is cleared when another device's newer version replaces the board.
- Tests: `Finding09Tests` (6). Sample boards "Last year's trip", "Old bench plan" (put away); `-search trip`.

### 15.12 Already there

- Pick 38 (Scan) already existed; no change. Picks 3, 4, 5, 6, 8, 9, 11, 13, 31, 32, 34, 43, 44, 45 were turned down.

- `Guide.swift`: a new 0.9 entry in What's new (above 0.8); How it works changed in: Starters, The board, Mind maps, Shapes and arrows, Timeline board, Drawing by hand, Pictures, Who does what, Send to PARAGON, Your boards, Choosing … undo, Shortcuts … Spotlight, Menus and keys; new part "Marks on ideas, legend, focus and show". Every text that said seven starters now says ten.
- Data tests added in 0.9: `Timing09Tests` 3, `Marks09Tests` 8, `Views09Tests` 6, `Maps09Tests` 7, `Pencil09Tests` 9, `Paragon09Tests` 4, `Finding09Tests` 6, `Phases09Tests` 3.

## 16. New in 0.10

**Version 0.10 (build 10, 2026-11-19)** changes nothing but marks. Eleven buttons that in 0.9 borrowed another button's mark or showed only a word now have a hand-drawn mark of their own, all in the AMS-Icons package (commit `a6bf561`): 24-point grid, one 1.9 stroke, round ends, no fill. The board format stays **6**. Where part 15 says a button has the even-gaps mark, only a word, or no mark, part 16 is right.

| Button | Where | Mark (AMS-Icons) | Before (0.9) | Now |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Balance | mind map bar, More | `seedBalance` | the Even gaps mark | a middle idea with two branches each side |
| Highlighter | Draw plate | `seedHighlighter` | the word only | a broad-tipped pen over a band |
| Lasso | Draw plate | `seedLasso` | the word only | a rope loop with a loose end |
| Make typed | handwriting bar | `seedMakeTyped` | the Words mark | a squiggle turning into a typed T |
| Draw on it | picture bar | `seedDrawOnIt` | the plain Draw pencil | a picture with a pencil at its corner |
| Vote | bar | `seedVote` | a plain dot | a note with a vote dot on its corner |
| Tick list | note bar | `seedTickList` | the list mark | two ticked lines |
| Colour legend | More "Colour legend…", heading of the Colour legend page | `seedLegend` | no mark of its own | a small card with two colour dots |
| Focus | bar for a chosen frame | `seedFocus` | the Fit window mark | frame corners round a ring with a dot |
| Show frames | More, left end of the show bar | `seedShowFrames` | no mark | frame corners round a play triangle |
| Small map | More switch | `seedSmallMap` | no mark | a board with a small map in its lower right corner |

- Identifiers, keys, menus and rules of these buttons are unchanged.
- Version 0.10 (build 10).
- `Guide.swift`: a new 0.10 entry in What's new (above 0.9); How it works changed in: The board, Mind maps, Drawing by hand, Pictures, Marks on ideas, legend, focus and show.
- `Docs/WHAT-TO-TEST-0.10.txt` is new.

## 17. New in 0.11

**Version 0.11 (build 11, 2026-11-19).** Board format **7**: the one new stored field is `Board.due` ("yyyy-MM-dd"; a stored day outside 1900–2200 is ignored). An older version opens a 0.11-changed board read-only. Notes of each lane are in `Docs/notes-0.11/built-*.md`. Not built: 13 (timeline bars and Who does what messages for VoiceOver). 14: all 29 fixed letter sizes were checked (drawn board text, an invisible 1-point test label, or a typing field that must match drawn text; none is a button, chip or bar label; those use Apple text styles in 96 places), so nothing changed.

### 17.1 Pencil (31, 36, 37, 38)
- Core `Pencil11.swift`: `Ruler` (centre, angle in radians, length; `start`, `end`, `snapped(_:reach:)`, `Ruler.screenReach` 16, `defaultLength` 360), `Board.addReadNote(besideDrawing:words:)` (yellow note at x = drawing.x + w + 16, y = drawing.y; nil for no words, not a drawing, board cannot change, or a map-placed thing).
- `snapped`: nil unless 2 or more finite points, every point within reach of the ruler's line, and the snapped length at least 4; else [start foot, end foot]. Applied in `InkDesk.inkEnded` before thinning (pressure dropped for a snapped line).
- App: `Ruler11.swift` (`RulerLayer`, `BoardSession.toggleRuler`), `InkDesk.ruler`, `InkDesk.fade`, `Fade.opacity` 0.3 (InkDraw.swift), `BoardPainter.inkOpacity`, `BoardSession.readThis` (InkWords.swift). The ruler is cleared by `InkDesk.leave()`; fade is not cleared by leaving the tool. Neither is saved.
- Words: plate buttons Ruler and Fade; line Pencil lines are faded with Show; bar action Read this (mark of Make typed; no new mark); No words found in this sketch; rubber ring word Small, Medium or Large (ring reach 20 / 40 / 80 points).
- Identifiers: `draw.ruler`, `draw.fade`, `draw.fadeLine`, `draw.fadeOff`, `ruler.move`, `ruler.turn`, `ink.readthis`.
- Test: Core `Pencil11Tests`.

### 17.2 Pictures (21, 22, 23, 24, 39)
- Core `Pictures11.swift`: `Item.captionMeasure` (default 6.6 points a letter; the app installs `PictureCaption.install()` at start with `Lettering.measure` at size 12), `Item.captionWrapped(_:width:)` (one function for drawing and layout), `Item.captionLines`, `Item.foundWords`, `Board.noteWithPictureWords(_:)` (yellow note 24 points right of the picture and its caption).
- `Tidy.swift` lineUp / evenGaps use `Item.reach` (picture plus caption). `ParagonExport.whereLine(_:picture:)` is the one wording rule: A picture (on the board), A picture: After the makeover (see the picture), A sketch (on the board); the words did not change.
- Bar ids `<prefix>copywords` (Copy words; flash Words copied) and `<prefix>wordsnote` (Words to a note); not shown without found words; mark is the Make typed stand-in.
- Legend card: `GlyphView(.new(.legend), 14)` at its top left (`LegendCardRow`, MarksViews.swift).
- Test: `Pictures11Tests` (4).

### 17.3 Paragon (5, 26, 27, 40)
- Core `Paragon11.swift`: `SendCause` (`classify(Error)`, `words(_:what:)`, `words(for:what:)`, `inboxFolderMissing`). Mapping: POSIX 28/69 disk full; 1/13/30 locked; 16/26/11/35 in use; 2/20 missing; Cocoa 640 disk full, 513/642 locked, 4/260/2 missing, 4354 iCloud full, 4352/4353/4355 iCloud unavailable; the underlying error is looked at too (4 levels); else a plain line with the system's reason.
- `SendProblem.because(String)`; `.noInbox` says: PARAGON's Inbox folder is missing on this device. Open PARAGON once, then check again in Settings. A note or a project can still be sent. Note, picture and Inbox write failures use `because`; `append` throws the POSIX error of `open`. Example: The Inbox could not be saved: the disk is full. Free some space, then send again. Nothing was changed.
- `InboxWait` (`waitingTexts(in:)`, states waiting / filed / late, `lateAfter` 86,400 s, `state(of:in:seen:waiting:now:)`, `line(for:seen:waiting:now:)`). Reads (never changes) `capture-outbox.jsonl`: a sent line no longer in it is filed. Lines: N waiting for PARAGON, N in PARAGON, N not filed yet — open PARAGON. Unreadable list: only waiting. Time base: `board.sent.when`, else `board.changed`; seen = `ParagonEcho.read[board.id]`.
- App `Paragon11Views.swift` `InboxWaitLine` (ids `board.inboxWait`, `board.inboxWait.words`) after `SentLine` in BoardView (`low: true` for timeline and Who does what).
- `Board.chosenOnly(_:)`, `Board.chosenWords(_:)`; Send page `SendSwitch` id `send.chosenOnly`, label The N chosen things, caption Just these things are sent, as a note or as to-dos. The board is not marked as sent.; Project and Add what is new are hidden; `AppModel.send(... chosen:)`.
- `Board.emailText`: first line `Here is my board “Title”:`, the outline, last line `Made with Seedboard`. More line Copy the board as an email; flash The board is copied as an email.
- Test: `Paragon11Tests` (3). Known limits: Inbox-only boards are never read back, so they show waiting then not filed yet; no Open button on the line.

### 17.4 Done reaches PARAGON (28; needs PARAGON 341)
- Core `Tick11.swift`: `ParagonExport.DoneLine`, `doneData`, `Board.doneLines(since:now:)`; hook `BoardSession.change` (`onTicked`), `AppModel.tellParagonTicked`, `ParagonGate.appendDone`. Appends to the same `capture-outbox.jsonl` (O_APPEND). Nothing for sample boards or without a waiting list; undo and redo write nothing; taking Done off writes nothing.
- Line: `{"kind":"seedboardDone","words":"<SentTask.words>","board":"<title>","place":"inbox"|"note","path":"Projects/X.md"(note only),"when":"<ISO 8601>"}`; no `id`, no `text`. A note-place task needs `Board.sent.path`.
- Only timeline bars and milestones marked Done (`doneDay`) write a line; the to-do ring and tick lines in notes are not tied to PARAGON tasks.
- PARAGON 341: `CaptureItem.seedboardDone`, `Vault.tickSeedboardDone`, used in `AppModel.drainOutbox`; ticks only when exactly one open task has exactly those words (ignoring id, box, done) in the Inbox or the named note or project, with `Note.complete`. An older PARAGON (340) skips the unknown kind. Tests: Seedboard `Tick11Tests`, PARAGON `SeedboardDoneTests`.

### 17.5 Memory (6, 7, 8, 9, 19)
- Core `Memory11.swift`: `UndoBudget` (120 steps, 40 × 1024 × 1024 bytes; `weight(of:)` rough size: 2000 + 200 per link + 700 + text bytes per item + 100 + 8 per point (+8 per pressure) per stroke; `toLetGo(weights:)`; words Older steps were let go to save memory.), the newest step is always kept. `PictureKeeping.sharpMost` 12, `sharpToKeep(_:most:)` (most recently asked for).
- `BoardSession.undoWeights`, `@Published undoLetGo` (reset when another device's version replaces the board); `UndoListPage` id `undoList.letGo`.
- `PictureStore.sharpImage` gate at 12 (sharp plus loading); after a load, extras beyond `sharpToKeep` go.
- Export: `BoardArchive.make(name:files:fetched:fetch:missing:in:)` overload writes fetched files one by one; `ExportFile` has `url` + `place`; `ZipDocument(url:)`; `ExportPlace.clear()`.
- PDF: `BoardPicture.pdf` is async; `PaperPictures.readFiles` (off the main thread, `kCGImageSourceShouldCacheImmediately`), `PaperPictures.use(_:)` per page; `ShareMaker.files` async; `BoardPrint.ask` flash Making the pages… (60 s, cleared when done); Share plate says Making….
- Test: `Memory11Tests` (2). Limits: one very big page is drawn in one go; undo weights are an estimate.

### 17.6 Access (12, 14, 15)
- Core `Access11.swift`: `Board.spoken(_:)`, `Board.spokenThings` → `[SpokenThing]` (id, isArrow, sentence, canMove, canEdit). Sentence: kind word + ": " + words (lines joined by a space), then ", done", ", to do", ", 1 vote" / ", N votes", ", pinned", ", folded". Arrow: Arrow from A to B: words (a thing with no words is named by its kind, lowercase). Timeline and Who does what give an empty list.
- App `Access11.swift`: `BoardAccessChildren` used by `CanvasView` via `.accessibilityChildren`; identifier `board.canvas`, label Board and the count value stay on the canvas. Actions Choose, Edit words, Move left, Move right, Move up, Move down (10 points, the arrow-key nudge). Order: mind maps first branch by branch, then the rest row by row, then arrows.
- 15: `TimelinePainter.patterns` (from `accessibilityDifferentiateWithoutColor`); hatch in `TimelineProgress.drawState`; colour word in `LegendCardRow` (Yellow: Ideas). Off by default.
- Test: `Access11Tests.testSpokenSentenceAndOrder`. Not tried with VoiceOver on a device.

### 17.7 Store and unused pictures (16, 18)
- `UnusedPictures11.swift` (Core): `PictureKeeping.unusedNow(files:named:now:complete:)` (plain `.jpg` name, not named, date known, older than `freshHours` 1; `complete` false gives nothing), `unusedLine` (Pictures nobody uses: 3 (12 MB)), `sizeWords` (under 1 MB, 4.2 MB, 12 MB), `removeQuestion` (Remove 3 pictures nobody uses? This cannot be undone.).
- App `UnusedPictures11.swift`: `PictureStore.unusedPictures()`, `removeUnusedPictures()` (scans again at removal; coordinated delete in the device and iCloud Pictures folders), `UnusedPicturesRows`, `UnusedPicturesPage` (title Pictures nobody uses). Ids `settings.unusedLine`, `settings.unusedShow`, `settings.unusedRemove`, `settings.unusedTold`, `unused.page`. Results: 1 picture removed. / N pictures removed. / Nothing was removed: Seedboard could not be sure. If unsure: cannot tell right now. The weekly clean-up is unchanged (30 days, two looks a week apart).
- `PictureStore.keptBlobs(folders:places:)` is shared with the weekly tidy. `BoardDisk` is no longer private and has `init(support:cloudDocuments:)` (both nil in the app).
- Test: `UnusedPictures11Tests` (1).

### 17.8 Safety (1, 2, 4, 10, 11, 17)
- Core `Safe11.swift`: `SafeNumber` (`farthest` 100,000, `biggest` 20,000, `int`, `position`, `size`), `Board.unreadableCount(in:)`, `Board.fileDateKey` (decoder userInfo), `SafetyWords` (`damaged`, `importSkipped`, `incompleteLink`).
- `Item.init(from:)` cleans x, y, w, h. `DotGrid.dots` returns [] for a non-finite rect or more than 400,000 dots. `TimelineGeometry.day(atX:)`, `row(atY:)`, `days(forDrag:)` use `SafeNumber.int`.
- `BoardStore.interpret(_:fileDate:)` keeps the original bytes with `setAside(..., "seedboard")` and reports `.damaged`; the warning shows after write problems. Words: 1 thing on “Plan” could not be read. The whole board as it was is kept under Earlier versions. (N things on …); 1 board was made by a newer Seedboard — update Seedboard first.; red flash That board link is not complete. A missing created time takes the file's modification date (readLocal, readCloud, parked versions); import paths other than the disk readers still use 1970.
- The line lasts for this run of the app. The board stays editable. Timeline year 0001/9999 is item 3.
- Test: about ten damaged files in one test (17).

### 17.9 Timeline and due day (3, 20, 47)
- Core `Timeline11.swift`: `DayRange` (`firstYear` 1900, `lastYear` 2200, `mostMarks` 1500, `refusal`, `drawEnd`), `StarterShelf.shownBoard` (dates moved to from today), `Board.dueDay`, `Board.dueWords(today:)` (Due 12 Jan; with the year when not this year, Due 12 Jan 2027; past = before today), `Board.setDue(_:)`, `ParagonExport.withBoardDue` (replaces or adds `due:` in the project's front matter in `projectHandover`; on a timeline it replaces the last bar's day). `TimelineGeometry.ticks` stops at 1500 marks; `Board.setDates` returns 0 for an insane day.
- Dates page: the refusal Pick a date between the years 1900 and 2200. shows on Save (id `dates.problem`).
- App `DueDay.swift`: `DueDesk`, `DueWords` (id `home.due.<title>`), `DueHost`, `DueSheet` (ids `due.picker`, `due.none`, `due.cancel`, `due.save`, `due.problem`); More menu id `more.dueDay` (Due day…); Mac File menu Due Day… (beside Rename). Page: date picker, Cancel, Save, No due day. Setting a due day touches the board's changed time. The list order is not changed.
- Test: Core `Timeline11Tests`.

### 17.10 Extras (30, 33, 41, 43)
- Core `Extras11.swift`: `Board.legendWord(forColour:)`, `legendWord(ofNote:)`; `ParagonExport.namedBullet(_:in:picture:)` (used by `notesBody` in SendFrames.swift for loose and per-frame notes: - Call Alex (Question); tick-list notes and project task lines get no word); `Board.showsMainBranchesOnly(_:)`, `canFoldToMainBranches(_:)`, `toggleMainBranchesOnly(_:)` (uses `Item.folded`); `RecentBoards.limit` 5, `.opened(_:in:)`, `.shown(_:among:)`.
- `Remembered.recent: [UUID]` (device settings key `seedboard.remembered`; not in the board file; not synced; tolerant decode; gone boards dropped). `Remember` is an ObservableObject with `@Published recent`.
- App `Extras11.swift`: `TickSound` (ambient, mixWithOthers, two notes 1318.5 and 1975.5 Hz, volume 0.5; UserDefaults key `tickSound`; default on for iOS, off for macOS; silent with `-sampleData` or `-uiTesting`; follows the silent switch), `SoundsSection` (id `settings.tickSound`, header Sounds, switch Tick sound), `OpenRecentMenu`, `RecentDesk`, `BoardSession.toggleMainBranches`; bar action id `mainBranches` (Main branches only / Open all; chevron mark; shown when the middle of a map with something to fold is chosen). Mac: File, Open Recent; Dock menu ends with Opened Recently (boards already higher in the Dock menu left out); Board menu Main Branches Only / Open All Branches.
- Ticking: `toggleTodo` and `sequenceToggleTodo` call `TickSound.play()` when ticking on; `pressMark` when a tick-list line goes from unticked to ticked.
- Colour dots: `.help` and accessibility label carry the legend word; caption id `colour.legendWord` under the dots.
- Test: `Extras11Tests` (3).

### 17.11 Tests
- Data tests added in 0.11 (Core): `Access11Tests`, `Extras11Tests` 3, `Memory11Tests` 2, `Paragon11Tests` 3, `Pencil11Tests`, `Pictures11Tests` 4, `Timeline11Tests`, `Tick11Tests`, `UnusedPictures11Tests` 1, a damaged-files test for Safe11; Core total 507 at the time of the store lane.
- New target **AMSSeedboardStoreTests** (macOS unit tests, `StoreTests/BoardStoreTests.swift`, compiles `BoardStore.swift` only; in the AMSSeedboard scheme's tests; declared in `project.yml`). 5 tests run on a throwaway folder with a stand-in for iCloud: save and read back; newer wins and the older is kept; iCloud's " 2" copy joined and cleared; removal mark and a later change bringing the board back; a file that cannot be read left alone. About 0.3 seconds. Run: `xcodebuild test -project AMSSeedboard.xcodeproj -scheme AMSSeedboard -only-testing:AMSSeedboardStoreTests -destination 'platform=macOS' CODE_SIGN_IDENTITY="-" CODE_SIGN_ENTITLEMENTS=""`. It does not test `PictureStore` (needs app singletons).
- `Guide.swift`: a new 0.11 entry in What's new (above 0.10); How it works changed in: Your boards, The board, Mind maps, Marks on ideas legend focus and show, Timeline board, Drawing by hand, Pictures, Paste a list, Send to PARAGON, Safety, Share PDF and print, Menus and keys, How it feels.
- Version 0.11 (build 11).

## 18. Version 0.12 (build 12, 2026-11-19): three marks
- AMS-Icons app marks `seedRuler` (slanted ruler with three tick marks), `seedFade` (a pencil line that breaks into shorter strokes and dots), `seedReadThis` (a scribble beside a note with two lines), drawn on the 24-point grid, stroke 1.9, round ends, no fill; picked by the owner from 2 options each (19 Nov 08:17: 1, 3, 5). `NewMark.ruler`, `.fade`, `.readThis` map to them (`NewMarks.swift`).
- Draw plate (`InkViews.swift`): `draw.ruler` and `draw.fade` are `tile(...)` mark tiles (were `word(...)` buttons); identifiers and help texts unchanged ("Ruler: lines drawn along it come out straight", "Fade: show the pencil lines of this board lighter").
- Bar actions (`BarActions.swift`): `readthis` "Read this" and `wordsnote` "Words to a note" show `.new(.readThis)` (were `.new(.makeTyped)`); `copywords` keeps `.makeTyped`.
- Board format unchanged (7).

## 19. 0.13 — switches, calendar by itself, still thinking (19 Nov 2026)
Version 0.13. Settings gets on/off switches, kept on THIS device only (each iPhone, iPad and Mac has its own). Where this part and an older part disagree about a thing 0.13 changed, this part is right.

### 19.1 Sounds and taps
- Section "Sounds and taps" (was "Sounds"): switch Tick sound (as before) and NEW switch "Taps you feel" (id `settings.taps`, key `switch.taps`, on by default). It gates the small taps felt on the iPhone (and on a Mac trackpad) when things snap, land, or a Send goes through. Off = none.

### 19.2 Calendar
- Section "Calendar": switch "Timeline days in Calendar" (id `settings.calendarAuto`, key `switch.calendarAuto`, OFF by default). Line under it: id `settings.calendarLine`, e.g. "14 events in the Seedboard calendar · updated 11:42".
- On: every milestone and bar of the timeline boards goes into its own calendar called "Seedboard", made the first time, with its own colour (so it can be hidden in Calendar or Fantastical in one tap). Its identifier is kept in device settings key `calendar.seedboardID`.
- It follows by itself: moving a bar moves its event, renaming changes the event's name, removing removes the event. Milestone = one-day event; bar = one event over all its days; all-day events. Done ones stay, with "(done)" after the name. Notes: From the Seedboard board “title”. Boards that are put away are left out.
- Events carry the link `seedboard://board/<board id>/item/<row id>` as their URL; that is how Seedboard finds its own events. Events in the Seedboard calendar without such a link are never touched.
- Turn it on on ONE device; the calendar brings the days to the others. Switching it off takes Seedboard's events out again, and the Seedboard calendar too when nothing else is in it.
- Asks once for full calendar access (it must read its own events to move them). If refused, the line says: Seedboard may not use your calendar yet. Allow it in Settings ▸ Privacy ▸ Calendars (on the Mac: System Settings ▸ Privacy & Security ▸ Calendars).
- The older page More ▸ "Add to Calendar…" stays for adding by hand. While the switch is on, that page only says: Your timeline days go to the Seedboard calendar by themselves (Settings ▸ Calendar).
- Core rules: `Core/Sources/SeedboardCore/Switches13.swift`: `CalendarFollow.wanted(boards:)` (the events that should exist) and `CalendarFollow.plan(wanted:existing:)` (what to add, change, remove).

### 19.3 Home page: Still thinking about these?
- Section "Home page": switch "Still thinking about these?" (id `settings.nudge`, key `switch.nudge`, on by default).
- Boards not changed for 2 weeks come up at the TOP of the boards (home page on Mac/iPad, the list on iPhone) under the heading "Still thinking about these?" (id `home.heading.Still thinking about these?`). At most 3, the oldest first. Each row (id `nudge.board.<title>`) says e.g. "Changed 3 weeks ago" and has two buttons: "Put away" (id `nudge.putAway.<title>`) and "Not now" (id `nudge.notNow.<title>`). Pressing the board opens it.
- Not now hides that board there for 2 weeks; then it may ask again. Stored in device settings key `nudge.notNow.v1` (board id → the day it may ask again).
- Left out: pinned boards, boards sent to PARAGON, boards already put away, boards that cannot be changed.
- Core rule: `Nudge.boards(_:now:notNow:)`.

### 19.4 PARAGON switches
- Section "PARAGON" gets two switches, both on by default. "Done ticks the PARAGON task" (id `settings.doneTicksParagon`, key `switch.doneTicksParagon`): marking a timeline bar or milestone Done also ticks its task in PARAGON (needs PARAGON 341); off = Seedboard leaves PARAGON alone.
- "Read back from PARAGON" (id `settings.readParagon`, key `switch.readParagon`): boards show what PARAGON did with them ("5 of 13 done", the Open in PARAGON line, due days); off = Seedboard no longer reads PARAGON's notes and those lines disappear.

### 19.5 Search
- Section "Search": switch "Boards in search" (id `settings.search`, key `switch.search`, on by default). Your boards show up when you search on this device (Spotlight). Off takes them out of the search; on puts them back.

### 19.6 Tests and words
- Data tests: `Switches13Tests` (Core).
- `Guide.swift`: new 0.13 entry in What's new (above 0.12); new How it works part "Switches in Settings"; changed parts: The home page, Timeline board, What PARAGON did with it.
- Board format unchanged (7).

- Launch word `-calendarTest` (simulator): the sample boards go to the Seedboard calendar too, to see it work.

## 20. New in 0.14 (build 14, 19 Nov 2026)
Version 0.14 (build 14): 29 ideas from `Docs/IDEAS-0.14.md` (1–29; idea 30, a quick idea from any app on the Mac, was not built), plus the changes of the owner listed in 20.1. Where this part and an older part disagree about a thing 0.14 changed, this part is right. Nothing was run on a screen when the lanes were written: what was not seen is said under each idea.

### 20.1 Format, marks, search, keys
- Board format is now **8** (until 0.18; **9** since 0.19, see 25.1). A board changed in 0.14 opens read-only in 0.13 and older (the existing "made by a newer Seedboard" rule). New stored fields of format 8: `Board.hideDone`, `Board.faceLock`, `Item.tilt` (degrees, clockwise, −45 to 45, straight = not stored), `Item.planStart` / `Item.planEnd`, `Item.textSize` ("title" or "small"; nil = normal). A block is an item of kind `.block` (no new field). Pieces are separate files (20.14).
- Mark of a to-do that is open in PARAGON: a purple ring with a white arrow up and out (before: a purple ring with a white tick). A tick now only ever means done. The To do button's mark is a plain ring (before: a tick). The to-do mark table above already says white arrow.
- The part "Signs in this app" of How it works (already written; at the end of the guide) lists every mark. New signs added in 0.14: the faint dashed outline of a planned place; the words "2 days late" / "3 days early" beside a row name; the grey line "Not told to PARAGON yet"; "Private" on a locked card; the tab of a block; the grey line "Shown smaller".
- A board locked with Face ID is found by the app's own search by its NAME only, never by the words on it. It is left out of the device's search (Spotlight) and of the Siri/Shortcuts name list.
- "Save as a piece…" is a word-only button (no mark).
- Paste Look is ⌃⌥⌘V (⌥⌘V stays Vote Dot).

### 20.2 Calendar (ideas 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 13)
- 1: a link `seedboard://board/<board>/item/<thing>` reads the board from its FIRST part (Core `BoardLink.id`). Plain `seedboard://board/<id>` links are unchanged.
- 2: `CalendarFollow.pass` waits while iCloud has not finished its first look at the folder or a board file is not on this device yet (`BoardStore.stillArriving`); it looks again after 20 s, up to 15 times. `CalendarFollow.limited`: at most 5 days are taken out per pass (`mostRemovals`), the rest follow in the next pass (retry after 30 s). Added, changed and twin copies are not limited.
- 3: a fingerprint of the wanted days is kept after each complete pass (device key `calendar.wantedSignature`); unchanged = the pass is skipped. A real look is forced when the switch is turned on, after a failure, while removals wait, and when the app comes to the front after more than an hour.
- 4: on failure nothing is saved, the old "updated" time and the signature stay, and the line under the switch reads "Could not update the calendar — will try again" (id `settings.calendarFailed`, problem colour); a new try follows after 60 s.
- 5: if no "Seedboard" calendar is found, it waits 8 s, refreshes the sources and looks again before making one. With two or more of that name the remembered one (else the first) is kept, our days in the others move into it, and an other one is removed only when nothing else is in it.
- 6: switching off removes our events looking as far as our days were ever put (first and last day kept: `calendar.firstDay`, `calendar.lastDay`), then removes the calendar only if no other event is found from 5 years back to 10 ahead (or as far as our days reach). Else the calendar stays and a line says: "Seedboard's days are out of the calendar. The calendar itself stays: other days are in it."
- 13 Copy link to this: bar button and menu line "Copy link to this" (ids `sel.copyLink`, `menu.copyLink`), uses the existing Copy mark. Line after: "Link copied. It opens this board at this thing." Where: one chosen idea, note, text, frame, picture, sketch, door or shape on an ordinary board, before Remove; behind "…" when the bar is narrow (drop 7). Opening: the board opens, the thing is chosen and the view glides and zooms (60 % to 120 %). An open board just glides. On timelines and Who does what boards the row is chosen and brought into sight (no zoom). A gone thing: "That thing is no longer on the board." (problem colour, 5 s). Does NOT: not for timeline bars or Who does what things (`placesItself`), not for several things, does not unfold a folded branch.
- Not seen: nothing run (no simulator, real calendar or iCloud); the glide and the arrival test on a real iCloud folder. Tests: `Calendar14Tests` (3).

### 20.3 Safety (ideas 7–12)
- 7: a picture is never made bigger than about 100 million pixels; a board that would pass that is drawn at a smaller scale. Share page (Picture): a small grey line "Shown smaller" (id `share.shownSmaller`), only when the board is that large. Ordinary boards unchanged (2x, 1x above 3,000 points).
- 8: a PDF page whose board is shrunk four times or more to fit the paper, or that holds more than 500 things, is drawn as 4 strips top to bottom, one after the other with the screen free in between. Looks the same. No words, no controls. Not compared with a printed page.
- 9: Undo of "Move to a new board" also puts the new board away (never deleted; under Put away). Board notice: "The new board “<name>” is put away." Redo brings it back (only if Undo put it away). Only for moves made in this open session.
- 10: "Make a copy" of a put-away board is not put away (Core `Board.becomeCopy`; other resets as before).
- 11: Share page problem line "Only the first 20,000 letters were taken." (same place and style as the picture problem lines).
- 12: when the "this bar is done" line cannot be written (no waiting list, folder refuses; `appendDone` now returns true/false), it is kept on the device (key `paragon.doneNotTold`, up to 200, 60 days) and tried again at the next start and at Settings ▸ Check again. Duplicates are not kept. Small grey line "Not told to PARAGON yet" (no mark; id `board.notTold`) at the top of the board until it went through. It shows on the board that holds the untold tick, not on one bar. PARAGON not changed.
- Tests: `Safety14Tests` (5). No screen test.

### 20.4 Tilt (idea 14)
- Notes, pictures, free texts and the eight flowchart shapes (box, round, diamond, oval, person, document, slanted, store). NOT mind-map ideas, frames, doors, sketches, tables, timeline or Who does what things. Stored in `Item.tilt`.
- Bar: words-only button "Tilt" (id `sel.tilt`, no mark): straight → a little left (−4°) → a little right (4°) → straight; lit while tilted; behind "…" when narrow (drop 7, goes first with Vote); also on several things when all can tilt. Menu: "Tilt" (`menu.tilt`) with "Straighten" (`menu.tilt.straighten`, grey when straight), "Lean left" (`menu.tilt.left`), "Lean right" (`menu.tilt.right`).
- iPhone and iPad: a two-finger twist on the one chosen thing follows the fingers; the board does not slide or zoom meanwhile; lifting within 3° of straight snaps straight. One undo step per twist and per button press. Not on timelines / Who does what; not on a read-only board.
- Drawing: thing, words and picture turn round the middle (one painter, so PDF, print and the picture for PARAGON show it). Vote dots, pin and link line turn with it; the chosen frame and grips follow. Presses use the turned shape (`Tilt.contains/rotate`, `Board.hit`). Arrows still end on the edge of the un-turned box.
- Does NOT: free angle slider, twist handle on the Mac (Mac: button and menu only), trackpad twist. Known rough edges: a size-knob drag on a tilted thing resizes along the straight box; typing in a tilted thing shows the text field upright; the press area of a very small tilted thing uses the finger rule. Not seen: the look turned, the twist on a real touch screen. Tests: `Tilt14Tests` (3).

### 20.5 Copy look, text sizes, zoom to these, today (ideas 15, 17, 19, 26)
- 15: with ONE thing chosen, "Copy look" keeps its colour, shape kind and (free text) text size on this device only (not in the board, not in iCloud, gone when the app closes); message "Look copied" (2 s). "Paste look" (shown only when a look is kept and a chosen thing can take part of it) puts it on all chosen things in ONE undo step, only what fits: note = colour only (from the note colours); shape = colour + shape kind from a shape; mind-map branch idea = branch colour (main idea nothing); frame and sketch = colour; free text = text size from a free text; pictures, doors, timeline rows = nothing. A colour a kind lacks is skipped (grey is not a note colour). Ids `sel.copylook`, `sel.pastelook`, `menu.copylook`, `menu.pastelook`; existing Copy and Paste marks, word beside each (mark only on a narrow iPhone). Mac Edit: Copy Look ⌥⌘C, Paste Look ⌃⌥⌘V. Not on timelines / Who does what. Rules `Board.lookToCopy`, `canTakeLook`, `pasteLook`.
- 17: `Item.textSize`: "title" = 28 pt semibold, nil = 17 normal, "small" = 13; measuring, drawing and typing use `Lettering.style`. Same for everyone. Text sent to PARAGON unchanged. Bar "Size" for one chosen free text (list Title / Normal / Small, current one on); menu "Size" with the same list; ids `sel.size`, `sel.size.title`, `.normal`, `.small`, `menu.size…`. Does NOT change ideas, notes or shapes; no other sizes; a menu with several chosen has no Size.
- 19: "Zoom to these" (bar, menu, Mac View ▸ Zoom to Chosen ⌘3; ids `sel.zoomchosen`, `menu.zoomchosen`) glides 0.30 s so the chosen things fill the screen (margin 60 pt sides, 110 pt top/bottom), in up to 2x, out down to 20 %. `Viewport.fitChosen`. Not on timelines / Who does what; not for one chosen arrow alone.
- 26: keyboard bar button "Today" (iPhone/iPad, left of Done; VoiceOver "Insert today's date"; id `board.key.today`) and Mac Edit ▸ Insert Today's Date ⇧⌘D (only while typing) put "19 Nov 2026" (day, month in three letters, year; English, same on every device) at the cursor, replacing chosen words, cursor after. Before iOS 18 / macOS 15 (cursor unknown) it goes at the end. No other date forms; no mark beside "Today". `DateWords.short`, `Typing.insert`.
- Not seen: the bar on a narrow iPhone with the new buttons; the glide on a real board; "Today" on iPad. Tests: `Look14Tests` (4).

### 20.6 Pictures (ideas 16, 28)
- 16 "Lift out" (id `picture.liftout`): Vision's foreground mask (iOS 17 / macOS 14) makes a NEW picture with clear background, just right of the old (28 pt gap, same middle height); old stays; one undo step; new one chosen. Lines: "Lifting the subject out…" (2 s), "No subject found in this picture" (problem), "That picture is not here yet.", "The subject was not lifted out: the board was closed." Where: the picture's bar (after Crop) and its menu; grey while the file is not here yet. Saved as `<UUID>.png` (`PictureStore.takePNG`, long side at most 2048, transparency kept); tidy pass, "Pictures nobody uses", iCloud watch and export-back now accept `.png` too (`PictureKeeping.isPictureFileName`; an old test changed from ".png is refused" to ".gif is refused"). Does NOT: choose a subject (all found come together), soften edges, undo the file itself. Not seen: the real Vision result; the layout.
- 28 PDF: dropped (Finder, Files, Mail), pasted (a PDF file copied in the Finder, or PDF data) or chosen in the Mac picture chooser (Insert ▸ Picture…, now lists PDFs). Page 1 on white, long side 2048 px, kept as JPEG. More than one page asks "This PDF has 6 pages": "Only page 1" / "All pages" / Cancel (ids `pdf.ask.first`, `pdf.ask.all`, `pdf.ask.cancel`). All pages = up to 20 in a row (20 pt apart); more: "A PDF lays 20 pages at most. The first 20 of 47 are here." Unreadable: "That PDF could not be read." ("3 PDFs could not be read."). Cancel leaves that PDF out. One page asks nothing. Mixed drops work (`layMixed`). The iPhone/iPad Photos picker shows photos only: there a PDF comes by drop (iPad) or Paste; no new "Files" way on the Add a picture plate (it would need a new mark). Launch word `-askPDF`. Does NOT: page range, keep the PDF, make text of it. Not seen: a real drop; the dialog; Mail's drag on iPad.
- Tests: `Pictures14Tests` (5), screen tests `Pictures14UITests` (2, built only).

### 20.7 Timeline (ideas 18, 29)
- 18 "Hide done" (`timeline.hideDone`; becomes "Show done"): in the timeline's own buttons after Today / Catch up; shown only when something is done or hiding is on. Leaves out every done bar and milestone and a phase whose rows are all done (an empty phase stays); rows below move up; arrows to a hidden bar are not drawn; nothing removed. Line above the controls "3 done hidden · Show" (`timeline.doneHidden`, button `timeline.showDone`). Stored `Board.hideDone`. Mac board menu: "Hide Done Bars" / "Show Done Bars". One Undo step. Not on read-only boards; no key, no right-click line. Core `hidesDone`, `hiddenDoneIDs`, `hiddenDoneCount`.
- 29 "Set the plan" (`more.setPlan`) writes each bar's and milestone's start/end into `Item.planStart/planEnd` (replaces an earlier plan). A bar whose days differ gets a faint dashed outline (own colour, ~45 % opacity) where it was planned, and its row name shows small grey "2 days late" / "3 days early" (by last day; a bar that only changed length but ends on the planned day shows the ghost only). A bar added after the plan has none. "Clear the plan" (`more.clearPlan`, off when no plan). Message "The plan is set: N rows as they stand today." Mac menu "Set the Plan" / "Clear the Plan". One Undo each. Bars without days get no plan. Not: no right-click line, no different word on a done bar, no plan day on screen. Core `Item.planSpan`, `slipDays`, `slipWords`, `movedFromPlan`, `Board.setPlan/clearPlan`.
- Not seen: the ghost and the slip words in a narrow names column. Tests: `Timeline14Tests` (3).

### 20.8 Who does what: blocks (idea 23)
- A thin rounded frame round messages, a word in a small tab at its top left; placed from its first and last message each time (never by hand); nested blocks get +8 pt margin per block held; room is made above and under.
- Make: choose one or more messages ▸ "Block" (`sel.block`, beside To do) ▸ "Repeat" (`sel.block.repeat`, teal), "If" (`.if`, orange), "Maybe" (`.maybe`, blue), "Other words…" (`.other`, grey; opens the tab for typing, Escape takes the block away). Runs from the first to the last chosen message in order, with messages, notes and sections between inside. Only messages count; with none chosen no Block chip. The new block is chosen.
- Choose: press its tab (two quick presses type its word). Bar: "Words", "Colour" ("No colour" = grey), "Remove block" (`sel.removeBlock`; frame only). Delete/Backspace also removes the frame only. Select all includes blocks. A block losing its first or last message shrinks to what is left; with nothing left it goes.
- Word stored in lower case from the menu; an empty word shows "block". `sequence.chosen` says the word.
- PARAGON text (note and "copy as text"): messages inside are indented 3 spaces per block under a line "Repeat:" / "If:" / "Maybe:" / "Block:" (first letter capital). The project task lines (`- [ ]`) and Inbox tasks stay flat. Count line "… · 1 block" / "N blocks". No Mac menu line, no key.
- Does NOT: fill colour, an "else" divider (use two blocks), drag or resize a block by hand (0.19: dragging a MESSAGE now glides and keeps blocks right, see 25.6, idea 33), copy a block with Duplicate, appear in task lines. Core `SequenceBlocks14.swift`, `ItemKind.isSequence` includes `.block`. Not seen: tab position and margins; two tabs at the same first message (outer one 20 pt higher); menu item ids inside a macOS menu unproven. Tests: `Sequence14Tests` (4), screen test built only.

### 20.9 Table (idea 25)
- A small grid of cells on an ordinary board. New: 3 columns × 3 rows, empty; first row = heading row (bold, light band of the table's colour); thin lines; each column as wide as its widest cell (max 220 pt, longer words wrap; 0.19: unless a hand width is stored, see 25.6, idea 35); each row as high as its highest cell; text 15; day and night colours from Paint; one painter for the PDF and the PARAGON picture.
- Make: Mac Insert ▸ "Table" (grey on a read-only board, a timeline, Who does what); Shapes plate line "Table" (`plate.shape.table`); right-click empty board "New table here" (`menu.table`, plus mark, lands where pressed). The new table is chosen, not typed in.
- Type: two presses on a cell (green ring); Words, Return or Space on a chosen table types in the cell typed in last (else the first). Tab keeps the words and goes to the next cell (first of the next row after a row's last); after the last cell it ends. Return ends. Escape = Cancel. Each cell is one undo step.
- Bar: "Add row", "Add column" (plus mark), "Remove row", "Remove column" (minus-circle mark; grey when only one left), "Words", "Colour", "Duplicate", behind "…" the rest, "Remove". Ids `sel.table.addRow`, `.addColumn`, `.removeRow`, `.removeColumn`; menu has the same lines (without Words) with `menu.` ids plus Colour, Copy, Cut, Duplicate, Remove. New row/column after that of the cell typed in last, else at the end; removal takes that one, else the last. At least 1 row and 1 column; at most 60 rows, 20 columns. No To do.
- PARAGON note: a plain list under "Notes" (and under the frame's heading): `- <top-left cell or "A table">` then `  - Row heading — Column heading: words` for each filled cell (missing headings say "Row 1", "Column 1"). Search (list, device) finds every cell; "Copy as outline" has the same list; counts as ONE thing ("1 idea").
- Does NOT: merged cells, (until 0.18: column dragging; done in 0.19, see 25.6, idea 35) cell colours, arrows into cells, formulas, tilt. iPhone plate corner is hold-the-tile only. Not seen: layout, ring, band colours by night, field position at zoom. Tests: `Table14Tests` (5), screen test built only.

### 20.10 Boards (ideas 20, 21, 24)
- 20 "Join with…" (`card.join`) in a board's menu (card press-and-hold / right-click, iPhone row, Mac right-click) after "Make a copy". Not on timelines / Who does what, boards put away, boards a newer Seedboard made. Page: heading "Join with…", hint "The things of “Garden” are laid to the right of the board you choose. “Garden” goes under Put away.", rows = other ordinary, changeable, not-put-away boards (name + list line), chosen row says "Chosen"; empty "There is no other board to join with."; buttons "Cancel" (Escape) and "Join" (full colour, off until chosen). Ids `join.page`, `join.cancel`, `join.join`, `join.hint`, `join.empty`, `join.list`, `join.row.<title>`. Result: all things of THIS board are laid right of the chosen board's things (80 pt gap, tops level), arrows kept, ids made new only where one clashes; the chosen board is saved and opened (name, colour, id stay); THIS board is put away. Line "“Garden” was joined in. It is under Put away." (6 s). The chosen board's undo list holds the join as one step when it was open. Not done: drag one card onto another; doors in other boards that pointed at the put-away board still open it; not on boards that place things themselves. Joining a locked board asks first.
- 21 "Lock with Face ID" / "Unlock": card menu and board More (`card.lock`, `more.lock`); the word says Touch ID on a Mac with Touch ID, "Lock with device code" where there is no biometric. Sets `Board.faceLock`. Policy `.deviceOwnerAuthentication`. Locking does not ask; Unlocking asks ("Unlock “Garden”."); opening asks ("Open “Garden”.") on every way in (card, list, link, Handoff, Shortcuts, Spotlight: all through `AppModel.open(_:)`, old body `openNow`). Failures: "The board stays closed." / "The board stays locked."; no code on the device: "This device has no code set up, so the board cannot be opened." / "Set up a code or Face ID on this device first." Lines: "“Garden” is locked. It asks for Face ID to open." / "“Garden” is unlocked." Locked card (and iPhone row picture): title and colour dot, picture area empty with the word "Private" (`card.private`). Left out of Spotlight (and forgotten there on locking), of the Siri/Shortcuts name list and of "Still thinking about these?". The app's own search finds a locked board by NAME only (owner's decision). `NSFaceIDUsageDescription` = "To open boards you locked." (`project.yml`). NOT done: the list row's counts (for example "12 ideas") still show; the lock is not asked again when the app comes back from the background for an already open board; the lock change is in the board's undo list; a lock is not encryption — the file is not scrambled (the guide says so).
- 24 "Open an outline file": Mac File ▸ "Open Outline…" (after Starters…); iPhone/iPad a small "More" menu in the list's bottom line (next to Settings) with "Open an outline file" (`boards.openOutline`, menu `boards.more`). Takes .md, .txt, .opml (and xml). A new mind map titled by the file's name (first heading / OPML title only if the name is empty); one top entry = centre, several hang under a centre called the file name. Markdown reads like Paste a list (`Outline.parse`); OPML via `OpmlOutline`. "No outline was found in that file." Over 5 MB refused. NOT done: drop of a file on the window; export to OPML.
- Not seen: any running screen, the Face ID prompt. Tests: `Boards14Tests` (3), screen test built only.

### 20.11 Brainstorm timer (idea 22)
- Board More ▸ "Brainstorm timer" (`more.brainstorm`; ordinary boards only) and Mac View ▸ "Brainstorm Timer". A small pill at the top with a thin ring that fills. Before start: "Brainstorm", "5 min" (`timer.min.5`), "10 min" (`timer.min.10`), "Cancel" (`timer.cancel`); running: "4:12 left" (`timer.left`) and "Stop" (`timer.stop`); end: "Time!" for 4 s (`timer.timeUp`), then the pill goes. Pill id `timer.pill`. Choosing the line again while it runs offers the choices again.
- End: one soft tap (`Feel.timeUp()`, respects "Taps you feel") and `TickSound.play()` (follows its own "Tick sound" switch: off on the Mac at first). Nothing is saved; leaving the board stops it. Rule `BrainstormTimer` (Core `Pieces14.swift`).
- Not: pause, custom length, a sound file, a notification when the app is left. Not seen on a screen.

### 20.12 Pieces (idea 27)
- With things chosen on an ordinary board: "Save as a piece…" (`sel.savePiece`, `menu.savePiece`; word-only button; several chosen: in the bar, moving behind "…" when narrow; one chosen: behind "…"; menu after Copy/Cut/Duplicate). Keeps them with the arrows between them; an idea's whole branch comes along. Sheet: heading "Save as a piece", field "Name" (starts with the first chosen idea's words), line "5 ideas with the arrows between them. You can add the piece to any board later.", Cancel / Save (`pieceName.sheet`, `.field`, `.cancel`, `.save`). After: "Saved as the piece “Name”."
- More ▸ "Add a piece…" (`more.addPiece`) and Mac Insert ▸ "Piece…" open the page "Pieces" (`pieces.page`, `pieces.list`, `pieces.row.<id>`): count line under each name ("5 ideas", "3 things", "1 idea"), footer "Press a piece to add a copy of it to this board.", empty "No pieces yet. Choose some things on a board, then press “Save as a piece…”.", buttons Close (`pieces.close`) and Edit (`pieces.edit`). Edit mode: names editable (`pieces.name.<id>`), "Remove" / "Keep" per piece (`pieces.remove.<id>`), Cancel / Done (`pieces.cancel`, `pieces.done`), footer "Rename a piece or mark it to remove. Removed pieces are kept for 30 days." `pieces.empty` for the empty page.
- Pressing a piece drops a copy (new ids) in a free spot near the middle of the screen, chosen, one undo step: "Added the piece “Name”."
- Stored like own starters: `Pieces/<id>.seedpiece` beside Starters, on the device (Application Support) and iCloud Documents; newer `changed` wins; removed ones keep their file 30 days. Simpler than starters: no working copy, no "SafetyCopies" set-aside; an iCloud file not yet arrived or unreadable is never written over; iCloud "<id> 2" copies are ignored. Rules `PieceShelf.cut/.drop/.save/.rename/.remove/.purge`, `PieceEntry.countLine/newer`, `PieceFileName`.
- Not: pieces on timelines / Who does what; reordering; thumbnails; a picture in a piece keeps only the picture's file reference (may not show in a board without that file — same as copy/paste); no stored field on boards.
- Not seen: the page or the bar button's room on the iPhone bar. Tests: `Pieces14Tests` (3), screen test built only.

### 20.13 Words and tests
- `Guide.swift`: new 0.14 entry in What's new (above 0.13); How it works changed in: Your boards, Sticky notes text and frames, Shapes and arrows, Moving around, Timeline board, Who does what, Pictures, new parts Tables, Brainstorm timer and Pieces, Switches in Settings, Safety, What PARAGON did with it, Share PDF and print, Sharing to Seedboard, Shortcuts Siri Raycast and Spotlight, Menus and keys, The bar above the keyboard, Signs in this app.
- Core tests 515+ green when the lanes were written; screen tests of the new screens are built, not run. Version 0.14 (build 14).

## 21. New in 0.15 (build 15, 19 Nov 2026)
Version 0.15 (build 15): colour, tilt and lock fixes. Where this part and an older part disagree, this part is right. Board format stays 8; no new stored fields.

### 21.1 Milestone and timeline colours
- One colour, one meaning: purple means PARAGON only; red means late (and, in the lines at the bottom, something did not work).
- A NEW milestone is orange (before: purple). A milestone with no colour (empty colour) is drawn orange (before: purple).
- Colours a timeline picks in turn never include purple or red. Bars: pink, orange, teal, blue, green, then round again. Phases: teal, blue, orange, green, pink.
- Milestones, bars and phases already on a board keep their stored colour. "Colour" in the bar changes them (nine colours, unchanged).
- Texts: How it works (Timeline, Signs in this app) says purple = PARAGON only, orange diamond = a milestone, red = late / did not work. Older parts of this file and notes-0.2 that say "milestone purple" are history.

### 21.2 Tilt: sizing and typing
- Dragging a size knob of a tilted note, picture, free text or flowchart shape sizes it along its own tilted edges; the opposite corner stays where it is on the screen. Before: the drag sized the straight box (the known rough edge of 20.4).
- Snap guides (the thin catch lines) are not offered while a tilted thing is sized.
- The typing field of a tilted thing is tilted by the same angle, so the words typed stand where they will be drawn. Before: the field was straight.

### 21.3 Lock closes on leaving
- A board locked with Face ID / Touch ID / device code closes when the app goes to the background: iPhone and iPad when you go Home or switch app; Mac when the app is hidden.
- On return the list (or the home page) shows, with a line: “<board name>” was closed while you were away. (the real name in the quotes). Opening it asks again.
- Boards that are not locked are not closed.

### 21.4 Course and texts
- The course (the tour web page) gets a chapter "New in 0.14" (outside the app; What's new says it in one line).
- `Guide.swift`: new 0.15 entry in What's new (above 0.14); How it works changed in: Timeline (milestone, bar and phase colours), Sticky notes text and frames (tilt), Your boards (lock), Signs in this app (purple = PARAGON only, orange diamond, red).

## 22. New in 0.16 (build 16, 19 Nov 2026)
Version 0.16 (build 16): one question about old purple milestones. Where this part and an older part disagree, this part is right. Board format stays 8; no new stored fields.

### 22.1 Make purple milestones orange?
- Background: before 0.15 a new milestone was purple; since 0.15 it is orange, because purple means PARAGON only. Milestones made earlier kept their purple.
- When: once the boards have loaded, if any timeline that can be changed (not read-only) has at least one purple milestone, a question comes up ONCE per device.
- Heading: "Make purple milestones orange?" Words, for example: "5 milestones on 2 timelines are purple. Purple means PARAGON in Seedboard, and new milestones are orange." The numbers are the real counts.
- Buttons: "Make Orange" (the main, full-colour button) and "Keep Purple" (Escape on the Mac).
- Make Orange: every purple milestone on every such timeline becomes orange; milestones of other colours stay as they are. A line says, for example, "5 milestones are orange now." On a board that is open, Undo takes it back for that board (one Undo step per board).
- Keep Purple: nothing changes. The question does not come again on this device. Colour in the bar still changes any single milestone.
- Once per device: the answer is remembered on this device after either button. Boards sync, so after Make Orange on one device the others find nothing purple and do not ask. Timelines that cannot be changed (read-only, for example made by a newer Seedboard) are left out and are not counted.
- A milestone with no colour is still drawn orange (21.1); only a stored purple colour counts as purple.

### 22.2 Course and texts
