- The course (the tour web page) draws a tilted note in its Tilt step and a locked board's card with the word Private in its Lock step (outside the app; What's new says it in one line).
- `Guide.swift`: new 0.16 entry in What's new (above 0.15); How it works changed in Timeline board (the milestone-colour text). Signs in this app is unchanged: it already says purple = PARAGON only.

## 23. New in 0.17 (build 17, 20 Nov 2026)
Version 0.17 (build 17): coloured cards on the list, folding, and purple only for PARAGON. Where this part and an older part disagree, this part is right. Board format stays 8; no new stored board fields.

### 23.1 iPhone first page
- Heading: a large green "Seedboard" at the top of the list (green = Seedboard's own colour). Under it one grey line with live numbers, for example "14 boards · 2 pinned · 4 in PARAGON".
- The settings gear sits alone at the top right, no word "Settings". The bottom line with "Settings", "More" and the version number is gone on iPhone and iPad. The version number is in Settings (its existing Version line, under About).
- Cards: each part of the list sits on a soft card in its own colour: Starters, Pinned, Recent (green), Sent to PARAGON (purple, PARAGON's colour). The heading of a card shows a count in a small round-ended pill and a round arrow button at the right.
- Starters card (iPhone only): the starters as a strip of small cards that slide sideways; press one to start a new board from it; press and hold for its menu. Not shown on the very first start (no board at all), when the list itself shows the starters.
- "Open an outline file" (formerly the only line under the old "More" button) is now the last line of the New board chooser (the + chooser), on iPhone and iPad.

### 23.2 Folding
- Pressing a card heading or its arrow folds the card to a slim line; pressing again unfolds it. The arrow points down when open, right when folded.
- Folds are kept per device in UserDefaults: `fold.list.starters`, `fold.list.pinned`, `fold.list.recent`, `fold.list.sent` (Bool, true = folded; missing = open). They do not sync.
- While a search is typed, every card is shown unfolded; the saved folds are not changed by searching.
- Mac: the look is unchanged (bottom line with Settings and version stays), but the list's headings also fold when pressed, with the same keys.

### 23.3 iPad
- The list column gets the same big green heading and count line, the gear alone at the top right (no word), no bottom line, and the coloured folding cards (Pinned, Recent, Sent to PARAGON). No Starters card: the home page already shows the starters large. "Open an outline file" is in the New board chooser.

### 23.4 Purple only for PARAGON
- Timeline: More line "Make Milestones Orange" (Mac: also in the menu bar next to Set the Plan / Clear the Plan). Same action as the one-time question of 22.1, any time later (for example after Keep Purple). Shown only while that timeline has purple milestones (Mac: greyed out when none). One Undo step per board.
- Mind map: `Palette.branches` is now pink, orange, teal, blue, yellow, green, red (yellow replaced purple as fifth; 0.18 takes red out again, see section 24). A main branch never comes purple by itself.
- One-time question "Recolour purple branches?", buttons "New Colours" (main) and "Keep Purple"; remembered per device under the key `ask.purpleBranches.v1` after either button. New Colours gives every purple main branch a colour its sister main branches do not use yet, else yellow. It comes after the milestone question (22.1); never both at once.
- More line "Recolour Purple Branches" (Mac: also in the menu bar): shown only while the board has purple main branches; can be undone.
- Who does what: new names take `Board.whoColours` = blue, teal, orange, yellow, green, pink in turn (purple removed in 0.17, red in 0.18); a name with no stored colour falls back to `Palette.branch(place)` (also without purple). Names stored purple before 0.17 keep it.

### 23.5 Signs and texts
- Signs in this app has four new lines: the round arrow on a card heading (down = open, right = folded), the count pill, the gear alone at the top right = Settings, and the card colours (green = boards, purple = Sent to PARAGON). Purple still means PARAGON only.
- `Guide.swift`: new 0.17 entry in What's new; How it works changed in Your boards, Starters chooser (New board), Settings, Open an outline file, mind-map branch colours, Timeline, Who does what, iPhone, Version, Signs.

## 24. New in 0.18 (build 18, 20 Nov 2026)
Version 0.18 (build 18): branches no longer take red (red is kept for late); one question for purple and red branches. Where this part and an older part disagree, this part is right. Board format stays 8; no new stored board fields.

### 24.1 Branch colours
- `Palette.branches` goes from 7 colours to 6: pink, orange, teal, blue, yellow, green (red removed; 23.4 had it as seventh). `branch(i)` wraps round after 6.
- A main branch never takes purple (PARAGON) or red (late) by itself. Red can still be picked by hand from the colour row (`board.colours`, dot `colour.red`); the same for purple. Branches already red or purple are not changed unless the question or the menu line (24.3) is used.
- Who does what: `Board.whoColours` = blue, teal, orange, yellow, green, pink (six; red removed, purple was removed in 0.17). A name with no stored colour falls back to `Palette.branch(place)`. Names that already have a stored colour keep it.
- One colour, one meaning: purple = PARAGON, red = a warning (late on timelines, the today line, "did not work" in the short lines at the bottom).

### 24.2 One question: Recolour purple and red branches?
- Core file `Branches18.swift`: `OddBranches` (counts of odd main branches and mind maps), `Board.oddBranches(_:)` (the purple or red main branches of a board, read-only boards left out by the caller) and `Board.recolourBranches(_:)` (does the change, one Undo step per board). Ideas further out are not touched: they follow their branch's colour.
- App `Orange16.swift` holds the question, next to the milestone question of 22.1.
- When: once the boards have loaded, if a mind map that can be changed (not read-only) has at least one purple or red main branch. Once per device. It comes after the milestone question; never both at once.
- Keys (UserDefaults): `ask.oddBranches.v1` is new in 0.18 and is set after either button. `ask.purpleBranches.v1` (0.17) is only read: when it is set, this device already answered the purple question, so the question counts red only.
- Heading: "Recolour purple and red branches?"; when `ask.purpleBranches.v1` is set: "Recolour red branches?".
- Message, for example: "3 branches on 2 mind maps are purple or red. Purple means PARAGON and red means late in Seedboard, and new branches skip both." Red only: "1 branch on 1 mind map is red. Red means late in Seedboard, and new branches skip it." The numbers are the real counts (singular and plural follow them).
- Buttons: "New Colours" (main, full colour) and "Keep Them" (Escape on the Mac; before: "Keep Purple").
- New Colours: each such main branch gets a colour its sister main branches do not use yet, in the order of `Palette.branches`, else yellow. Ideas further out follow their branch. A line says how many branches changed.
- Keep Them: nothing changes; the question does not come again on this device. Colour in the bar still changes a branch.
- Boards sync, so after New Colours on one device the others find nothing odd and do not ask.

### 24.3 Menu line
- More line (iPhone and iPad) and Mac menu-bar line, id `more.recolourBranches`: now "Recolour Branches" (before: "Recolour Purple Branches"). Shown while the board has purple or red main branches (Mac: greyed out when none). Does what 24.2 does, at any time; one Undo step per board.

### 24.4 Course and texts
- The course (`Docs/intro/index.html`, done separately) shows the 0.17 iPhone list (coloured folding cards, gear top right) and has steps for the cards, the gear and the branch colours.
- `Guide.swift`: new 0.18 entry in What's new (above 0.17); How it works changed in Mind maps (branch colours; the recolour paragraph), Who does what (name colours), Signs in this app (red = late stated in one line; branches never take purple or red by themselves). Older entries in What's new keep their words, as they were true then.
- Ideas for later: `Docs/IDEAS-0.18.md` (50 ideas, none built yet). To try by hand: `Docs/WHAT-TO-TEST-0.18.txt`.
- Tests: one data test, `Core/Tests/SeedboardCoreTests/Branches18Tests.swift` — the six-colour order and `whoColours` without red, `OddBranches` (count, title, line), `oddBranches`, `recolourBranches` (red only keeps purple; both clears both). No new screen, so no new screen test; the question keeps the 0.17 identifiers `branches.recolour` / `branches.keep`.

## 25. New in 0.19 (build 19, 20 Nov 2026)
Version 0.19 (build 19): 50 numbered ideas of `Docs/IDEAS-0.18.md`, 48 ticked by the owner (1–10, 12–46, 48–50); 46 built new, 2 were already there (19 and 23). 11 (copy by dragging) and 47 (PDF with one page per frame) were turned down. Where this part and an older part disagree, this part is right. Built in nine lanes (list, canvasa, canvasb, maps, timeline, seqtable, pencil, share, mac), each with a data test; screen tests (`UITests/*19UITests.swift`) were written and compile, none was run. No simulator and no device was used for any of it, so every "Not seen" below is open. Owner's answers (one numbered picture): all 17 new marks OK (they are Apple's own SF Symbols), the paper colours as shown, the Mac sidebar key ⌃⌘S, a direct Camera tile added.

### 25.1 Board format 9, the stored fields, the shared groundwork
- `Board.currentFormat` = **9**. A board that 0.19 changes opens read-only in 0.18 and older (the "made by a newer Seedboard" rule). New stored fields: item `note`, `initials`, `cropShape` ("circle"), `printFrame`, `widths` (per column, 0 or missing = by words), `tickColumn`; arrow `bend` ([dx, dy] from the plain line's middle; under 4 points = straight, nothing stored); board `paper`, `numberBranches`, `mapLook`. `StoredFieldsTests` carries the golden list.
- Not stored in the board file: Read mode (UserDefaults `readMode.<board id>`), the list sort (`list.sort`), recent colours (`recentColours19`, last 6), recent pen colours (`ink.recentColours`), iPad tool-bar edge (`tool.dock`), Mac sidebar (`mac.sidebarHidden`), the Draw plate tip counter (`ink.holdTip`), the last table sort (`TableSortMemory`, memory only).
- `Glyph` (BoardView.swift) gained the case `.symbol(String)` (an Apple SF Symbol by name; one case, merged from lanes that each made their own; a second `.system` of the maps lane also exists). The + tile's hold menu is WORDS ONLY (Note, Idea, Shape, Text) because the note mark means "has a note" and the lightbulb means "Grow ideas" elsewhere.
- Ticked lines of a pasted checklist (done ideas) are never sent to the PARAGON Inbox, like done timeline bars.
- Double-tap on empty board (idea 9, lane canvasa): `Finger.twoPressesFit` (FingerSizes.swift) false everywhere. Two presses within 0.42 s on empty board make a sticky note with its middle at the press, typing at once (`session.add(.note, at:)`; refused on read-only, Read mode and self-placing boards). Before: iPhone and iPad showed the whole board. Fit stays on the zoom buttons and ⌘1. The empty-board hint "or double-click anywhere for a sticky note" stays Mac-only. `testTwoPressesOnEmptyBoardShowTheWholeBoard` (TouchUITests) now expects "1 note" in `board.count` (not run). The old lines about this in 3.x, 2.x and 11.x (456, 1109, 1183) were corrected in place.

### 25.2 The list (lane list; ids `boards.*`)
- Core `List19.swift`; tests `List19Tests` (5). App `List19.swift`, edits in RootView and HomeView. Mac: 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 8, 9 in the sidebar; 2, 5, 6, 8, 9 and the Recent order also on the home cards. Swipe and pull-down touch only.
- Idea 1 swipe (`RowSwipe19`, rows are not in a List): swipe right = blue "Pin" (pin) / "Unpin" (pin.slash); left = slate "Put away" (archivebox). Over 44 pt leaves the 88-pt button open; over 150 pt does it at once; a tap on the open row closes it. Uses `model.togglePin` / `model.putAway`. Not offered on put-away boards or boards that cannot be changed. VoiceOver actions Pin / Unpin / Put away. Ids `boards.swipePin.<title>`, `boards.swipeAway.<title>`. Not seen: the feel; on iPad the row's drag-to-door (`onDrag`) may fight a quick sideways swipe.
- Idea 2 found line (`Board.foundLine(_:)` → `FoundLine`, pieces with a bold flag): small grey line under the name, the first line (of a thing, an arrow, or words read in a picture) holding most of the wanted words; found words bold; cut round the first hit to about 70 characters with "…". Not shown when only the name matched, nor for Face-ID-locked boards. Id `boards.found.<title>`.
- Idea 3 sort (`BoardSort`): menu "Sort" (arrow.up.arrow.down) at the top right of the Recent card, shown with 2+ boards: "Last changed" (default), "Date made", "Name" (A–Z, no case or accents); tick on the current; UserDefaults `list.sort`; the home Recent grid follows. Ids `boards.sort`, `boards.sort.changed/made/name`.
- Idea 4 Due soon (`DueSoon.entries`, `DueSoonEntry`): card key `dueSoon`, folds like the others, colour = Timeline orange; at the top above Pinned when a board's due day is today to +7 days or past; none = no card; hidden while searching; put-away boards left out. Rows: dot + name, right "today", "tomorrow", "in 3 days" (grey), "1 day late" / "3 days late" (red); nearest/latest first. Ids `boards.card.dueSoon`, `boards.due.<title>`. NOT on the iPad/Mac home grid (the brief said the list).
- Idea 5: the existing line "5 things · 1 done · 2 late": only "2 late" red (`LineText19`), count = `Board.lateThings` (the timeline rule `timelineProgress.late`). Id `boards.line.<title>`.
- Idea 6 menu line "Colour…" (paintpalette) with green, pink, orange, blue, teal (no purple, no red), tick on the current; hidden on read-only boards; id `card.colour`. Before: "Colour" with all of `Palette.boards`.
- Idea 7 pull down (iPhone, iPad): the line "Release to make a board from the clipboard" (`boards.pullLine`) fades in; released past 70 pt (about 60 is enough) the clipboard becomes a mind map through `Outline.parse` + `addMap` (one top line = centre and name; several = centre and the name "From the clipboard"), saved and opened. Empty: "Nothing on the clipboard to make a board from." (problem tone). Core `ClipboardBoard.board(from:)`. Not seen: release is detected by the drag ending while the pull is past the line (no scroll-phase API on iOS 17); iOS may ask "Allow Paste".
- Idea 8 done bar: 3 pt capsule under the name, the done green on a 10 % track; share = (to-do boxes done in PARAGON + timeline bars and milestones done) / all such things; hidden with none; VoiceOver "3 of 8 done". Id `boards.doneBar.<title>`; `Board.doneShare(doneThings:)`.
- Idea 45 "Send to PARAGON…" (paperplane, `card.send`): opens the board, then the Send page after 0.5 s. Not on put-away boards; a board that cannot be changed: "This board was made by a newer Seedboard. Update the app to send it." A Face-ID board unlocks first and the Send page does not follow.

### 25.3 Board, canvas, Read mode, paper, + tile (lanes canvasa, canvasb, mac)
- Core `Paper19.swift` (`PaperColour`), `Canvas19.swift`; tests `Canvas19Tests` (colours, Stack, small note, frame) and `Paper19Tests` (paper, Read mode). App `Canvas19.swift`, `Bar19.swift`, `Mac19.swift`.
- Idea 17 Read mode: `BoardSession.readMode`; `readOnly` is also true while it is on (no typing, adding, Undo, tool pill or handles; dragging a thing pans; scroll, zoom and choosing work). Pill with SF Symbol "eyeglasses" + "Read mode" + full-colour "Done" (`readmode.pill`, `readmode.done`), replacing the chosen-bar row. More "Read mode" / "Leave Read mode" (`more.readMode`); Mac View "Read Mode" / "Leave Read Mode" ⌥⌘R. Not on boards that cannot be changed or starter drafts. Entering ends typing and an arrow in progress. The newer-version banner now tests `!board.canBeChanged`. UserDefaults `readMode.<UUID>`.
- Idea 18 paper: More "Background colour…" (`more.paper`) under Background; page "Background colour", four round swatches with words Plain, Paper, Warm grey, Night blue (`paper.swatch.plain|paper|warmGrey|nightBlue`), line "It goes with the dots or squares of Background.", full-colour Done (`paper.done`, Return; Escape closes). A swatch applies at once, one undo step (`setPaper`), refused on read-only, Read mode, timeline. Ring 3 pt in the Boards colour. Colours (day / night): Plain F6F7F9 / 17181B (as before); Paper FBF7EE / 1F1C17; Warm grey F0EEEA / 252321; Night blue EDF1F7 / 151C2C. `Paint.canvas` returns the paper; board fill, safe area, arrow-word patches, tick-box rings, Focus dimming match. Share/export picture: the paper's DAY colour behind (white with none). Not covered: timeline and Who does what; list thumbnails and the Kept page draw Plain. Not checked by eye.
- Idea 10 colour row: last 3 colours used first (newest first, no doubles, only colours the row offers), "No colour" stays first; UserDefaults `recentColours19`; recorded by `setColour` and `colourChosen`; also the menu Colour list. Not branch recolour or list colour menus.
- Idea 12 Stack (`sel.stack`, `menu.stack`, `rectangle.stack`; Mac Board "Stack", no key): 2+ chosen stackable things (notes, flow shapes, free text; not frames, ideas, pictures, sketches, tables, doors, pinned or hidden things; not on timeline or Who does what). Reading order (`Thinking.readingOrder`); the first stays; the rest under it, same left edge, 10 pt gaps; one undo step; they stay chosen.
- Idea 14 small note (`sel.smallNote` / `sel.normalNote`, `square.arrowtriangle.4.outward`): Small = `textSize` "small", 96 x 64, text 13; Normal = 150 x 110, `textSize` nil (a note resized by hand goes back to 150 x 110); top left kept; pinned notes untouched; shown with one note or only notes ("Normal Note" when all are small).
- Idea 15 Put in a frame (`sel.putInFrame` / `menu.putInFrame`, `square.dashed`; Mac Board "Put in a Frame" ⇧⌘F, because ⌥⌘F is Focus on the Frame): frame = union of the chosen things (pictures with caption) grown 24 pt each side, empty name, under everything, then the only thing chosen with its name field open (Escape removes it by the new-thing rule). Refused: an idea of a map without its centre chosen; timeline / Who does what; read-only. One undo step. The 30 pt name band lies over the top 6 pt of the 24 pt air.
- Idea 16 + tile (`tool.add`, first tile; the + of New board, Boards colour): press = sticky note; hold (touch) or click-and-hold / right-click (Mac) = menu "Note", "Idea", "Shape" (the shape picked last, Box at first), "Text" (`tool.add.note/idea/shape/text`). Help: "Add a sticky note — hold for Note, Idea, Shape, Text"; VoiceOver "Add". Not on timeline / Who does what bars. The bar has 10 tiles (11 with Camera).
- Idea 48 Mac sidebar: View "Hide Sidebar" / "Show Sidebar", ⌃⌘S (`Shortcut19.sidebarKey/sidebarModifiers`); UserDefaults `mac.sidebarHidden` (not with -sampleData); synced both ways with `NavigationSplitView` column visibility, so the toolbar button agrees. ⌘0 stays Actual Size.
- Idea 49 iPad tool bar: grabber capsule (36 x 5, grey 55 %), id `board.tools.grabber`; drag, release snaps with a spring (response 0.38, damping 0.78) to the nearest of bottom / left / right (`ToolDock.nearest`; top is NOT offered, it would cover the top bars). `tool.dock`, iPad only. Left/right: vertical bar mid-height, `ViewThatFits` tiles 40/36/32 then scroll, 10 pt from the edge. Only ordinary boards that can be changed. VoiceOver "Move to the left / right / bottom". The iPad bar is 10 pt taller. Not seen on an iPad.
- Idea 50 Quick look (Mac): hover a row 0.6 s (`BoardQuickLook.delay`), popover 240 wide, name + first 6 lines of "Copy the board as text" (`Board.quickLookLines`), empty: "Nothing on it yet"; closes when the pointer leaves; id `boards.quicklook`. Not seen with a real pointer.

### 25.4 Mind maps (lane maps; Core `Maps19.swift`, `Arrows.swift`, `Outline.swift`; tests `Maps19Tests` (4))
- Idea 13 bent arrows: diamond knob in the middle of a chosen arrow, hollow straight, filled bent; drag = smooth curve through the point (two curves, same direction), live, one undo step; press twice = straight. Menu line "Straighten" (`line.diagonal`, `menu.straighten`, grey unless bent); bar "Straighten" when bent; Mac Board "Straighten the Arrow". Rules `Route.bent`, `bentPieces`, `bend(toReach:plainMiddle:)`, `takesKnob` (reach 13 pt), `Board.bendLink`, `straightenLink`, `knobHit`. Arrow menu "Dashed" is now "Dashed (maybe)" (`menu.dashed`); the bar still says "Dashed". Not seen.
- Idea 20 thick near the middle (`Route.branchTaper`): filled tapered line, main branch 5 pt to 3.4 at the far end of the words' underline, second level 3.4 to 2.4, third 2.4 to 1.8, deeper 1.8; round ends. Boxes look: even 1.8.
- Idea 21 numbered branches: Mac Board "Number the Branches" / "Stop Numbering the Branches"; More (`more.numberBranches`) "Number the branches" / "Stop numbering the branches" on boards with a map. 1, 2, 3 … right side top to bottom, then left, per map. Look: the step-number badge (round, 17 pt, white ring), filled in the branch's colour, at the outer top corner of the branch's words. `Board.numberBranches`. "Copy as text" writes "2. Food"; the PARAGON task text is not changed.
- Idea 22 "Move to the Other Side" (`arrow.left.arrow.right`, `menu.otherSide`; Mac Board same words): a main branch of a both-sided map, goes last on the other side, one undo step; not deeper ideas, one-sided maps, read-only.
- Idea 24 Map look: More "Map look" (`more.mapLook`, `.lines`, `.curves`, `.boxes`), Mac Board "Map Look"; Lines, Curves, Boxes with a check. `Board.mapLook`; nothing stored = Curves = what 0.18 drew (choosing Curves stores nothing). Lines: straight to the branch. Boxes: each idea except the centre in a rounded box (soft fill, branch-colour edge, 1.6 line, 5 x 2 pt around the words), thin right-angled joins with 8 pt rounded corners. Undoable.
- Idea 25 "Grow Ideas from This" (`lightbulb`, `menu.grow`; Mac Board same): sheet "Grow Ideas from This", the idea's words, "Thinking on this device…", up to 3 chips (`grow.chip.0..2`; a press adds a child idea, last, and the chip goes), "Add All" (`grow.addAll`, full colour, Return), "Cancel" (`grow.cancel`, Escape) becoming "Done" once something was added, "No suggestions for this one." (`grow.none`). On device only: Apple's FoundationModels (`SystemLanguageModel.default`, behind `#if canImport(FoundationModels)`, `#available(iOS 26, macOS 26)` and availability) asking for five short ideas in the idea's language; else NaturalLanguage `NLEmbedding.wordEmbedding` for the language `NLLanguageRecognizer` finds (English second; the longest word of a phrase). Rule `GrowIdeas.clean`: trimmed, bullets / numbers / quotes removed, no doubles (capitals and accents ignored), not the idea's own words, not already a child, empty or over 40 letters out, max 3. Each add is one undo step. Not seen: the model needs Apple Intelligence; on a simulator the word list is the likely path.
- Already there: 19 (Return = sister, Tab = child, since 0.2/0.3, also on an iPad keyboard) and 23 (the "+5" pill on a folded branch, since 0.9).

### 25.5 Timelines (lane timeline; Core `Timeline19.swift`; tests `Timeline19Tests` (5); App `Timeline19.swift`)
- Idea 26: same rule and result as before (dead zone 0.2 row, one undo step); now the dragged bar follows the finger smoothly and pushed rows start where they were and slide on a spring (response 0.34, damping 0.66) via `SpringFrame` and `painter.rowShift`. Also for a row dragged by its NAME (`carryRow`, phases too). Off with Reduce Motion (`Feel.moves`). Phase bands and grid lines do not spring.
- Idea 27 note: chip "Note" (`sel.note`, filled when a note exists); Mac line "Note…" (`menu.note`); page `timeline.notePage`, field `note.field`, counter "n of 280 letters", Cancel (`note.cancel`, Esc), Save (`note.save`, Return); empty takes it away. Stored `Item.note`. A tiny note mark (`note.text` on a small round, neutral ink) after the bar or milestone; a tap shows the bubble `timeline.noteBubble` above (below in the top rows), closed by the next press elsewhere or tapping the bar again; double-tap still edits the name. The mark shows in the PARAGON picture. Bars too.
- Idea 28 dates: shown while held (after 0.28 s still) and while dragged or stretched, 17 pt semibold on a plate 32 high, live, above the bar (below in the top row). "1 Dec – 12 Dec · 12 days"; year only outside the current year ("1 Dec 2026 – 12 Jan 2027 · 43 days"); one day "1 Dec · 1 day"; milestone "1 Dec" (`Item.spanCallout`). Only one chosen bar; a quick tap does not flash it. Before: 11 pt, only after moving.
- Idea 29 switch: "Weeks" and "Months" in a capsule above the zoom plate (`timeline.scaleSwitch`, `.weeks`, `.months`); the lit one is full colour (Timeline colour); same scale as the zoom; at Days neither lit; the zoom's middle word (`zoom.value`) still shows Days / Weeks / Months.
- Idea 30 group by colour (`groupByColour`): More "Group by colour" (`more.groupColour`); Mac Board "Group by Colour"; Mac right-click on a timeline. Colours in order of first appearance from the top; inside a colour the old order; own colour (empty = blue for bars, orange for milestones); phases stay, rows grouped inside each phase block (rows above the first phase are a block); links untouched; one undo step; grey when already grouped or not changeable. Says "Grouped by colour: 3 colours." or "The colours are in groups already."
- Idea 31 repeat: chip "Repeat" (`sel.repeat`), Mac "Repeat…" (`menu.repeat`); page: Day / Week / 2 Weeks / Month (`repeat.every`), stepper "6 times in all" 2 to 12 (`repeat.times`), summary (`repeat.summary`), Cancel (`repeat.cancel`), Repeat (`repeat.go`, full colour); default Week, 6 times; says "5 copies made, under the bar." Decisions: "times" counts the bar (6 = bar + 5); a timeline row holds one item, so the copies go on the rows right under the bar, in date order, each as long as the bar, in its colour, with name, who and note, not done, no plan, no "comes after"; Month keeps the day or the last day of a shorter month; dates outside 1900 to 2200 are left out; one undo step.
- Idea 32 who: chip "Who" (`sel.who`), Mac "Who…" (`menu.who`); page "Who does it", field "Initials, one to three letters" (`who.field`, letters only, capitals at once), Cancel (`who.cancel`), Save (`who.save`); stored `Item.initials`. A 16 pt round at the start of the bar inside it (bar at least 34 pt wide) or before it; grey fill (day 0.88, night 0.30), grey edge, ink letters. Bars only.

### 25.6 Who does what and tables (lane seqtable; Core `Sequence19.swift`; tests `SeqTable19Tests` (6); App `SeqTable19.swift`)
- Idea 33 drag a message: same gesture and hold (0.25 s on touch, at once on the Mac). The thin landing line is gone; passed messages glide with a soft spring (about 1/3 s), the dragged message's faint place sits in the gap, the copy is drawn from where it came from, blocks stretch with the springs. Reduce Motion: no gliding. Core `Board.sequenceRoom(moving:to:before:)`, `SequenceRoom`, `SequenceLayout.shifted`, `keepBlocksAfterMove`. Blocks rule in `moveStep`: a message landing between the others of a block stays in it; dragged outside leaves it and the block shrinks (first/last updated); a block that held only it follows it; into the middle of a block it becomes held. Before, `moveStep` did not touch blocks. Move up / Move down and ⌥ arrows use the same rule. One undo step. Not seen; no auto-scroll near the edge (never existed). The line in 20.8 about not dragging "by hand" meant blocks; messages are here.
- Idea 34 note: chip "Note…" (`sel.note`, `note.text`, lit when set); page "Note", field `note.field` ("Words beside the message", 1 to 5 lines), footer "A grey box beside the message. Leave it empty and press Save to take the note away.", Cancel (`note.cancel`), Save (`note.save`). `Item.note`. Drawn: grey soft plate with a folded top right corner, caption 11, right of the message (12 pt gap), top aligned, width 54 to 150, wraps; the row grows and the diagram widens. Press the box = choose its message. `Board.setNote`, `SequenceNote` (size estimate: 6 pt per letter, 14 pt per line, 7 pt padding). Not in the PARAGON text or "copy as text"; in the board's picture and PDF. Different from the Note tool (kind `band`).
- Idea 35 column widths: a chosen table (alone) shows a blue handle at the right edge of each column in the heading row; drag (hit 6 pt pointer, 14 pt finger), 40 to 800 pt, live; double-press = fit to the longest words on one line (also 40 to 800; the tick column returns to its narrow self); one undo step each. `Item.widths[column]` (0 or missing = by words, 56 to 220 as before); follows Add / Remove column. A corner handle has priority. `TableRules.settingWidth`, `ownWidth`, `leastWidth` 40; `tableSetWidth`, `tableClearWidth`.
- Idea 36 tick column: "Tick Column" (`sel.table.tick`, `menu.table.tick`, `checklist`; word on Mac and iPad, mark only on iPhone; lit when on); adds a first column of boxes or takes it away (ticks go; Undo brings them back); a one-column table loses the box mode and keeps the column; not at 20 columns. Box 16 pt rounded square, hollow when open; filled green (#2E8B57, night #3FA26C) with a white tick when ticked; a tap ticks (cell "x" / ""), also on an unchosen table; `TickSound` plays. Under the table at the left "3 of 8 done" (12 pt, faint, green when all ticked; rows under the heading only; nothing with no rows); part of the table's height. `TableRules.isTicked`, `tickCount`, `tickLine`; `tableTickColumnOn/Off`, `tableToggleTick`; Tab skips boxes.
- Idea 37 sort: a tap on a heading sorts A to Z, then Z to A, then A to Z; another column resets. Numbers by value (also "3,5"), words ignoring capitals and accents ("item 2" before "item 10"), numbers before words, empty always last, equal keep their order. A grey chevron (`chevron.up` A to Z, `chevron.down` Z to A) at the right of the sorted heading while the cells stand so; any other change hides it. Works on an unchosen table (the first press also chooses). Two presses on a heading still type (first sorts, second types). A tap while a heading is typed in does not sort. `TableRules.sorted`, `nextSort`, `Item.tableSort`; memory `TableSortMemory`. One undo step.
- The old "Does NOT ... column dragging" (20.9) is wrong since 0.19. Not seen: dragging, handles, tap-sort and the springs on a screen.

### 25.7 Pencil and pictures (lane pencil; Core `InkClean.swift`, `InkRecent.swift`, `Pictures19.swift`; tests `Pencil19Tests` (6), `Pencil19RecentTests` (1), `Pictures19Tests` (2); App `Pencil19.swift`)
- Idea 38 neat shapes: keeping the tip still ~0.4 s (`InkShape.rest`) at the end of a stroke cleans the stroke itself (ink, same colour and thickness; pressure dropped). Recognised: rectangle, ellipse, triangle, diamond, straight line, arrow (shaft + head in one stroke becomes shaft + two barbs). Else as drawn. Numbers: a line needs 36 pt and within 7 % of its length from the chord; a loop at least 44 x 28, once round, fit 3.5 % of the diagonal (`InkShape.shape`), triangle fit 4.5 %; arrow barbs 22 % of the shaft (12 to 40 pt) at 28 degrees. Not on the highlighter or lasso. "Tidy shapes" ON still wins (a real thing). Plate tip "Hold at the end of a shape to make it neat." (`draw.holdTip`) until the first snap or 3 plate openings (`ink.holdTip`; always with -sampleData). Not seen: the numbers are guesses to tune.
- Idea 39: pen colours, the last 3 first (`ink.recentColours`; plain ink counts as a colour).
- Idea 40 Round: picture menu / bar line "Round" (`picture.round`), then "Square"; `circle` / `square`; stored `Item.cropShape` "circle". Round also crops a non-square box to a square round its middle (the crop narrowed on the longer side; "Whole picture" gives it back); Square keeps the box and crop. Drawn round on the board, in exports and thumbnails; marks drawn on it clip round. Refused on read-only. Not done: no Rectangle / Circle choice on the crop page; taps in a corner outside the circle still hit the picture.
- Idea 41 Camera tile (`tool.camera`, SF Symbol `camera`, accessibility "Camera"; no word on the tile) after Picture; opens the `CameraPage` of "Take a photo"; hidden on the Mac and without a camera (simulator too). Switch `CameraTile19.shown`. The tile ladder got a step: 40 → 36 → 32 → 30 → scroll (mark 18 at 30); at 375 pt 30-pt tiles (337 wide). Holding the Picture tile still offers it.
- Idea 42 Print Frame (`picture.printFrame`, `photo.artframe`, lit when on): white border = 4 % of the shorter side, minimum 6 pt (`Item.printBorder`), soft shadow (day 0.28 black radius 5, 2 down; night 0.16 radius 4); in exports and thumbnails; `Item.printFrame`; undoable.

### 25.8 PARAGON and sharing (lane share; Core `Share19.swift`; tests `Share19Tests` (4); App `Share19.swift`)
- Idea 43 "Share Picture of This Frame" (share mark; `sel.frame.picture`, `menu.frame.picture`; Mac File after "Share…", grey unless exactly one frame is chosen): `Board.framePicture(of:)` + `BoardPicture.png` (white, day look, 40 air, 2x), pictures read from their files; content = frame + things whose middle lies inside + everything under an idea + arrows between them (the same rule as "Send this frame"); file name = the frame's words ("/ : \" become "-"), else "Frame". Opens the system share choice. Works on read-only boards; not on self-placing boards. Failure "The picture could not be made." (red). An empty frame still gives a picture.
- Idea 44 Preview on the Send page: part "Preview" (`send.preview`, text `send.preview.text`) under "What PARAGON gets": the first 8 lines of the written text, small monospaced in a soft rounded box, selectable; "Show all (N more lines)" / "Show less" (`send.preview.toggle`) only with more than 8 lines. Follows the choice, name and switches. Built by `ParagonExport.handoverText(project:board:title:picture:now:choices:)`, which `ParagonGate.send` also calls (test: preview == written text, note and project). The preview uses the picture name "picture.png" and the current time.
- Idea 46 checklist paste (`Checklist.parse`): open = "- [ ]", "* [ ]", "+ [ ]", "[ ]", "☐"; done = "- [x]", "* [x]", "[X]", "☑", "☒", "✓", "✔"; bullets "•", "◦", "–", "- x", "* x" = open. Every non-empty line must start with a mark and at least one must be a box or tick, else paste is as before. Result: ideas with `Item.todo`; ticked ones also `Item.doneDay` = today. `Board.todoState(of:)` returns `.done` for a non-timeline thing with To do + `doneDay` (green ring, white tick, grey words). Layout: none chosen = a small map with the centre "Checklist" (one line: just that idea); one idea chosen = under it. One undo step; not on read-only, timeline or Who does what boards. Taking To do off clears the done day (`BoardSession.toggleTodo`). Send to PARAGON still sends a done idea as an OPEN task (only done timeline bars are skipped; `Paragon.swift:165`) but the Inbox skips such done ideas (join decision); nesting by indent is not kept.

### 25.11 Texts, tests, what is not seen
- `Guide.swift`: new 0.19 entry in What's new; How it works changed in Your boards, The board, Mind maps, Shapes and arrows, Tables, Pictures, Drawing by hand, Timeline board, Who does what, Paste a list, Send to PARAGON, Menus and keys, On the Mac, iPhone, Safety, Moving around, Sticky notes (the two presses); Signs in this app has a line for every new mark and colour. Tester's list: `Docs/WHAT-TO-TEST-0.19.txt`.
- Core tests after the join: 563+ green (per lane: list 5, mac 2, maps 4, timeline 5, seqtable 6, pencil 9, share 4, canvas 4). Screen tests only compile (`List19UITests`, `Canvas19UITests`, `Paper19UITests`, `Mac19UITests`, `Maps19UITests`, `Timeline19UITests`, `SeqTable19UITests`, `Pencil19UITests`, `Share19UITests`).
- Nothing of 0.19 was seen on a screen or a device: swipe, pull-down, Pencil hold, gliding bars and rows, bar layout with 11 tiles, the iPad grip, Quick look, Grow ideas, paper colours, popovers and menus.
- Marks that share a shape (one meaning each): the diamond knob of a bent arrow (on arrows) and the orange timeline milestone diamond never share a board; the branch number badge has the shape of the Who does what step number, both meaning "its number in the order"; the note mark (`note.text`) means "this has a note" on timeline bars and on messages alike.
